## 1. System overview

TAQINOR OS is a multi-tenant ERP for a Moroccan solar installer. A browser loads a
**React/Vite single-page app** (`frontend/`). All traffic enters through **nginx**
(ports 80/443), which reverse-proxies three upstreams: the SPA static bundle, the
**Django REST API** (`backend/django_core`, served by gunicorn on :8000), and a
**FastAPI AI/OCR service** (`backend/fastapi_ia`, uvicorn on :8001). The SPA calls
the Django API under the prefix `/api/django/Ã¢â‚¬Â¦` and the AI service under
`/api/fastapi/Ã¢â‚¬Â¦`. Django persists everything to **PostgreSQL 16 (pgvector)** and
uses **Redis** as cache plus Celery broker; a **Celery worker** (same Django image)
runs async jobs such as quote-PDF generation. Generated PDFs and uploads live in
**MinIO** (S3-compatible object storage, buckets `erp-pdf` and `erp-uploads`).
Authentication is cookie-based JWT (httpOnly refresh cookie); every API request is
scoped to the caller's `company` (the tenant). The FastAPI service shares the same
Postgres for its OCR (Zhipu) and natural-language-SQL-agent (LangChain) features,
both JWT-protected and key-gated.

Request flow, front to back: SPA dispatches a Redux thunk Ã¢â€ â€™ axios `GET/POST
/api/django/<app>/Ã¢â‚¬Â¦` with the JWT cookie Ã¢â€ â€™ nginx Ã¢â€ â€™ gunicorn/Django Ã¢â€ â€™ DRF ViewSet
(queryset filtered to `request.user.company`) Ã¢â€ â€™ Postgres Ã¢â€ â€™ JSON back. Quote PDFs
are the exception: the ViewSet hands off to the vendored premium engine (sync via
`/proposal`, or async via Celery) which renders with WeasyPrint and stores the file
in MinIO.

---

## 4. Backend, app by app

All multi-tenant models carry a `company` FK Ã¢â€ â€™ `authentication.Company`. ViewSets
filter `get_queryset()` by `request.user.company` and force-assign `company` in
`perform_create` (never read from the request body). The literal tenant field is
**`company`** Ã¢â‚¬â€ there is no field named `tenant_id`.

> **Contrainte transversale de conception (VX125).** Budget de densitÃƒÂ© de
> signaux : max 3 signaux ambiants simultanÃƒÂ©s par ÃƒÂ©cran de liste, jamais deux
> redisant le mÃƒÂªme chiffre ; badge de maturitÃƒÂ© `<BetaBadge>` sur les modules
> jeunes avec critÃƒÂ¨re de retrait objectif. Voir `docs/design-density-budget.md`.

API prefixes (from `erp_agentique/urls.py`, all under `/api/django/`):
`authentication` Ã¢â€ â€™ root, `stock/`, `crm/`, `ventes/`, `parametres/`, `roles/`,
`reporting/`, `contact/`, `installations/`, `sav/`, `records/`, `imports/`
(dataimport), `custom-fields/`, `documents/`, `public/` (tokenized PDFs, no login),
`pos/`, `scm/`, `mrp/`, `transport/`, `douane/`, `ecommerce-connect/`,
`promotions/` (this batch's 7 new apps + the pre-existing `pos`, all `_APP_URLS`).
JWT lives at `token/`, `token/refresh/`, `token/verify/`.

### authentication Ã¢â‚¬â€ tenant root, users, JWT  *(path: `backend/django_core/authentication`, NOT under apps/)*
Owns the tenant (`Company`), the user model, registration, and JWT issuance.
- **Company** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `nom`, `slug` (unique), `actif` (bool), `date_creation`; **NTDMO27** adds `tours_actifs` (bool, default True) Ã¢â‚¬â€ company-wide off-switch for auto-appearing `<ProductTour>` visits (any company, not demo-only, unlike `mode_presentation_actif`). The tenant every other model points at.
- **CustomUser** (extends AbstractUser) Ã¢â‚¬â€ `company` FKÃ¢â€ â€™Company; `role` FKÃ¢â€ â€™roles.Role (nullable); `role_legacy` (deprecated CharField admin/responsable/normal, now kept in sync with `role`'s tier on create/update + a one-off additive data backfill); derived `menu_tier` property = the **authoritative** menu tier read from the *new* Role (AdministrateurÃ¢â€ â€™admin, ResponsableÃ¢â€ â€™responsable, Utilisateur/customÃ¢â€ â€™normal; superuserÃ¢â€ â€™admin; legacy fallback only when role-less), exposed on `/auth/me/` and the JWT and used by the sidebar; `tier_for_role` + the pure `authentication/role_tiers.py` are the single source of truth; `poste`, `phone_number`, `avatar_key` (MinIO); `is_protected` (owner-account guard), `is_active`, `is_superuser`; **`supervisor`** self-FK (nullable, Feature E) driving team/subtree record-visibility. Record-visibility scoping lives in `authentication/scoping.py` (`record_scope_for`, `visible_user_ids`, `scope_queryset`) and is applied opt-in on the list+detail querysets of crm/ventes/installations/sav (only the new scoped roles narrow; admins/legacy/custom roles see all; users always keep their own records). Buy prices gated by `can_view_buy_prices` (`prix_achat_voir`).
- Endpoints (mounted at `/api/django/`): `POST /auth/register-company/` (public onboarding: new company + admin) Ã‚Â· `POST /register/` (admin adds user to own company) Ã‚Â· `GET /auth/me/` Ã‚Â· `POST /auth/logout/` Ã‚Â· `POST /auth/token/refresh/` Ã‚Â· `GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE /users/Ã¢â‚¬Â¦` + `POST /users/{id}/avatar/` (Administrateur + Responsable tier Ã¢â‚¬â€ `IsAdminOrResponsableTier`, limited tier blocked) Ã‚Â· `GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE /companies/Ã¢â‚¬Â¦` (superuser). **PACT116** gives `register-company` its first frontend door Ã¢â‚¬â€ public `/register` route (no login, no ERP layout), previously reachable only by direct API call.
- **Tenant lifecycle (SCA18-22).** `Company.statut` (actif/suspendu/fermeture, mirrors the historical `actif` bool both ways) gates login/JWT-refresh/API access Ã¢â‚¬â€ enforced in the authenticator (`cookie_auth.py`) and JWT issuance, never a middleware (SCA18, applied where the request already resolves the user Ã¢â‚¬â€ zero extra queries). `selectors.active_companies` (SCA19) is the single source of "sweepable" companies for beat fan-outs. `services.close_company`/soft-close (SCA21) moves a tenant to `fermeture` + timestamps `date_fermeture` (30-day grace before purge). A staff-only founder console (SCA22, `IsSuperuserConsole`) at `auth/console/tenants/` (list + usage counts via other apps' selectors, never their models) + `.../statut/` (change actif/suspendu/fermeture) + `.../note/` (free-text `plan_flag`, never billing). **SCA46** adds `Company.benchmarking_opt_in` (opt-in consent, default False) gating the k-anonymized cross-tenant aggregates (`core.benchmarking`). **SCA23** adds a "day-2" tenant test (`tests/test_day2_tenant.py`) as a CI sellability gate: registers a second company and exercises its full journey (quoteÃ¢â€ â€™PDF proposalÃ¢â€ â€™accept) in isolation from the first, catching cross-tenant regressions the single-company test suite would miss. **N100/N101 (2026-08-05, the ERP is now FOR SALE):** console gains `auth/console/tenants/creer/` (`views_console_create.py` Ã¢â‚¬â€ Company + CompanyProfile + first admin invitation + `init_roles` + SCA20 signup hooks, idempotent on slug/email) and the tenant payload gains plan/seats/health/licence-billing (import-guarded reads); `CustomUser.is_taqinor_support` (migration 0027) = editor-support flag granting only the right to REQUEST an impersonation (adminops NTADM22); public `POST /api/django/auth/signup-demande/` (design partners) is parked behind `TENANT_SIGNUP_ENABLED` (default OFF Ã¢â€ â€™ 404, throttled + honeypot) and only ever creates a `DemandeInscription` Ã¢â‚¬â€ accounts are created solely by founder approval in the console.

### crm Ã¢â‚¬â€ sales funnel + clients
Leads from creation through funnel stages, client records, Odoo-style chatter,
duplicate detection/merge, reversible archive.
- **Client** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `company` FK; `type_client` (PARTICULIER/ENTREPRISE); `nom/prenom`, `email` (optional), `telephone`, `adresse`; Moroccan IDs `cin/ice/if_fiscal/rc`; `custom_data` JSON. Unique `(company, email)` when email set. **NTCRM14** adds `derniere_alerte_dormance` (timestamp, anti-spam guard on the dormant-account nudge).
- **Lead** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `company` FK; `client` FKÃ¢â€ â€™Client (nullable); `owner` FKÃ¢â€ â€™CustomUser; `stage` (**STAGES.py keys**: NEW/CONTACTED/QUOTE_SENT/FOLLOW_UP/SIGNED/COLD, default NEW); `perdu` (bool lost-flag) + `motif_perte`; `canal` (META_ADS/WHATSAPP_CTWA/SITE_WEB/REFERENCE/TELEPHONE/WALK_IN/AUTRE); `priorite`, `tags`, `relance_date`; `type_installation` (RESIDENTIEL/COMMERCIAL/INDUSTRIEL/AGRICOLE); energy profile (`facture_hiver/ete`, `ete_differente` bool, `regularisation_8221` bool); **L4** (21/08/2026, + founder extension) phone-script fields: `occupation_jour` (PRESENT/ABSENT/PARTIEL, nullable Ã¢â‚¬â€ primes over the founder default in `courbes_journalieres._occupation` when set) and equipment `equip_piscine(_pompe_kw,_heures_jour)`/`equip_voiture_electrique`/`equip_ve_km_semaine(_chargeur_kw,_creneau)`/`equip_clim(_pieces,_kw)`/`equip_chauffe_eau_electrique(_kw,_creneau)` (migration crm/0079 adds the 6 power/crÃƒÂ©neau fields, crm/0080 adds `equip_clim_creneau`/`equip_piscine_creneau` Ã¢â‚¬â€ every one moves the hourly curve; the tunnel Ã‚Â« Affiner mon profil Ã‚Â» captures the kW/crÃƒÂ©neau details optionally per checked equipment and the webhook maps them with strict validation, never overwriting an existing value with emptiness; GPS rule (fondateur 24/08): `gps_lat`/`gps_lng` are NEVER overwritten by address-only updates Ã¢â‚¬â€ guards in the webhook anti-replay loop and `LeadSerializer.validate`, and the tunnel no longer requires the city when a valid pin is set; all nullable tri-state Ã¢â‚¬â€ `None` = not asked yet, distinct from `futures_charges`'s paramless web-questionnaire checklist; migration crm/0078; composed into `courbes_journalieres.equipements` Ã¢â‚¬â€ see below); roof/site + pump fields; `source` (OS_NATIVE/ODOO_IMPORT_TEST/SITE_WEB); `is_archived` (bool) + `archived_by/at`; `custom_data` JSON; `web_questionnaire`/`web_estimate` JSON (quote-journey v2: pro/agricole answers without a column + the exact numbers shown to the visitor, whitelisted server-side; migration crm/0062 Ã¢â‚¬â€ the webhook also maps 'professionnel' Ã¢â€ â€™ INDUSTRIEL and writes one automatic chatter note per created lead).
- **LeadActivity** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `lead` FK; `kind` (CREATION/MODIFICATION/NOTE); field-change log (`field/old_value/new_value`) or manual `body`; `user` FK; `bulk` bool.
- **QuestionnaireLien** (L-QUEST 25/08, migration crm/0081, TenantModel) Ã¢â‚¬â€ questionnaire Ã‚Â« ÃƒÂ  remplir chez soi Ã‚Â» par lead : deux jetons (`token` client / `token_interne` aperÃƒÂ§u muet, POST refusÃƒÂ©), `expires_at` 30 j rouvrable (magic-link), `questions` JSON 3-ÃƒÂ©tats, `sections_repondues`. **Whitelist ET ordre d'affichage `SECTIONS_CLES` (recalÃƒÂ© 25/08 Ã¢â‚¬â€ engagement croissant, donnÃƒÂ©es personnelles en dernier)** : occupation/equipements/energie/toiture/gps/photo_facture/photo_compteur/photo_tableau/**contact**. RÃƒÂ¨gles pures dans `apps/crm/questionnaire.py` (manquantes()/prefill()/appliquer_section() Ã¢â‚¬â€ rÃƒÂ©ponses mappÃƒÂ©es par le MÃƒÅ ME extracteur que le webhook site, jamais une seconde validation ; le vide n'ÃƒÂ©crase jamais, GPS jamais ÃƒÂ©crasÃƒÂ©) + **grain CHAMP `champs_a_poser()`/`sections_a_servir()` (ordre fondateur 25/08 Ã‚Â« on ne redemande JAMAIS une donnÃƒÂ©e dÃƒÂ©jÃƒÂ  connue Ã‚Â») : `_COUVERT_PAR` retire `adresse` des questions dÃƒÂ¨s que gps_lat+gps_lng sont posÃƒÂ©s (le repÃƒÂ¨re localise le toit au mÃƒÂ¨tre), une colonne connue revient prÃƒÂ©-remplie donc confirmable, et une section sans rien ÃƒÂ  montrer n'ouvre pas d'ÃƒÂ©cran mort**. Mint privÃƒÂ© `POST leads/{id}/questionnaire-lien/` (dÃƒÂ©faut = sections MANQUANTES, chatter Ã‚Â« crÃƒÂ©ÃƒÂ©/mis ÃƒÂ  jour Ã‚Â», jamais Ã‚Â« envoyÃƒÂ© Ã‚Â») ; public `GET/POST /api/django/crm/public/questionnaire/<token>/` (throttle, 404 gÃƒÂ©nÃƒÂ©rique, enregistrement PAR section ; le GET sert `champs` en plus de `prefill`). Page publique `apps/web /questionnaire/[token]` (mobile-first, **6 ÃƒÂ©crans max au lieu de 9 sections Ã¢â‚¬â€ regroupement `ECRANS` dans `lib/questionnaire.ts` : les 3 photos sur UN ÃƒÂ©cran, toiture+gps sur un autre ; la barre de progression compte les Ãƒâ€°CRANS**, reprise oÃƒÂ¹ on s'est arrÃƒÂªtÃƒÂ©, GPS gÃƒÂ©oloc, photos via intake, POST navigateur via proxy same-origin `/api/questionnaire-repondre` Ã¢â‚¬â€ un POST PAR SECTION, contrat inchangÃƒÂ©) ; dialogue ERP `QuestionnaireDialog` (workspace lead, patron L-SECT).
- **VisiteExterne** (L-TRACE 25/08, migrations crm/0082-0083 + notifications/0048, TenantModel) Ã¢â‚¬â€ traÃƒÂ§age anti-fraude de TOUS les points publics (visite_site/tunnel_lead/proposition/questionnaire/booking) : `appareil_id` PRIMAIRE (uuid localStorage Ã¢â‚¬â€ la MAC n'est pas collectable par le web ; IP = signal secondaire, partagÃƒÂ©e au Maroc), ip/user_agent/langue/duree_s, heartbeat regroupÃƒÂ©, `Lead.appareil_id` (crm/0082). Service `apps/crm/visites.py` (best-effort, jamais un point public cassÃƒÂ©) ; beacon public `POST public/visite/` AUTH = le MÃƒÅ ME secret que le webhook lead (X-Webhook-Secret, importÃƒÂ© littÃƒÂ©ralement de webhooks.py) via proxy worker `/api/visite` ; balise site `apps/web lib/visite.ts` (consentement WB29, jamais en aperÃƒÂ§u interne). Notifications enrichies (historique de visites ÃƒÂ  la crÃƒÂ©ation de lead, IP/appareil ÃƒÂ  l'ouverture de proposition), direction (rÃƒÂ´les Directeur/Administrateur) TOUJOURS destinataire, alertes ROUGES (sÃƒÂ©vÃƒÂ©ritÃƒÂ© `critique`) : re-demande de devis d'un appareil dÃƒÂ©jÃƒÂ  rattachÃƒÂ© ÃƒÂ  un autre lead + corrÃƒÂ©lation concurrent (mÃƒÂªme appareil sur Ã¢â€°Â¥2 leads sous 30 j).
- **RelanceEtape** (RELANCE FOUNDATION, migration crm/0084, TenantModel) -- plan de relance structure multi-touches par lead (distinct du rappel UNIQUE `Lead.relance_date`/`sync_relance_activity`) : `lead` FK, `ordre`/`due_date`/`canal` (appel/whatsapp/email/visite)/`statut` (a_faire/fait/sautee)/`note`. Gabarit founder-editable `parametres.CadenceRelanceEtape` (cadence neutre par defaut J+2/J+5/J+10/J+20/J+35, seedee au signup + a la volee, API `/api/django/parametres/cadence-relance/`). Services `crm.services.initialiser_plan_relance` (idempotent, pose `Lead.relance_date` sur la 1ere echeance) / `marquer_etape_relance` (fait/sautee, journalise le chatter, fait AVANCER `Lead.relance_date` vers la prochaine etape -- jamais un second systeme de rappel concurrent). API `crm.RelanceEtapeViewSet` (`GET /crm/relance-etapes/?scope=overdue|today|all&owner=`, actions `fait`/`sauter`) + `POST leads/{id}/relance/initialiser/`. Panneau ERP-interne Â« Relances du jour Â» (`pages/crm/RelancesDuJourWidget.jsx`, monte dans `CrmCockpit.jsx`) -- AUCUN envoi automatique WhatsApp/e-mail, rappels visuels seulement.
- **LeadTag / Canal / MotifPerte** Ã¢â‚¬â€ per-company managed lists for tags, channels, loss reasons (each has `archived` bool; Canal has `protege`).
- **WebsiteLeadPayload** Ã¢â‚¬â€ raw webhook capture from taqinor.ma; `payload` JSON, `processed` bool, `lead` FK (never loses inbound data).
- **Parrainage** (referral program, N98) Ã¢â‚¬â€ `company` FK; `parrain` FKÃ¢â€ â€™Client (the referrer); `filleul_lead` FKÃ¢â€ â€™Lead and/or `filleul_client` FKÃ¢â€ â€™Client (the referred) + free-text `filleul_nom`; `statut` (en_attente/converti/recompense_versee); `recompense` (Decimal, pre-filled from `parametres.CompanyProfile.referral_reward`); `notes`; `created_by`. Feature on/off via `CompanyProfile.referral_enabled`.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/crm/`): `clients/` and `leads/` ViewSets (CRUD) plus `leads/{id}/archiver|restaurer|whatsapp-devis|devis-auto|noter|merge|bulk`, `leads/{id}/duplicates`, `leads/doublons`, `leads/historique`, `leads/export-xlsx`, `clients/export-xlsx`; managed-list ViewSets `tags/`, `canaux/`, `motifs-perte/`; `parrainages/` (referrals); `assignable-users/`; `POST webhooks/website-leads/` (public, static secret).
- **management/import_odoo_leads** (N107) Ã¢â‚¬â€ `manage.py import_odoo_leads <path> --company <slug|id> [--dry-run]`: idempotent Odoo `crm.lead` importer reusing the `dataimport` parser (CSV/XLSX) + JSON; forces company server-side, reconciles on normalized email/phone + the existing `(company, external_system, external_id)` unique key (never duplicates), stage names from STAGES.py (unknown Ã¢â€ â€™ NEW). No-op without a file. The real 619-lead extraction stays manual/gated on the actual Odoo backup (PII, never committed). **FG242** adds **ConcurrentPerte** Ã¢â‚¬â€ on a LOST lead, captures the winning competitor + price/devise/motif (reuses the existing `Lead.perdu` flag, no hardcoded STAGES.py stage); acting user + company server-side, optional LeadActivity chatter note; ViewSet `concurrents-perte/` (`?lead=`). Migration crm/0029. **FG204** adds **PointContact** Ã¢â‚¬â€ a per-lead multi-touch attribution journal (canal reusing `Lead.Canal`, source, date, ordre, optional paid-channel cost) with a timeline + first/last-touch summary selector; endpoints `points-contact/` (+ `attribution/`) and a `leads/{id}/points-contact/` read action. Migration crm/0030.
- **ARC4** sweeps all 13 crm ViewSets onto `CompanyScopedModelViewSet` (behaviour identical, isolation now auto-covered by the generic tenant sweep test). **ARC18/56** add nullable `tiers` FKs mirroring `Client` (migration crm/0050) and `Lead` (migration crm/0051) into the `tiers.Tiers` directory (`tiers_bridge.py`). **ARC28/29/30/33** add `apps/crm/platform.py` (the first platform manifest, alongside `contrats`) declaring `searchable_models`/`record_targets`/`agent_actions_module` Ã¢â‚¬â€ `agent_actions.py` exposes read-only lead/client agent actions.
- **SiteProfile** (DC12, previously undocumented here) Ã¢â‚¬â€ a REUSABLE per-client energy/roof/pumping profile (OneToOneÃ¢â€ â€™`Client`; taxonomies reuse `Lead`'s choices rather than redeclaring a divergent second list), saved once instead of re-entered on every devis. Endpoint `site-profiles/`; consumed read-only by `apps.ventes` via `selectors.site_profile_for_client` Ã¢â‚¬â€ see the ventes `prefill-site` action (**WIR99**), the action that finally gave this model a caller.
- **LB39** Ã¢â‚¬â€ the VX95 Ã‚Â« Annuler Ã‚Â» toast on a lead-stage change was PATCHing back to the previous stage and always hitting the funnel guard's 400 (a lead never regresses an ÃƒÂ©tape) Ã¢â‚¬â€ the undo was dead in production. `LeadSerializer` now accepts a write-only, never-persisted `undo` boolean: `validate()` allows the regression ONLY when a server-side check (`_undo_of_last_stage_change`) confirms the PATCH is the EXACT inverse of this lead's most recent `stage` chatter entry, within a 300 s window Ã¢â‚¬â€ any other backward move stays refused. No model/migration change.
- **NTMIG26** adds a certification layer on **Partenaire** (apporteur d'affaires / sous-revendeur, historical table `compta_partenaire`): `niveau_certification` (aucun/enregistre/certifie/or/platine), `date_certification`, `date_expiration_certification`, `specialites` (JSON), `nb_deploiements_reussis` (denormalized mirror). `services.poser_compteur_deploiements` is the ONE write path for the counter Ã¢â‚¬â€ called from `apps.migration` (which owns the deployments table), never a direct `Partenaire.objects.update()` from another app. Migration crm/0069.
- **Dormance & engagement (NTCRM14/15/16/29)** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `management/detecter_comptes_dormants` + `selectors.comptes_dormants(company, seuil_jours=90)` flags signed `Client`s with no recent activity; `clients/dormants/` (list) + `clients/{id}/relancer-dormance/` (nudge, stamps `derniere_alerte_dormance`). `engagement.py` (NTCRM16) is a pure/stateless multi-signal engagement score (0-100: frÃƒÂ©quence contact/activitÃƒÂ© rÃƒÂ©cente/paiements ÃƒÂ  temps/devis acceptÃƒÂ©s, Chaud/TiÃƒÂ¨de/Froid labels like `scoring.py`'s lead-scoring) at `clients/{id}/engagement/` + `clients/engagement-bulk/`. `selectors.portefeuille_commercial` (NTCRM29) Ã¢â‚¬â€ a commercial's owned accounts + risk flags, at `clients/mon-portefeuille/`. Frontend: `DormantAccountsWidget.jsx` + `dashboard/PortfolioWidget.jsx` on `CrmCockpit`.
- **SalleVente / SalleVenteItem / SalleVenteVue** (NTCRM17-19) Ã¢â‚¬â€ a tokenized public page bundling several devis/documents/links for ONE lead OR ONE client (never both/neither, `clean()`-enforced); security modelled on `ged.PartageGed` (unpredictable `token`, `expires_at` default 30 d, optional hashed `password_hash`, `actif` kill-switch). `SalleVenteItem` (devis/document/video_lien/note, `ordre`). `SalleVenteVue` logs each public visit (hashed IP only). `selectors.salle_vente_analytics`/`salle_vente_summary_for_lead`; **NTCRM27** `detecter_signal_interet_salle_vente` flags repeat views on a salle linked to an open lead and emits a `salle_vente_signal_interet` domain event (best-effort). Endpoints: `salles-vente/` CRUD (+ `items/`, `DELETE items/{item_id}/`, `analytics/`), `leads/{id}/salle-vente-analytics/`; public `GET /api/django/crm/salle-vente/<token>/` (frontend `/salle-vente/:token`, no login/layout). Frontend: `pages/crm/salle-vente/PublicSalleVentePage.jsx` (public) + `pages/crm/leads/SalleVenteAnalyticsBadge.jsx` (internal, on the lead workspace).
- **Apporteur / DealEnregistre** (NTCRM20-22) Ã¢â‚¬â€ a B2B deal-registration registry, deliberately SEPARATE from the pre-existing `Partenaire`/FG234 lead-submission portal (a concurrent-registration protection window has no equivalent there). `Apporteur` (nom/type/commission %/RIB/`token_acces` Ã¢â‚¬â€ read-only portal secret, no CustomUser account). `DealEnregistre` (`apporteur` FK, `lead` OneToOne, `statut` en_attente/approuve/rejete/expire/**a_payer** Ã¢â‚¬â€ NTCRM22 sets this + `montant_commission_du` on the linked devis's acceptance; `clean()` refuses a second apporteur registering the SAME prospect Ã¢â‚¬â€ email/phone/client identity match Ã¢â‚¬â€ while another's protection window, default 90 d, is active). Endpoints: `apporteurs/`, `deals-enregistres/` (+ `approuver/`, `rejeter/`, `a-payer/`); public read-only `GET /api/django/crm/apporteur-portail/<token>/mes-deals/` (token-only, never a session).
- **Defi** (NTCRM23/24/28) Ã¢â‚¬â€ a temporary, PUBLIC team gamification challenge (date window + leaderboard), distinct from the private/permanent individual `ObjectifCommercial` (FG39); reuses `ObjectifCommercial.Metric` strictly (no second metric taxonomy). `selectors.classement_defi` ranks users by the dÃƒÂ©fi's metric over its window. Endpoints: `defis/` CRUD (+ `classement/` NTCRM24 leaderboard, `export-xlsx/` NTCRM28), frontend `/crm/defis` (DefisPage, visible to the whole team, not just managers).
- **NTCRM26/30 Ã¢â‚¬â€ playbook + account-review gap closure.** `Playbook.condition` (JSON `core.rules` condition tree evaluated against `{type_installation, canal}`, empty = applies to every lead) + `selectors.playbooks_recommandes(lead, stage)` (similarity-recommended playbooks, consumed by the pre-existing `lead_playbook_view`). **NTCRM30** closes the backend test-coverage gap on `PlanCompte`/`RevueCompte` (structured account-review notes, scaffolded by NTCRM10/11 migration crm/0063, frontend already wired by PACT105 Ã¢â‚¬â€ previously undocumented here): a review posted to `revues-compte/` appears immediately, most-recent-first, in the account plan's timeline.

### ventes Ã¢â‚¬â€ quotes, orders, invoices, credit notes, payments, quote engine
The largest app: full quoteÃ¢â€ â€™orderÃ¢â€ â€™invoiceÃ¢â€ â€™recovery lifecycle plus the vendored
premium quote-PDF engine.
- **Devis** (quote) Ã¢â‚¬â€ `company` FK; `reference` (per company+month); `client` FKÃ¢â€ â€™crm.Client; `lead` FKÃ¢â€ â€™crm.Lead (nullable, lead-primary quoting); `statut` (**brouillon/envoye/accepte/refuse/expire**); `mode_installation` (residentiel/industriel/agricole); `option_acceptee` (sans_batterie/avec_batterie); `etude_params` JSON (kWc, production, autoconso, payback, pump CV/HMT/dÃƒÂ©bitÃ¢â‚¬Â¦); `taux_tva`, `remise_globale`; versioning (`version`, `version_parent`, `superseded_by`, `is_active`); discount approval (`remise_approuvee`, `remise_approuvee_par`); `fichier_pdf` (MinIO key). **FG52** adds `devise` (ISO 4217, default MAD) + `taux_change` to **Devis** and **Facture** (and `parametres.CompanyProfile.devise_defaut`): on API create without an explicit devise, the company default is applied (fallback MAD); the premium PDF `fmt()` and UBL export (`dgi_export.py`/`utils/ubl.py`) emit the document currency. No base-currency conversion (currency is document-borne). **SCA47** adds `prix_par_kwc` (Total TTC ÃƒÂ· kWc, DecimalField) Ã¢â‚¬â€ derived and FROZEN write-once at first save when a kWc + total exist (null for kWc-less/pompage devis, never recomputed after); internal generator/BI signal ONLY, same regime as `stock.Produit.prix_achat` Ã¢â‚¬â€ never on a PDF or client-facing output. **SCA39** adds `Meta.indexes` `(company, statut)` + `(company, date_creation)` on Devis and Facture (concurrent `AddIndex`, no blocking lock) Ã¢â‚¬â€ the money-path query subset of NTPLT20. **NTCPQ16** adds `variante_de`/`variante_tier` (self-FK + ÃƒÂ©conomique/standard/premium tag Ã¢â‚¬â€ a tier variant is a full draft Devis, distinct from the size-variant `version_parent`/QJ15 and the sans/avec-batterie couple). **NTCPQ13** adds `devis_origine`/`numero_renouvellement` (self-FK to the renewal chain's root devis; the source devis is never touched Ã¢â‚¬â€ distinct from the T10 revision, which only applies to a not-yet-accepted devis). **NTCPQ11** adds `clauses_appliquees` (JSON snapshot of the applicable `cpq.ClauseCGV` list, frozen once at brouillonÃ¢â€ â€™envoyÃƒÂ©, read-only by the quote_engine, never a new renderer). **TAILLES (26/08/2026)** adds `offres_tailles_config` (migration ventes/0104) Ã¢â‚¬â€ per-devis CONFIGURATION of the three explorable installation sizes Ãƒâ€°co/RecommandÃƒÂ©/Max (`{cle: {config, ajuste, modifie_le, modifie_par}}`); it stores ONLY inputs (panel count, battery modules, swapped catalogue products) Ã¢â‚¬â€ every derived number (prix TTC, ÃƒÂ©conomie, payback, couverture, kWc) is re-stamped by the engine on read, so a hand-typed price is structurally impossible. NULL = byte-identical historical behaviour (the engine derives all three). Distinct from `variante_de`/`variante_tier` (NTCPQ16, a full draft Devis) and from the sans/avec-batterie couple: a size is EXPLORATION only Ã¢â‚¬â€ no lines, no status, no devis created. **MAX RÃ‰EL (28/08/2026)** : sans calepinage MESURABLE (tout devis automatique, layout contour-seul), le compte dessinÃ©/vendu ne borne plus le toit â€” Max = derniÃ¨re taille Ã©ligible du balayage plafonnÃ©e par le MUR PHYSIQUE du tracÃ© client (`dimensionnement.plafond_physique_du_devis`, aire Ã· empreinte, jamais publiÃ© comme contenance ni comme verdict `toit_ok`, plancher = le champ du devis) ; la contenance mesurÃ©e reprend la main dÃ¨s que le 3D est ouvert+enregistrÃ©. Cartes servies filtrables par lien (voir ShareLink `taille_eco`/`taille_max`) ; page client : cartes en tÃªte, le dÃ©tail (hÃ©ros TTC/Ã©co, kWc/panneaux) suit la carte cliquÃ©e par copie du texte rendu (omission honnÃªte, restauration exacte sur RecommandÃ©). PDF rÃ©sidentiel : rÃ´le divergent entre options = UNE ligne Ã  deux valeurs Â« sans Â· avec Â» + comparatif deux colonnes (`quote_engine/residential/options.py::_pair_divergents`) â€” devis deux-options divergent tient en 3 pages, non-divergent byte-identique. **LOT 29/08/2026** : (a) le DIMENSIONNEMENT rÃ©sidentiel passe TOUT ENTIER par le moteur horaire â€” la rÃ¨gle des 900 DH est SUPPRIMÃ‰E backend (`build_devis_auto` : cible explicite souveraine, sinon moteur, sinon `AutoDevisError` nommant la donnÃ©e manquante â€” facture_hiver/ville/catalogue) ET Ã©cran (le gÃ©nÃ©rateur prÃ©remplit via le dry-run `devis/composition/`, plus jamais `estimerPanneaux`) ; (b) endpoints tailles `devis/{id}/offres-tailles/` (GET) + `offres-tailles/config` (PATCH) + `offres-tailles/regenerer` + **`offres-tailles/appliquer`** (POST â€” la config `recommande` RECOMPOSE LE DEVIS via `sync_devis_from_layout` : brouillon seul, lignes nÃ©gociÃ©es refusÃ©es nommÃ©ment, chatter, config consommÃ©e ; Ã‰co/Max restent exploration-carte) ; dialogue Â« Modifier les options Â» large (`lw-context-devis-edit-tailles`) dans DevisTab ; (c) options CHARGEABLES sur la page publique â€” `GET public/proposal/{token}/taille/{cle}/?variante=` (module `apps/ventes/taille_detail.py`, contrat `contract_samples/taille_detail.json`, 404 gÃ©nÃ©rique, cache 15 min) sert Ã©conomies mensuelles/couverture/banque/cashflow de la taille cliquÃ©e ; carte RÃ‰CAP RecommandÃ© en tÃªte de page quand une seule option est envoyÃ©e.
- **LigneDevis** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `devis` FK, `produit` FKÃ¢â€ â€™stock.Produit, `designation`, `quantite`, `prix_unitaire`, `remise`, `taux_tva` (nullable Ã¢â€ â€™ falls back to devis rate; 10% panels / 20% other). **NTCPQ18** adds an optional `lot` FKÃ¢â€ â€™`LotDevis` (NULL = hors lot, historical behaviour unchanged). **L-2OPT (25/08/2026)** adds `variante` (`''` commune | `'sans'` | `'avec'`, migration 0102) : les deux options d'un devis rÃƒÂ©sidentiel peuvent porter des dimensionnements DIFFÃƒâ€°RENTS (ex. 22 panneaux sans / 26 avec) Ã¢â‚¬â€ composition fusionnÃƒÂ©e `services.composition_deux_optimiseurs`/`fusionner_kits`, split PDF/aval variante-d'abord (`quote_engine/builder._repartir_options`, `utils/options.filter_lines_for_option`), payload public `dimensionnement_options`+`production_par_option` (contrat `contract_samples/dimensionnement_options.json`); lignes historiques (`''`) inchangÃƒÂ©es au bit prÃƒÂ¨s.

- **Facture** (invoice) Ã¢â‚¬â€ `devis` FK (new ÃƒÂ©chÃƒÂ©ancier path) **and/or** `bon_commande` OneToOne (legacy path); `client` FK; `type_facture` (**acompte/intermediaire/solde/complete**); `statut` (**brouillon/emise/payee/en_retard/annulee**); `pourcentage`, `libelle`, frozen `montant_ht/tva/ttc`; recovery (`prochaine_relance`, `exclu_relances`); computed `montant_paye`, `avoirs_total`, `montant_du` (= TTC Ã¢Ë†â€™ paid Ã¢Ë†â€™ credits); `fichier_pdf/ubl`. **ARC24** adds an optional `condition_paiement_ref` FKÃ¢â€ â€™`parametres.ConditionPaiement` (source of the default `conditions_paiement` label; the TextField stays master/overridable).
- **LigneFacture** Ã¢â‚¬â€ same shape as LigneDevis (`facture` FK).

- **ShareLink** Ã¢â‚¬â€ public tokenized link (`token` unique, `devis`/`facture` FK, `expires_at`, 30-day) for WhatsApp PDF delivery without login; `niveau` standard|confiance (migration 0100), `otp_lecture` bool, and `sections` JSON (migration 0101 Ã¢â‚¬â€ per-link section toggles roof3d/sld/pdf/bankable/economies/jour_type/gammes + **taille_eco/taille_max (28/08/2026, envoi 1/2/3 options)** : RecommandÃ© n'a pas de clÃ© (c'est LE devis, toujours servi), les deux Ã  False = page mono-option sans cartes, aucune migration (whitelist JSON) ; absent = served, chosen by the commercial in the fiche-lead Ã‚Â« Envoyer au client Ã‚Â» dialog Ã¢â‚¬â€ qui porte dÃ©sormais le curseur segmentÃ© 1/2/3 + cases par taille et Â« Modifier les options Â» (rÃ©utilise l'Ã©cran `DevisOffresTailles`). **L-INTPREV (25/08)** adds `token_interne` (migration 0103, 3-step unique-after-backfill) : second jeton d'APERÃƒâ€¡U INTERNE Ã¢â‚¬â€ mÃƒÂªme page proposition, ZÃƒâ€°RO trace (ni compteur/first-view, ni chatter, ni avance de stage YLEAD10, ni notification, ni beacon d'engagement), OTP non exigÃƒÂ©, signature refusÃƒÂ©e (404 gÃƒÂ©nÃƒÂ©rique) ; mint `share-link` renvoie `token_interne`/`path_interne`, ligne dÃƒÂ©diÃƒÂ©e du dialogue DevisTab (le WhatsApp n'envoie JAMAIS l'interne). **ANALYT1 (audit item 64, 26/08/2026)** adds two nullable fields `friction_alert_logged_at`/`friction_alert_section` (migration 0105) â€” a per-link, one-shot FRICTION signal (client re-reads the SAME public-page section on â‰¥3 DISTINCT visits, doctrine: losing proposals are re-opened more than winning ones) logged once as a lead-chatter note, same idiom as `deep_engagement_logged_at`; `engagement` (XSAL16) gains an internal `visits`/`visit_ids` per-section counter (never exposed by `engagement_summary`, only by `ShareLink.visites_par_section`).
- **ParametresGammes** Ã¢â‚¬â€ PVMRQ (fondateur 18/08) per-company singleton: `deux_gammes` toggle, renamable `nom_essentielle`/`nom_premium`, `marques` JSON (fixed slots Essentielle/Premium Ã¢â€ â€™ {role: marque}, roles = `ROLES_AUTO_COMPOSITION` mirroring frontend `PRODUCT_CATEGORIES`); read by `_pick_product` (pinned brand wins; missing brand Ã¢â€ â€™ None, never a substitute) and by the quote-generator auto-fill. Endpoint `GET/PATCH parametres-gammes/` (GET any authenticated company user, PATCH responsable/admin). Migration ventes/0097.

dÃƒÂ©faut U2 = les DEUX options sans+avec batterie), et `POST devis/composition/` est son DRY-RUN (compose et rend les lignes sans RIEN crÃƒÂ©er, contrat figÃƒÂ© dans `apps/ventes/contract_samples/devis_composition.json`) Ã¢â‚¬â€ les deux lisent la mÃƒÂªme fonction pure `services.composition_residentielle`, qui porte dÃƒÂ©sormais cÃƒÂ¢bles au mÃƒÂ¨tre, marques ÃƒÂ©pinglÃƒÂ©es (PVMRQ) et ordre des lignes (PVORD) ; le frontend rÃƒÂ©sidentiel ne compose plus rien ; `bons-commande/{id}/confirmer|marquer-livre|annuler|creer-facture`, `bons-commande/{id}/pdf/` (**ZSAL8** Ã¢â‚¬â€ client BC printable PDF, no buy price, company-scoped 404); recovery (`relances/`, `balance-agee/`, `clients/{id}/releve(-pdf)/`, `factures/{id}/lettre-relance-pdf/`, `niveaux-relance/`); accounting (`journal-ventes/` .xlsx, `numerotation-audit/`); public `GET /api/django/public/document/{token}/` (tokenized PDF, no auth, no buy prices). **WIR99** adds `devis/prefill-site/?client=<id>` Ã¢â‚¬â€ wires the pre-existing `crm.SiteProfile` (DC12, a per-client reusable energy/roof/pumping profile Ã¢â‚¬â€ see Ã‚Â§4 crm) into the quote-generator prefill for a lead-less devis, via `crm.selectors.site_profile_for_client` (never a `crm.models` import; company-scoped). **WIR96** adds `devis/{id}/suivi-partage/` Ã¢â‚¬â€ read-only marketing follow-up on a devis (`ShareLink` open tracking + abandoned-quote reminders) via `apps.marketing.selectors` (never a `marketing.models` import); - **services.py — pure re-export facade since QJR M3 (30/08/2026)** : the implementation lives in `apps/ventes/domain/` (19 modules — entrees, argent, overrides, etude_schema, bordereau, facturation_ops, encaissements, recouvrement, cycle_vie, catalogue, geometrie, lignes, composition, etudes, taille, gammes, catalogue_events, dimensionnement_devis, resynchronisation) ; `services.py` keeps every historical name importable (313 re-exports, `__all__` of 178 — pinned by `test_services_surface`) and `dimensionnement.py` re-exports its devis-bound half from `domain/dimensionnement_devis.py` (pinned by `test_qjr_dimensionnement_surface`). The website capture page (`/devis/mon-toit`) posts the enriched lead (exact bills, `ete_differente`, `raccordement` incl. `inconnu`, reverse-geocoded `adresse`/GPS) to the CRM webhook; **Meriem designs INSIDE the ERP** (authenticated React route `frontend` `/devis-design/:id`, same-origin cookie session Ã¢â‚¬â€ the roofPro11 builder is Vite-alias-imported from `apps/web`, no second login) and the client signs at the public `/proposition/<token>`. `GET /api/django/ventes/roof-config/` exposes the public MapTiler key same-origin (needs `PUBLIC_MAPTILER_KEY` in the ERP env).
- **quote_engine/** Ã¢â‚¬â€ premium PDF engine. `builder.py` maps an OS Devis Ã¢â€ â€™ the generator data dict (only sell-side `prix_unitaire`; `prix_achat` excluded) and routes by market mode to one of three renderers: `residential/` (redesigned 3-page residential proposal), `agricole/` (4-page pompage-solaire proposal Ã¢â‚¬â€ cover/at-a-glance, ÃƒÂ©tude+schÃƒÂ©ma+charts, ÃƒÂ©quipement+prix+FDA+garanties, rentabilitÃƒÂ© solaire-vs-butane-vs-diesel+signature; modules `renderer/render/theme/cover/study/yield_page/economics_page/charts/schematic/economics/constants/sample_data`), and the legacy `generate_devis_premium.py` (one-page + industriel + fallback). `installations.py` = shared cover-hero photo library that picks the installation photo whose kWc is **nearest** the quote (agricole falls back to residential/industriel of similar power); photos in `assets/installations/<mode>-<kwc>.jpg`. `pricing.py`, `catalog.py`. Buy-price exclusion asserted by `apps/ventes/tests/test_quote_engine_formats.py`; agricole engine by `test_agricole_quote.py`. The old 3 704-line `test_quote_engine.py` was split (19/08/2026) into `_formats` (page counts/totaux/TVA), `_residential`, `_documents`, `_builder` + `_quote_engine_common.py` (shared fixtures); `test_quote_engine.py` remains as a compatibility shim re-exporting those fixtures.
- **solar_design.py** (FG246/247/249) Ã¢â‚¬â€ electrical-engineering helpers: `string_design` (distributes N panels across the inverter MPPT inputs, checks string Vmp/Voc at cold temperature vs the MPPT/voltage window, reports the DC/AC ratio), `match_inverter` (picks a compatible catalogue inverter, classification keywords aligned with `builder.py`, never a price-less product), `optimize_orientation` (tilt/azimuth sweep via the existing PVGIS client). Pure + fully tested (`tests/test_solar_design.py`); not yet surfaced in an endpoint. **FG255** adds `ev_charger_sizing` Ã¢â‚¬â€ EV charging-station (borne de recharge VE) sizing: line current (mono 230 V / tri 400 V Ã¢Ë†Å¡3), dedicated breaker calibre, charge duration/window-fit, recommended standard borne (3.7/7.4/11/22 kW), and PV coupling (solar surplus feeds the borne first, reporting solar-covered kWh + the lifted autoconsommation rate). Pure math, input-freedom preserved, no model/endpoint/PDF change. **FG256** adds `battery_storage_sizing` Ã¢â‚¬â€ two objectives: MAX autoconsommation (store daytime surplus, capped by the re-dischargeable night load) vs BACKUP for N critical hours (usable kWh/kW from critical load Ãƒâ€” hours); returns usable + nominal kWh (ÃƒÂ· DoDÃƒâ€”Ã¢Ë†Å¡round-trip), recommended pack and the binding objective. Pure math. **FG257** adds `simulate_bankable_yield` Ã¢â‚¬â€ financial-grade P50/P90: Performance Ratio = ÃŽÂ (1Ã¢Ë†â€™loss) over temperature/soiling/wiring/inverter/mismatch/availability, P50 = baseÃƒâ€”PR, P90/P75 via the Gaussian lower-tail quantile (z=1.282/0.674) of an annual-variability ÃÆ’ (default 6 %); returns PR, loss breakdown, P50/P90/P75, optional specific yield. Pure math. **FG258** adds `hourly_self_consumption` Ã¢â‚¬â€ per-hour min(load, production) over an 8760-h (or 24-h) load curve Ã¢â€ â€™ real autoconsommation rate, coverage rate, injected surplus, grid import; typical residential/commercial load + PV profiles as fallbacks, and `load_curve_from_xlsx` (parses a column via the already-present openpyxl). Pure math, divide-by-zero guarded. **FG259** adds `net_metering_savings` Ã¢â‚¬â€ values the injected hourly surplus (from FG258) under Moroccan net-metering (loi 13-09): compensated only up to same-tranche import Ãƒâ€” `compensation_ratio` at the tranche tariff (pointe/pleine/creuse), honours the `surplus_injecte_compense` toggle, optional annual cap + residual spill tariff. Pure math. **FG260** adds `tariff_escalation_projection` Ã¢â‚¬â€ a 20Ã¢â‚¬â€œ25-year financial model: year-by-year escalated savings (ONEE escalation Ãƒâ€” module degradation), cumulative + net, simple & discounted payback year, **NPV (VAN)** and **IRR (TRI)** solved by stdlib bisection/Newton (capped iterations, `None` on non-convergence). Pure math. **FG261** adds `optimize_subscribed_power` Ã¢â‚¬â€ for C&I clients: net grid demand (loadÃ¢Ë†â€™PV) Ã¢â€ â€™ post-PV peak Ã¢â€ â€™ recommended subscribed power = ceil(peakÃƒâ€”margin) (never above current) + the annual capacity-charge saving, optional kWÃ¢â€ â€™kVA via power factor. Pure math. **FG262** adds `module_degradation_curve` Ã¢â‚¬â€ per-year PV production factor (compound or linear, with a year-1 LID drop) confronted against manufacturer warranty floors (e.g. 90% @ yr10, 80% @ yr25), flagging the first breach year + shortfall. Pure math.
- **courbes_journalieres.py** (CJ1, 21/08/2026) Ã¢â‚¬â€ builds the public `courbes_journalieres` payload block (kept OUT of the money engine): per-season PVGIS production shapes in LOCAL Moroccan time (`apps/parametres/pvgis_profils.py` Ã¢â‚¬â€ 7 reference curves for 13 cities, live DRcalc/PVcalc at the lead's GPS with a 30-day system cache + outage circuit-breaker, honest omission when city unknown), real daily consumption levels from the 12 real bills, `options`/`batterie_kwh` mirroring the quote, `occupation` flag (residential default `presence_jour`, founder field decision). - **etude_horaire.py + quote_engine/bareme.py + dimensionnement.py + etude_horaire_view.py** (CJ2a, 21/08/2026) Ã¢â‚¬â€ **THE canonical residential savings engine**, replacing the `AUTOCONSO_SANS = 0.60` forfait (founder order: savings must follow the real consumption curves per season, not a flat 60 % of production). `bareme.py` = bidirectional kWhÃ¢â€¡â€žMAD **calibrated on three REAL founder bills** (SRM Casablanca-Settat 08/05/2026, 20/12/2025, 20/01/2026): reproduces the 30-day bill to the centime (energy 496,03 / fixed 39,94 / TPPAN 56,80 / total 592,77 + 0,25 % cash stamp), proves the selective rule and the progressive day-prorated TPPAN (art. 16 dahir 1-96-77, VAT-inclusive), adds the TWO fixed lines (meter rental 18,28 HT + connection upkeep 15,00 HT), and inverts MADÃ¢â€ â€™kWh by bisection on the TOTAL bill (exact on all three). Per-vintage tables with **per-tranche provenance**: T5 2026 corrected to 1,381704 (bill-proven; the repo extrapolated 1,405116 at constant HT) Ã¢â‚¬â€ `pricing.ONEE_TRANCHES` now ALIGNED by QJR26 (founder decision D5, 29/08/2026: drift locks recalibrated by derivation, ERP frontend mirrors updated; the apps/web mirrors are queued as WEB_PLAN QJW12), divergence history kept in `DIVERGENCES_PRICING` (incl. the unresolved T6 2026 conflict). `etude_horaire.py` integrates hour by hour, month by month: real PVGIS production shape Ãƒâ€” the client's occupancy silhouette + L4 equipment layers scaled to THAT month's real bill, overlap via the existing `solar_design.hourly_self_consumption`, battery by chronological daily charge/discharge (`BATTERY_ROUNDTRIP`, starts empty = conservative), money by the two-bill model AT THE MONTH. Returns `None` without real anchoring (rule Z2). `dimensionnement.py` = successor to the founder's Ã‚Â« 900 DH/month Ã‚Â» rule: sweeps candidate sizes (granularity = one catalogue panel; upper bound = production/consumption parity + 1 panel, since Moroccan surplus is worth nothing), reuses `composition_residentielle` for the cost AND the founder's 80 % inverter rule (never re-implemented), recommends by explicit named criterion (`meilleur_payback`, then coverage). `POST /ventes/etude-horaire/preview/` (`IsAnyRole`, company-scoped server-side, no writes) is the single source the generator screen will call (CJ2b) Ã¢â‚¬â€ contract `contract_samples/etude_horaire.json`. - **utils/references.py** Ã¢â‚¬â€ numbering = highest-used + 1 per company+month (savepoint + retry on races); never `count()+1`. **ARC6** relocates the algorithm to `core.numbering` (the foundation home ~15 apps import across app boundaries); this module is now a byte-identical re-export shim.
- **Sweep + performance (ARC4/5, SCA39-43/45/47/49).** **ARC5** sweeps all 21 ventes ViewSets onto `CompanyScopedModelViewSet` (unified scoping; the public `/proposal` + `ShareLink` endpoints stay explicitly excluded/documented, asserted by `test_arc5_proposal_public_access.py`). **SCA40** collapses the `par_commercial` reporting aggregate into one grouped query (ends an unbounded N+1). **SCA41** makes xlsx exports async past a row-count threshold (`ventes/export/status/` polling, 202 response, company-scoped MinIO key, pilot NTPLT29/30) Ã¢â‚¬â€ wired into **FactureList** (journal ventes / export comptable: on a 202 it polls `ventesApi.exportStatus(jobId)` then downloads). **SCA43** wires `core.request_cache` under the devis-list PDF-preview path (QPERF1 landing Ã¢â‚¬â€ the previously-skipped Devis query budget test now passes). **SCA49** freezes the Devis JSON contract (`etude_params` included) with a serializer-keys snapshot test (`docs/devis-json-contract.md`). **ARC28** adds `apps/ventes/platform.py` (searchable/record/import surfaces). **ARC49/53** Ã¢â‚¬â€ see Ã‚Â§5 frontend for the DevisList/FactureList migration onto the shared DataTable engine (parity proven, PDF/status flows untouched; see `docs/parity/ARC49-devislist-parite.md` and `ARC53-facturelist-parite.md`).
- **dgi/** (N105, silent DGI capability) Ã¢â‚¬â€ `dgi_export.py` (`build_ubl_xml`, UBL 2.1 invoice via stdlib `xml.etree`, carries seller+client ICE, per-line VAT, totals; no buy price), `dgi_validator.py` (`validate_dgi_conformity` Ã¢â€ â€™ list of FR problem messages), `toggle.py` (`is_dgi_enabled(company)`). Armed only by `parametres.CompanyProfile.dgi_export_actif` (default **OFF**): the two facture actions `dgi-export`/`dgi-conformite` and the `dgi_export_facture` management command **404/refuse when OFF**, and the Facture model/serializer/lists are byte-identical (no field, badge, status or column added). Simpl-TVA transmission + certified e-signature remain out of scope (G14). **FG263** adds `solar_design.ppa_model(...)` Ã¢â‚¬â€ a PPA / third-party-investor financial model (pure math): applies FG262 degradation, computes investor revenue (production Ãƒâ€” PPA tariff + escalation Ã¢Ë†â€™ O&M; NPV/IRR/payback reusing FG260) and client savings (grid Ã¢Ë†â€™ PPA tariff), both perspectives. No model; quote-PDF path untouched. **FG264** adds `solar_design.pumping_cycle_yield(...)` Ã¢â‚¬â€ pure-math daily/monthly pumped-water volume by operating cycle (flat mode = byte-parity with `solar.js`; profile mode integrates dÃƒÂ©bit hour-by-hour weighted by a normalized clearsky irradiation profile); curve-less pumps Ã¢â€ â€™ None. No model; quote-PDF path untouched.

### stock Ã¢â‚¬â€ catalogue, suppliers, inventory, procurement
Product catalogue, multi-supplier sourcing, stock movements/locations, supplier POs
and returns.
- **Produit** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `company` FK; `nom`, `sku` (unique per company); `prix_vente` (sell HT); **`prix_achat`** (buy price Ã¢â‚¬â€ internal/generator-only, **never client-facing**); `quantite_stock` (canonical), `seuil_alerte`; `categorie`/`fournisseur` FK; commercial sheet (`marque`, `description`, `garantie`, `garantie_mois`, `garantie_production_mois`); pump specs (`pompe_cv`, `hmt_m`, `pompe_kw`, `tension_v`, `courbe_pompe` JSON); `is_archived`; `custom_data` JSON. **ARC27** adds an optional `unite` FKÃ¢â€ â€™`parametres.UniteMesure`, mirroring the existing free-text `unite_stock` (which stays master; backfilled via `backfill_unites_mesure`). **Unicite du nom (29/08/2026, migrations `stock/0134`+`0135`)** : `UniqueConstraint (company, nom)` CONDITIONNELLE -- seulement sur les produits ACTIFS et SANS SKU (`is_archived=False` et `sku` NULL/vide), pour fermer la course de `get_or_create(company, nom)` du produit de service Â« Frais refacturÃ©s Â» (`ventes.services._produit_frais_refactures`, rÃ©Ã©crit en lecture -> crÃ©ation en savepoint -> relecture sur `IntegrityError`). Volontairement conditionnelle : un produit ARCHIVÃ‰ peut garder le nom d'un actif (rÃ¨gle du seeder) et deux SKU distincts peuvent Ãªtre homonymes (l'unicitÃ© des produits SKUÃ©s reste `unique_together (company, sku)`). La migration `0134` dÃ©-doublonne l'existant SANS RIEN SUPPRIMER (la fiche la plus ancienne intacte, les suivantes renommÃ©es Â« ... (doublon N) Â», rÃ©versible).
- **Protection du catalogue RÃƒâ€°EL (vague 2, 2026-07-31).** `Fournisseur` gagne `is_archived` (migration `stock/0082`) et le cycle produit : `destroy` ARCHIVE au lieu de dÃƒÂ©truire quand des donnÃƒÂ©es le retiennent, `?show_archived=true`, `PATCH unarchive/`, `DELETE force-delete/` (409 si un tarif subsiste). `achats.PrixFournisseur.fournisseur` passe **CASCADE Ã¢â€ â€™ PROTECT** (`achats/0003`) : supprimer un fournisseur ne peut plus dÃƒÂ©truire les prix d'achat NÃƒâ€°GOCIÃƒâ€°S. `FournisseurAdmin`/`ProduitAdmin`/`CompanyAdmin`/`ClientAdmin` refusent la suppression depuis l'admin Django (4 verrous, message nommant le chemin supportÃƒÂ©) ; `ProduitAdminForm` refuse de VIDER un `prix_achat`/`courbe_pompe` dÃƒÂ©jÃƒÂ  saisi. `CategorieAdmin` reste dÃƒÂ©libÃƒÂ©rÃƒÂ©ment sans garde (SET_NULL : les lignes produit survivent).
- **Categorie / Fournisseur / Marque** Ã¢â‚¬â€ referentials (Marque/`archived`). **ARC18** adds a nullable `Fournisseur.tiers` FK (migration stock/0075, `tiers_bridge.py`) mirroring identity one-way into `tiers.Tiers`. **ARC24** adds an optional `Fournisseur.condition_paiement_ref` FKÃ¢â€ â€™`parametres.ConditionPaiement` mirroring the existing `delai_paiement_jours`/`fin_de_mois`/`escompte_pct` fields (backfilled via `backfill_conditions_paiement`).

- **MouvementStock** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `produit` FK; `type_mouvement` (entree/sortie/transfert/ajustement); `quantite_avant/apres`; `created_by`; the audit trail for every quantity change.

Read cross-app ONLY through `stock.selectors.specs_for_produit`, which returns the block of the `type_fiche` **flat** (not a dict of blocks). **L-DECH** (2026-08-24, migration `stock/0130`) adds the battery-path POWER limits the hourly engine needed: `bat_max_decharge_kw` (discharge **per pack** Ã¢â‚¬â€ summed Ãƒâ€” line quantity by `ventes.etude_horaire.puissances_batterie_des_lignes`, never derived from the charge figure) and `ond_bat_max_charge_kw`/`ond_bat_max_decharge_kw` (the hybrid inverter's battery PORT, the second bottleneck: the served/absorbed power is `min(ÃŽÂ£ packs, ÃŽÂ£ ports)`, in both directions). Values are seeded from official datasheets by `seed_catalogue` (Dyness 100 A Ãƒâ€” 51,2 V; Deye ports 120Ã¢â‚¬â€œ350 A at the same 51,2 V, so both bounds are comparable); NULL means NOT PUBLISHED Ã¢â‚¬â€ the engine then applies the founder's conservative rule rather than a supposed power. **BATHOMO** (2026-08-26, migrations `stock/0132`+`0133`) adds `bat_max_modules_par_banc` on the battery block: a founder-editable cap on the number of IDENTICAL modules a single bank may stack for that product (`MinValueValidator(1)` -- 0 is invalid, NULL means unlimited). `ventes.services.composition_residentielle` rejects (never truncates) a homogeneous candidate that would exceed it; seeded to 200 on the 5 kWh Dyness.
