### pos Ã¢â‚¬â€ vente comptoir / point of sale (Groupe XPOS, new app)  *( `/api/django/pos/` )*
Counter sales of accessories, built entirely on the existing ventes/stock/compta write paths (never a bespoke ledger).
- **Models** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `VenteComptoir` + `LigneVenteComptoir` (XPOS1: a counter sale that, on `valider/`, becomes a real `ventes.Facture` + `Paiement`(s) + a stock movement via `ventes.services`/`stock.services` Ã¢â‚¬â€ never a direct model import; `uuid_client` dedups an OFFLINE-created sale replayed on reconnect, NTRET1; `montant_arrhes`/`marchandise_remise` + `EN_ATTENTE_SOLDE` statut = deposit-on-order for backordered/custom items, NTRET5), `SessionCaisse` (XPOS4: caisse open/close, backed by the EXISTING `compta.Caisse` via `compta.services` Ã¢â‚¬â€ no duplicate cash journal; `numero_rapport_z` race-safe one-shot legal Z-report number, NTRET2; `montant_tpe_compte`/`ecart_tpe` card-terminal reconciliation, XPOS18), `ShareLinkTicket` (XPOS3: tokenized public link to the ticket PDF, same pattern as `ventes.ShareLink`, kept local to `apps/pos`), `CommandeRetrait` + `LigneCommandeRetrait` (XPOS15: click-and-collect, ÃƒÂ _preparerÃ¢â€ â€™pretÃ¢â€ â€™retire), `ConfigMaterielPOS` (XPOS18: ESC/POS printer config + TPE card-terminal reconciliation), `CodePinCaissier` (NTRET3: hashed quick-lock PIN per company+user Ã¢â‚¬â€ switch cashier on a shared till without a full re-login or losing the in-progress cart).
- Endpoints: `ventes/` (+ `{id}/lignes`, `{id}/valider`, `{id}/promotions`, `{id}/coupon`, `{id}/arrhes|solde-arrhes|remettre-marchandise` (NTRET5), `{id}/ticket-pdf|ticket-arrhes-pdf|ticket-escpos|ticket-share-link`, `encaisser-facture/`, `emettre-carte-cadeau/`, `{id}/payer-carte-cadeau`, `factures-recherche/`, `dashboard/`, `dashboard-export/`, `dashboard-retail/` + `dashboard-retail-export/` (NTRET16 Ã¢â‚¬â€ panier moyen/transformation/ventes-mÃ‚Â²/top produits, reads `parametres.BoutiquePos`)), `sessions/` (+ `{id}/cloturer`, `{id}/rapport-x` (NTRET2, in-progress read-only), `{id}/rapport-z|rapport-z-pdf` (official, one-shot numbered)), `retraits/` (+ `{id}/lignes`, `{id}/marquer-pret`, `{id}/remettre`), `config-materiel/`; `verifier-pin/`+`definir-pin/` (NTRET3, PIN lock); public `GET /api/django/public/pos/ticket/<token>/` (tokenized ticket PDF, no auth, same pattern as `ventes.ShareLink`). Promotions/coupons/gift cards are `apps.promotions` models consumed here (never duplicated) Ã¢â‚¬â€ see the `promotions` section below.

### installations Ã¢â‚¬â€ chantiers / field execution
Installation projects spun up once a quote is signed, through to commissioning and
closure; work orders, checklists, regulatory (law 82-21) tracking. **ARC3** sweeps
all 61 installations ViewSets onto `CompanyScopedModelViewSet` (behaviour
identical). **ARC28** adds `apps/installations/platform.py`.
- **Installation** (chantier) Ã¢â‚¬â€ `company` FK; `reference`; `client` FK; `devis` FKÃ¢â€ â€™ventes.Devis; `bon_commande` FKÃ¢â€ â€™ventes.BonCommande; `lead` FKÃ¢â€ â€™crm.Lead; `statut` (SIGNE/MATERIEL_COMMANDE/PLANIFIE/EN_COURS/INSTALLE/RECEPTIONNE/CLOTURE + legacy values); `puissance_installee_kwc`; `type_installation`; `technicien_responsable` FK; `bom` JSON (frozen BoM from devis); `regime_8221` + `dossier_statut` (regulatory); `annule` bool + `motif_annulation`; milestone dates.
- **StockReservation** (N14) Ã¢â‚¬â€ `company` FK; `installation` FK; `produit` FKÃ¢â€ â€™stock.Produit; `quantite`; `consomme` bool (`unique_together (installation, produit)`). Seeded from the chantier's frozen `bom` at creation; consumed exactly once when the chantier reaches the canonical INSTALLE statut (one `MouvementStock` SORTIE per SKU, idempotent under `select_for_update()`/atomic Ã¢â‚¬â€ re-entering INSTALLE emits nothing); cancel/close releases the remaining (un-consumed) reservation. Drives the reservation-aware availability on the stock serializer (rÃƒÂ©servÃƒÂ© vs disponible) and low-stock alerts.
- **Intervention** (sortie chantier, F3) Ã¢â‚¬â€ `installation` FK; `ticket` FKÃ¢â€ â€™sav.Ticket (nullable); `type_intervention` (POSE/RACCORDEMENT/MISE_EN_SERVICE/CONTROLE/DEPANNAGE); `technicien` FK; `equipe` M2MÃ¢â€ â€™users (default = chantier installer, set server-side); `camionnette` FKÃ¢â€ â€™stock.EmplacementStock (nullable); `date_prevue/realisee`; **`statut`** Ã¢â‚¬â€ its OWN ordered state machine (`a_preparer/prete/en_route/sur_site/terminee/validee` + `STATUT_ORDER`, default `a_preparer`) **completely separate from the chantier statut and the STAGES.py contract** (changing it never touches either). RÃƒÂ©f/client/devis/ville/GPS are read-only, pulled from the chantier.
- **InterventionActivity** (F3) Ã¢â‚¬â€ per-intervention chatter (same pattern as InstallationActivity), helper `intervention_activity.py` (creation + tracked-field changes incl. statut + manual notes; user/company server-side).
- **ChecklistTemplate** (N74) Ã¢â‚¬â€ `company` FK; `nom`; `type_installation` (nullable; auto-selects the template for a chantier of that market); `ordre`; `actif`; `protege` (the per-company "DÃƒÂ©faut" fallback that carries today's 7 steps). **ChecklistEtapeModele / ChantierChecklistItem** Ã¢â‚¬â€ template steps (now FKÃ¢â€ â€™ChecklistTemplate, `unique_together (company, template, cle)`) and per-chantier checklist state; `capture_serie` flags serial-number capture steps (feeds the equipment registry); `fait` bool. Auto-selection (`template_for_installation`, services.py) matches by `type_installation`, falls back to DÃƒÂ©faut Ã¢â‚¬â€ behaviour preserved.
- **TypeIntervention / InstallationActivity** Ã¢â‚¬â€ configurable intervention types and chantier chatter.
- **JalonProjet / ModeleProjet (+ ModeleProjetJalon, ModeleProjetBomLigne) / ReunionChantier** (FG293/296/298, `models_projet.py`) Ã¢â‚¬â€ project milestones/phases (ÃƒÂ©tude/appro/pose/MES/rÃƒÂ©ception with `date_cible`/`date_reelle`/`atteint`), chantier-type templates (`services.instantiate_modele_projet` pre-creates standard jalons + appends BoM-type lines to the frozen `bom`, idempotent + additive), and timestamped site-meeting minutes (ordre du jour/prÃƒÂ©sents/dÃƒÂ©cisions/actions, author + company server-side). Endpoints `jalons-projet/`, `modeles-projet/` (+ `{id}/instancier/`), `reunions-chantier/`.
- **FG291 Ã¢â‚¬â€ Projet (programme multi-chantiers, `models_program.py`)** Ã¢â‚¬â€ a `Projet` regrouping the chantiers + devis + tickets of one client/site (ferme ÃƒÂ  4 forages, toiture par tranches), with link tables **ProjetChantier/ProjetDevis/ProjetTicket** referencing other apps by **string FK only** (`ventes.Devis`/`sav.Ticket`/`crm.Client` Ã¢â‚¬â€ resolved via FK metadata + tenant-checked, never imported). Its OWN status machine (`brouillon/actif/en_pause/termine/annule`) Ã¢â‚¬â€ **independent of STAGES.py and of devis/ticket document statuses** (attachment never touches them). Reference `PRG-` via the race-safe factory; company/created_by forced server-side. Idempotent `attacher_chantier|devis|ticket` actions (get_or_create); ViewSets `programmes/`, `programme-chantiers/`, `programme-devis/`, `programme-tickets/`. NEW architectural component.
- **FG292 Ã¢â‚¬â€ ProjetTache** (project tasks/sub-tasks with dependencies, on `models_program.py`): `projet` FKÃ¢â€ â€™Projet (same-app), `parent` self-FK (sub-tasks), `predecesseur` self-FK (dependency), `assigne` FKÃ¢â€ â€™user, `date_echeance`, own `Statut` (a_faire/en_cours/termine Ã¢â‚¬â€ NOT STAGES.py), `ordre`. `clean()` cycle-guards BOTH `parent` and `predecesseur` (rollback in an atomic block). ViewSet `programme-taches/` (`?projet`/`?statut`/`?parent`/`?assigne` filters, tenant checks on every FK).
- **FG294 Ã¢â‚¬â€ BudgetProjet + BudgetEngagement** (budget projet vs rÃƒÂ©el): `BudgetProjet` (1-to-1 with Projet; HT envelopes matÃƒÂ©riel/main-d'Ã…â€œuvre/sous-traitance/divers + `tarif_jour_mo` + `seuil_alerte_pct`) and `BudgetEngagement` (attaches a supplier cost Ã¢â‚¬â€ BCF/facture Ã¢â‚¬â€ by string-FK). `selectors.budget_projet_synthese` aggregates ACTUALS Ã¢â‚¬â€ devis via `apps.get_model('ventes','Devis')`, BCF/FactureFournisseur via function-local `apps.stock.selectors`, labour from same-app Installation Ã¢â‚¬â€ vs budget with an over-budget flag (no cross-app model import; import-linter 4/4). ViewSets `programme-budgets/` (+ `synthese`), `programme-engagements/`. INTERNE (responsable/admin Ã¢â‚¬â€ exposes purchase costs). **FG295** adds `selectors.projet_pnl` Ã¢â‚¬â€ consolidated project P&L: REVENUE (client factures on the project's devis, cancelled excluded) Ã¢Ë†â€™ COSTS (matÃƒÂ©riel/sous-traitance/imports via FG294 engagements + main-d'Ã…â€œuvre) Ã¢â€ â€™ `marge_brute` + `marge_pct`; action `programmes/{id}/pnl/` (responsable/admin, cross-app reads reuse FG294's `get_model`/selector helpers Ã¢â‚¬â€ import-linter safe). No model. **FG299** adds `selectors.plan_de_charge_equipes` Ã¢â‚¬â€ the FIELD-TEAM workload view: per-technicien capacity (working days Ãƒâ€” hours) vs allocated Interventions (principal `technicien` OR `equipe` member, de-duped, windowed) with a `sur_reservation` flag + `charge_pct`; endpoint `interventions/plan-de-charge/?debut=&fin=`. Distinct from gestion_projet's PROJ18 and STAGES.py. No model. **FG300** adds `selectors.conflits_affectation` Ã¢â‚¬â€ double-booking detection: same technicien (principal/ÃƒÂ©quipe) or same camionnette on Ã¢â€°Â¥2 interventions the same day (de-duped, material assets excluded), at `interventions/conflits-affectation/?debut=&fin=`. No model. **FG301** adds `selectors.nivellement_charge` (resource levelling) Ã¢â‚¬â€ proposes moving interventions off overloaded technicians to under-loaded ones **without creating an FG300 same-day conflict** (read-only proposal, mutates nothing), at `interventions/nivellement-charge/`. No model. **FG302** adds **IndisponibiliteRessource** (`models_indispo.py`) Ã¢â‚¬â€ a field-resource unavailability calendar: a `technicien` XOR `camionnette` absent over `[date_debut, date_fin]`, `type_indispo` congÃƒÂ©/formation/arrÃƒÂªt/autre; ViewSet `indisponibilites-ressource/` + `selectors.ressource_indisponible` that FG299/FG300/FG301 can call to exclude unavailable resources. Migration installations/0019.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/installations/`): `chantiers/` ViewSet + `creer-depuis-devis`, `regime-suggestion`, `{id}/historique|noter|mise-en-service|annuler|reactiver`, `{id}/checklist|cocher-checklist`, `{id}/besoin-materiel|commander-besoin` (now reports a per-SKU `reserve`); `interventions/` (F3: `?statut=`/`?type_intervention=`/`?installation=` filters + `{id}/historique|noter`); `types-intervention/`; `checklist-etapes/` (filterable `?template=`); `checklist-templates/` (N74, named template CRUD, DÃƒÂ©faut delete-protected). Frontend route `/interventions` (F4, CHANTIERS menu): list + statut kanban (drag-to-change-status, technicien reassign). **FG303** adds `selectors.planning_camionnettes(company, debut, fin)` Ã¢â‚¬â€ a per-van calendar grouping interventions (via `Intervention.camionnette`) with a daily load, zero-capacity on FG302 indisponibilitÃƒÂ©s (over-reservation visible), at the `planning-camionnettes` read action (IsAnyRole). No model. **FG304** adds **SousTraitant** Ã¢â‚¬â€ a subcontractor registry (mÃƒÂ©tier/contact/ICE/RIB + `actif` archive flag, default True content-type-independent), DISTINCT from material suppliers; company+created_by server-side. ViewSet `sous-traitants/` (mÃƒÂ©tier/actif filters, search). Migration installations/0020. **FG305** adds **OrdreSousTraitance** Ã¢â‚¬â€ subcontractor work orders (FKÃ¢â€ â€™SousTraitant FG304 + same-app chantier, race-safe `OST-` ref, prestation/montant/ÃƒÂ©chÃƒÂ©ance, cycle brouillonÃ¢â€ â€™ÃƒÂ©misÃ¢â€ â€™en_coursÃ¢â€ â€™rÃƒÂ©ceptionnÃƒÂ©Ã¢â€ â€™clos with lifecycle actions). ViewSet `ordres-sous-traitance/`. Migration installations/0021. **SCA34** converts `OrdreSousTraitance` into the FIRST pilot of the `core.documents.DocumentMetier` kit Ã¢â‚¬â€ a mixed conversion (redeclares `company` byte-identical, gains `created_at`/`updated_at` additively alongside the historical `date_creation`/`date_modification`, widens `statut` 20Ã¢â€ â€™32 chars for the abstract field, same 5 choices/default); reference format (`OST-YYYYMM-NNNN`) and the 3 existing lifecycle actions are unchanged (non-regression tested). **SCA36** converts `DemandeAchat` (models_demande_achat.py) into the kit's THIRD pilot (`LigneDocumentMetier` without a totals mixin Ã¢â‚¬â€ a purchase requisition has no money total). **NTP2P40** adds `demandes-achat/<id>/importer-lignes-csv/` Ã¢â‚¬â€ bulk-add requisition lines from an uploaded CSV. **NTP2P41** adds `rfq/<id>/comparatif-pdf/` (print-friendly PDF of `selectors.rfq_comparatif`) and `rfq/<id>/export-xlsx/` (one tab per consulted supplier + a comparatif tab, `apps.records.xlsx.build_workbook`) alongside the FG311 RFQ multi-supplier comparatif. **NTMOB21** adds `views_meteo.py`/`weather.py` Ã¢â‚¬â€ `GET installations/meteo/` (site weather for planning). **NTP2P2** adds **RegleApprobationAchat** + **EtapeApprobationAchat** (`models_approbation_achat.py`, migration installations/0101) Ã¢â‚¬â€ a same-app sequential approval plan for `DemandeAchat`, opt-in per company: a rule matches by montant bracket + optional chantier/programme scope (most-specific wins) and can `autorise_depassement_budget` (NTP2P4); no matching rule = the historical direct approve/refuse behaviour, byte-identical. ViewSet `regles-approbation-achat/`; `demandes-achat/{id}/` gains `etapes-approbation/` (GET), `approuver-etape/`, `rejeter-etape/` Ã¢â‚¬â€ the final step flips the demande to `approuvee`, a rejection to `refusee`. `services.controler_budget_demande_achat` (called at `soumettre`) reaches `stock.selectors/services` (never `stock.models`) to check the NTP2P4 department budget before the workflow starts.

### outillage Ã¢â‚¬â€ durable field tools & kits (F1/F2)
Durable tooling (drills, ladders, metersÃ¢â‚¬Â¦), tracked **strictly separate from the consumable stock catalogue** Ã¢â‚¬â€ never sellable, never consumed, never on a client-facing document.
- **Outillage** (F1) Ã¢â‚¬â€ `company` FK; `nom`; `categorie` (free text); `asset_tag`; `numero_serie`; `emplacement` FKÃ¢â€ â€™stock.EmplacementStock (nullable; the tool's home location among the existing dÃƒÂ©pÃƒÂ´t/camionnette); `statut` (DISPONIBLE/EN_INTERVENTION/EN_REPARATION/PERDU); `date_achat`; `note`. Optional photo via the generic `records.Attachment` (`outillage.outillage` whitelisted in `records.ALLOWED_TARGETS`).
- **KitOutillage / KitOutillageItem** (F2) Ã¢â‚¬â€ named, reusable tooling kit templates editable in ParamÃƒÂ¨tres; each an ordered list of catalogue tools (`KitOutillageItem.outil` FKÃ¢â€ â€™Outillage, `ordre`, `unique_together (kit, outil)`); `type_intervention` (TypeIntervention key) pre-selects a kit; `actif` toggle. Three defaults (pose structure / raccordement / mise en service) seeded on first list (idempotent), fully editable.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/outillage/`): `outils/` (read any role, write responsable/admin; filter `?statut=`/`?emplacement=`, search nom/asset_tag/numero_serie/categorie), `kits/` (seed-on-list, write admin), `kit-items/` (write admin, item company follows its kit). Frontend route `/outillage` (CHANTIERS menu) + ParamÃƒÂ¨tres Ã¢â€ â€™ Ã‚Â« Kits d'outillage Ã‚Â» tab.

### sav Ã¢â‚¬â€ after-sales: equipment registry, tickets, maintenance contracts
Tracks installed equipment + warranty clocks and the SAV ticket lifecycle.
- **Equipement** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `company` FK; `produit` FKÃ¢â€ â€™stock.Produit; `installation` FKÃ¢â€ â€™installations.Installation; `numero_serie`; `date_pose`; `date_fin_garantie(_production)` (computed from `date_pose` + product warranty); `statut` (EN_SERVICE/REMPLACE/HORS_SERVICE); `remplace_par_ticket` FKÃ¢â€ â€™Ticket.
- **Ticket** (SAV) Ã¢â‚¬â€ `company` FK; `reference`; `client` FK; `installation` FK (nullable); `equipement` FK (nullable); `type` (CORRECTIF/PREVENTIF); `statut` (NOUVEAU/PLANIFIE/EN_COURS/RESOLU/CLOTURE); `priorite`; `sous_garantie` (OUI/NON/A_DETERMINER, computed from equipment warranty if linked); `cout` (internal, never client-facing); `annule` bool + `motif_annulation`.
- **TicketActivity** Ã¢â‚¬â€ ticket chatter. **ContratMaintenance** Ã¢â‚¬â€ preventive contracts (`periodicite`, `date_debut`, `derniere_visite`, `actif`, `duree_mois`, `date_renouvellement`).
- **PieceConsommee** (N46) Ã¢â‚¬â€ parts consumed on a SAV ticket: `company` FK; `ticket` FKÃ¢â€ â€™Ticket; `produit` FKÃ¢â€ â€™stock.Produit; `quantite`; `stock_decremente` (guards double stock moves). Shown on the intervention report by designation/marque/quantitÃƒÂ© only Ã¢â‚¬â€ never buy price or margin; recording it can decrement stock via `MouvementStock`.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/sav/`): `equipements/`, `tickets/` (+ `{id}/historique|noter|annuler|reactiver|rapport-pdf`), `contrats-maintenance/`. **NTMOB23** adds `equipements/<id>/partage-qr/` Ã¢â‚¬â€ a QR-shareable equipment record for field techs. **FG280** adds **AlarmeOnduleur** Ã¢â‚¬â€ inverter alarms/faults DISTINCT from the SAV ticket (code/gravitÃƒÂ© info-warning-critique/ÃƒÂ©quipement, statut active/acquittee/resolue/escaladee), with `acquitter` (server-side user+date, idempotent) and `escalader` (links or opens a SAV ticket) actions; ViewSet `alarmes-onduleur/`. Migration sav/0011.
- **ARC4** sweeps all 15 sav ViewSets onto `CompanyScopedModelViewSet`. **ARC37** makes `sav` an emitter on the `core.events` bus Ã¢â‚¬â€ `services.emettre_changement_statut_ticket` fires `ticket_resolu`/`equipement_remplace` at the guarded transition site (never a second state machine). **ARC28/29/30/33** add `apps/sav/platform.py`.

### portail Ã¢â‚¬â€ client self-service portal (Groupe FG228-233, new app)  *( `/api/django/portail/` )*
The Odoo Portal equivalent: a client logs in to see their own devis/factures/chantiers/tickets. Originally built inside `compta`; **ODX12** moved the models here with `db_table = 'compta_<model>'` (`SeparateDatabaseAndState`, state-only Ã¢â‚¬â€ no data moved) + a re-export shim left in `apps/compta/models.py`. Links to `crm.Client`/`crm.Lead`/devis/factures/chantiers/tickets are STRING-FK (never a `models` import of ventes/crm/sav/installations). **WIR95** upgraded these from opaque `PositiveIntegerField` ids to REAL `ForeignKey`s string-referencing the target model (`'ventes.Devis'`, `'facturation.Facture'`, `'crm.Client'`, `'crm.Lead'`, `'installations.Installation'`, `'sav.Ticket'`) with `on_delete=SET_NULL, db_constraint=False` Ã¢â‚¬â€ same pattern as `pos.CommandeRetrait.devis`: no DB-level FK constraint (the 5 domains stay decoupled), but a deletion via the ORM now nulls the reference instead of leaving a silent orphan id; the `_id` attname stays usable everywhere the old field was read. Auth surface (portal tokens/accounts) preserved byte-identical Ã¢â‚¬â€ no access widened by the split.
- **Models** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `ComptePortailClient` (tokenized client access account, reuses the client's email Ã¢â‚¬â€ no 2nd identity copy per DC32), `AcceptationDevisPortail` (quote e-acceptance from the portal, `devis` FK), `PaiementFacturePortail` (self-service invoice payment record, `facture` FK), `DocumentClientPortail` (client-visible document refs, `client`/`lead` FK Ã¢â‚¬â€ **WIR94**: the upload is now ALSO deposited as a real `ged.Document` via `ged.services.deposit_document` (lazy cross-app call, never a `ged.models` import) and referenced by `document_ged`; the historical `fichier` FileField is kept for backward compat), `JalonChantierPortail` (site-progress milestones surfaced to the client), `DemandeTicketPortail` (SAV ticket requests raised from the portal).
- Endpoints (new prefix; the SAME ViewSets are also still served under `/api/django/compta/Ã¢â‚¬Â¦` for backward compatibility, incl. the public tokenized `portail/<token>/Ã¢â‚¬Â¦` views which stay on compta): `comptes-portail/`, `acceptations-devis-portail/`, `paiements-facture-portail/`, `documents-client-portail/`, `jalons-chantier-portail/`, `demandes-ticket-portail/`.

### reporting Ã¢â‚¬â€ dashboards, KPIs, insights, audit log  *(no models)*
Read-only aggregation across crm/ventes/installations/sav/stock, role-filtered.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/reporting/`): `dashboard/`, `search/`, `notifications/`, `calendar/` and `calendar/reschedule/` (agenda events + drag-reschedule), `pipeline/` (funnel value by STAGES, weighted forecast), `reports/sales|stock|service/` (+`?export=xlsx`), `insights/recurring-revenue|audit-log|job-costing|analytics|commissions/`, `archive/client/{id}/` and `archive/chantier/{id}/`. `job-costing` (margin via internal `prix_achat`) and `commissions` (sales commission per `CompanyProfile.commission_mode`) are admin-only.
- **RapportDefinition** (NTEXT10, `rapport_builder.py`, previously undocumented here) Ã¢â‚¬â€ a rejouable cross-tab report definition (source query + optional `pivot_spec`); endpoint `rapport-definitions/` (+ an execute action building rows via `PivotSpec`/`build_pivot`). This batch's **PACT146** fixes the execute response shape: without a `pivot_spec` the payload is now a LITERAL `{'rows': rows}` (the `pivot` key stays absent, not a dynamically-omitted one Ã¢â‚¬â€ `scripts/check_api_shapes.py` only sees literal dict keys) and gives it its first screen, `/reporting/rapport-builder`. **PACT144** gives the pre-existing `ai_governance` "rapport pÃƒÂ©riodique" feature (see below) its first screen, `/reporting/rapport-periode`. **NTEXT11** adds `rapport-definitions/{id}/export/?format=csv|xlsx` Ã¢â‚¬â€ a file export of the replayed definition, reusing the existing subscription-export infra, buy-price/margin columns stripped.

### parametres Ã¢â‚¬â€ company profile, business settings, WhatsApp templates
- **CompanyProfile** (one per company) Ã¢â‚¬â€ identity + Moroccan legal IDs (`ice`, `identifiant_fiscal`, `rc`, `patente`, `cnss`, `rib`); branding (`logo_key`, `signature_key`, `couleur_principale`); `responsable_defaut_leads` FK (default lead owner); quote-gen knobs (`payment_terms` JSON, `quote_validity_days`, `tva_standard/panneaux`, ROI constants `onee_tarif_kwh`/`productible_kwh_kwc`/`rendement_global`, `remise_max_pct`, `discount_approval_threshold`, `agricole_pump_hours`); `default_installer` FK (default technician for new chantiers, N66; NULL = creator is responsable); sales commission (`commission_mode` off/pct_devis/par_kwc + `commission_valeur`, sensitive/admin-only, N99); referral toggle (`referral_enabled` bool + `referral_reward`, N98); silent DGI export master switch (`dgi_export_actif` bool, **default OFF**, N105 Ã¢â‚¬â€ arms the ventes `dgi/` capability, invisible while off); `doc_prefixes`/`doc_numbering` JSON. **SCA27** adds `site_web` (migration 0055) Ã¢â‚¬â€ the tenant's own site URL, consumed by the quote-engine residential PDF footer/legal band instead of a hardcoded founder fallback (byte-identical repli when empty).
- **TauxTVA / ConditionPaiement / UniteMesure** (ARC23/24/27, `models_taxes.py`/`models_payment_terms.py`/`models_units.py`) Ã¢â‚¬â€ per-company referentials, each a `core.TenantModel` subclass (redeclares `company` for its own `related_name`, ARC1 motif): `TauxTVA` (code/libelle/taux, Moroccan rates 20/14/10/7/0, one `defaut=True`) feeds the default rate at devis/facture creation without rewriting an already-frozen document rate; `CompanyProfile.tva_standard` (existing knob, editable in ParamÃƒÂ¨tres) takes PRECEDENCE over the referential's seeded default. `ConditionPaiement` (libellÃƒÂ©/dÃƒÂ©lai jours/fin de mois/escompte %) sources the default `Facture.conditions_paiement` label and mirrors `stock.Fournisseur`'s existing numeric fields. `UniteMesure` (code/libellÃƒÂ©) mirrors `stock.Produit.unite_stock` (which stays master). All three purely additive/declarative Ã¢â‚¬â€ absent referential falls back to prior hardcoded behaviour; seeded at signup, backfillable via `backfill_conditions_paiement`/`backfill_unites_mesure` management commands.
- **MessageTemplate** Ã¢â‚¬â€ WhatsApp templates by `cle` (devis/facture/relance), `corps_fr` + `corps_darija`.
- **EmailTemplate** (FG17, in `models_email.py`) Ã¢â‚¬â€ editable e-mail templates by `cle` (devis/facture/relance/notification): `sujet` + `corps` with the same placeholder whitelist as WhatsApp (`{civilite}{nom}{reference}{lien}{n}`), `unique_together company+cle`. Helpers `EmailTemplate.get_template`/`render` (tolerant) for the future automation-email rewire (intentionally NOT wired yet). Endpoints `email-templates/` (CRUD) + `email-templates/effective/` (defaultsÃ¢Å â€¢overrides) + `email-templates/bulk/` (upsert), writes audited.
- **SettingsAuditLog** Ã¢â‚¬â€ who changed which setting field.
- **StatutConfig** (N58, in `models_statuses.py`) Ã¢â‚¬â€ per-company display overlay for chantier/SAV/bon-de-commande statuses: `domaine` + canonical `cle` + `libelle` + `ordre` + `actif` (`unique_together company+domaine+cle`). Display-only Ã¢â‚¬â€ canonical keys & state machines stay in their source models; defaults read live from `Installation.STATUT_ORDER`/`Ticket.STATUT_ORDER`/`BonCommande.Statut` (`statuses_defaults.py`), so output is byte-identical until edited.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/parametres/`): `GET /`, `PUT/PATCH /update/`, `POST /upload-logo|upload-signature/`, `DELETE /delete-logo|delete-signature/`, `GET+PUT/PATCH /messages/`, `GET /audit/`; `statuts/` ViewSet (N58) + `statuts/effective/?domaine=` (full ordered effective list) + `statuts/bulk/` (upsert a domaine). Reads `GET /` and `GET /messages/` are open to any role; every write/audit endpoint (incl. `statuts/` writes) is the Administrateur + Responsable tier (`IsAdminOrResponsableTier`), limited tier blocked.
- **NTEXT19** adds `gabarits.py`/`views_gabarits.py` Ã¢â‚¬â€ read-only custom document templates (`gabarits-document/`) + `gabarits-document/<code>/rendre/?cible_id=<id>` (renders the template as PDF for one target record). **NTEXT39** adds `gabarits-document/<code>/apercu/` Ã¢â‚¬â€ renders the same template with SAMPLE data (no real record touched), streamed as `<code>-apercu.pdf`.
- **NTEXT19** adds `gabarits.py`/`views_gabarits.py` Ã¢â‚¬â€ read-only custom document templates (`gabarits-document/`) + `gabarits-document/<code>/rendre/?cible_id=<id>` (renders the template as PDF for one target record).
- **ParametresPos / BoutiquePos** (NTRET8, `models_pos.py`) Ã¢â‚¬â€ ParamÃƒÂ¨tres Ã¢â€ â€™ Point de vente section (`PointDeVenteSection.jsx`, embedded in `ParametresEntreprise`, no dedicated route). `ParametresPos` (one per company) carries `taux_horaire_comptoir` (counter labour rate, distinct from `CompanyProfile.taux_horaire_sav`); the line-discount threshold and printer/TPE config are REUSED as-is from `CompanyProfile.discount_approval_threshold` and `pos.ConfigMaterielPOS` Ã¢â‚¬â€ never duplicated here. `BoutiquePos` marks an EXISTING `stock.EmplacementStock` (string-FK) as a physical point of sale, with its own receipt address/hours + `surface_m2` (NTRET16, feeds the POS retail dashboard's sales-per-mÃ‚Â² KPI). Endpoints: `GET /parametres/pos/` + `PUT /parametres/pos/update/`, `pos-boutiques/` ViewSet.
- **Licence tier (NTADM7/8, 2026-08-05)** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `CompanyProfile.plan` (nullable FKÃ¢â€ â€™`adminops.PlanLicence`, NULL = full access) + `nb_sieges_max` (nullable = unlimited); `parametres/feature_flags.py::has_feature(company, module_key)` (never restrictive by default Ã¢â‚¬â€ foundation only, nav masking stays on FG391/ODX); `services_licence.py` records plan changes in `SettingsAuditLog` + fires the `plan.changed` webhook; seat counting = `authentication.services.sieges_utilises`.

### scm Ã¢â‚¬â€ supply chain planning (Groupe NTSCM, new app)  *( `/api/django/scm/` )*
Demand forecasting, ABC classification, stock policies, a consolidated reappro dashboard, and a monthly S&OP cycle Ã¢â‚¬â€ a PLANNING layer above `apps.stock`'s existing execution (basic reappro/RFQ/DemandeAchat/transfers/supplier scorecard); reads `apps.stock` only via its `selectors.py`/`services.py`, never a model import.
- **PrevisionDemande** Ã¢â‚¬â€ per produit/segment/period (`YYYY-MM`) demand forecast; `methode` (moyenne_mobile/tendance/saisonnier/manuel). **EvenementDemande** Ã¢â‚¬â€ a signed % demand impact (promo/chantier majeur/rupture fournisseur/saisonnalitÃƒÂ©) over a date window, applied by `services.generer_previsions`.
- **ClassificationABC** Ã¢â‚¬â€ Pareto A/B/C ranking, recalculated server-side only (`selectors.classifier_abc`) and persisted HERE (not on `stock.Produit`, cross-app boundary) as a OneToOne. **PolitiqueStock** Ã¢â‚¬â€ per-produit min/max/ROP/safety-stock policy; `service_level_pct` seeded from the ABC class (A=95/B=90/C=85) but stays editable; ROP/safety-stock derived fields written ONLY by `services.recalculer_politiques_stock`.
- **CyclePlanificationSOP** Ã¢â‚¬â€ monthly S&OP cycle, a SEQUENTIAL state machine (brouillonÃ¢â€ â€™revue_demandeÃ¢â€ â€™revue_offreÃ¢â€ â€™revue_financeÃ¢â€ â€™rÃƒÂ©union_rÃƒÂ©conciliationÃ¢â€ â€™approuvÃƒÂ©Ã¢â€ â€™clos) advanced only via `services.avancer_statut_cycle` (no step-skipping) or reopened via `services.reouvrir_cycle` (admin-only, audited). **LigneDemandeSOP** Ã¢â‚¬â€ a FROZEN snapshot of consensus demand per produit, taken automatically on brouillonÃ¢â€ â€™revue_demande. **LigneOffreSOP** Ã¢â‚¬â€ mirrored supply snapshot (stock disponible + capacitÃƒÂ© appro fournisseur) vs the frozen demand, surfacing the offreÃ¢Ë†â€™demande gap.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/scm/`): `previsions-demande/` (+ `generer`), `evenements-demande/`, `classification-abc/` (+ `recalculer`), `politiques-stock/` (+ `recalculer`), `cycles-sop/` (+ `{id}/avancer-statut|reouvrir|historique|lignes-demande|ajuster-demande|calculer-offre|ecarts|impact-financier`), `tableau-bord-reappro/` (+ `creer-bcf`, groups lines by supplier into draft `stock.BonCommandeFournisseur`s via `stock.services`). Responsable/admin only. Frontend: `/scm/reappro` + `/scm/sop` (admin-only) Ã¢â‚¬â€ see Ã‚Â§5.

### mrp Ã¢â‚¬â€ Production / MRP II (Groupe NTMFG, new app)  *( `/api/django/mrp/` )*
Generic manufacturing engine Ã¢â‚¬â€ work centers, routings, capacitated manufacturing orders, MRP net-requirements, finite-capacity scheduling, an MES shop-floor terminal, and standard/actual costing. Distinct and complementary to `installations`' existing lightweight kitting (`Kit`/`KitComposant`/`OrdreAssemblage`) Ã¢â‚¬â€ never rebuilt here; reads `installations`/`stock` only via `selectors.py`/`services.py` or string-FK.
- **PosteDeCharge** Ã¢â‚¬â€ work center (machine/ligne/manuel/sous-traitÃƒÂ©), daily capacity + an INTERNAL hourly cost (never client-facing, like `Produit.prix_achat`); optional `sous_traitant` FKÃ¢â€ â€™`stock.Fournisseur`.
- **Gamme** + **OperationGamme** Ã¢â‚¬â€ a reusable routing (ordered poste+temps standard) for one produit, versioned; optional `kit_source` FKÃ¢â€ â€™`stock.KitProduit` read-only for backflush at OF closure when no `kit_ordre_assemblage` link exists.
- **OrdreFabrication** (OF) Ã¢â‚¬â€ capacitated manufacturing order; EXTENDS (never duplicates) `installations.OrdreAssemblage` via an optional `kit_ordre_assemblage` link (when present, that model owns the stock movement). **OperationOF** + **PauseOperationOF** Ã¢â‚¬â€ instantiated-from-gamme operations with MES start/pause/resume/finish timestamps + scrap tracking. **ReservationOF** Ã¢â‚¬â€ internal component reservation bookkeeping (does NOT affect the cross-app `stock.services.available_quantity`, which stays driven by `installations` reservations only).
- **CoutStandard** Ã¢â‚¬â€ versioned, immutable-once-created standard cost (matiÃƒÂ¨re/main-d'Ã…â€œuvre/indirect %) per produit Ã¢â‚¬â€ strictly internal, never in a client document.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/mrp/`): `postes-charge/` (+ `{id}/oee`), `gammes/`, `operations-gamme/`, `ordres-fabrication/` (+ `{id}/confirmer|cloturer|annuler|dispo-composants`), `operations-of/` (+ `{id}/replanifier|demarrer|pauser|reprendre|terminer`), `couts-standard/` (+ `figer`), `mrp-run/`, `charge-postes/`, `analyse-couts/`, `oee-postes/`. Frontend: `/mrp/ordres-fabrication|gantt|terminal|oee|analyse-couts` Ã¢â‚¬â€ see Ã‚Â§5.

### transport Ã¢â‚¬â€ transport orders (Groupe SUPPLY/NTLOG1-20, new app)  *( `/api/django/transport/` )*
EnlÃƒÂ¨vement/livraison/inter-site/import/export transport orders, from planning through delivery, with freight cost tracking and carrier disputes. Cross-app links (`ventes`/`stock`/`installations`/`flotte`) are ALL opaque integer string-FKs (`<app>_<model>_id`), resolved at read time via the target app's selectors or a function-local `apps.get_model` Ã¢â‚¬â€ never a static model import. History/attachments reuse the generic `records.Activity`/`records.Attachment` Ã¢â‚¬â€ no new `*Activity`/`FileField`.
- **OrdreTransport** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `numero` (race-safe `core.numbering`); `type_flux` (enlÃƒÂ¨vement_livraison/inter_site/import/export); its OWN `statut` machine (brouillon/planifiÃƒÂ©/en_cours/livrÃƒÂ©/annulÃƒÂ©, independent of STAGES.py); `mode_transport` (flotte_propre/affrÃƒÂ¨tement) + `mode_acheminement_physique` (route/mer/air, feeds the CO2 estimate). **LigneOrdreTransport** Ã¢â‚¬â€ merchandise (produit opaque ref, poids/volume/valeur dÃƒÂ©clarÃƒÂ©e). **EtapeTransport** Ã¢â‚¬â€ enlÃƒÂ¨vementÃ¢â€ â€™transitÃ¢â€ â€™livraison steps.
- **CoutFretReel** Ã¢â‚¬â€ actual freight cost lines (transport/assurance/manutention/dÃƒÂ©douanement), the read point a future landed-cost extension (FG316) can consume. **LitigeTransport** Ã¢â‚¬â€ an independent carrier-dispute state machine (ouvert/en_traitement/rÃƒÂ©solu/rejetÃƒÂ©), modeled on `litiges.Reclamation` without importing it; transitions log to the parent order's chatter. **ReserveReception** Ã¢â‚¬â€ a reception-time reserve (photos via `records.Attachment`) that automatically opens a `LitigeTransport`.
- **FacteurEmissionCO2** Ã¢â‚¬â€ per-company, per-mode (route/mer/air) kg-CO2-per-tonne-km factor, editable in ParamÃƒÂ¨tres.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/transport/`): `ordres-transport/` (+ `{id}/etapes|comparer-transporteurs|co2|livrer`), `lignes-transport/`, `etapes-transport/`, `couts-fret/`, `litiges-transport/` (+ `{id}/prendre-en-charge|resoudre|rejeter|reclamer-transporteur`), `reserves-reception/`, `facteurs-emission-co2/`. Frontend: `/transport/ordres` Ã¢â‚¬â€ see Ã‚Â§5.

### douane Ã¢â‚¬â€ customs & import/export (Groupe NTLOG, new app)  *( `/api/django/douane/` )*
Currently EXPORT-only (NTLOG14): `DossierExport`/`PieceDossierExport`/`ParametresDouane`. **The import side does not exist here** Ã¢â‚¬â€ NTLOG10's original plan conflicts with the pre-existing `installations.DossierImport` (FG315, owned by the PLAN_SERVICE domain); NTLOG10 stays `[BLOCKED]` pending a reconciliation that must edit `apps/installations` (out of this lane's write scope), and NTLOG11/12/13/21/22/26/30 all depend on it and are BLOCKED with it. **Trap for a future session: `douane.DossierImport` does NOT exist Ã¢â‚¬â€ do not assume it.**

### ecommerce_connect Ã¢â‚¬â€ Shopify/WooCommerce connectors (Groupe NTRET18/19, new app)  *( `/api/django/ecommerce-connect/` )*
**[GATED: no API key/secret in .env]** Ã¢â‚¬â€ every sync degrades to a total no-op (zero network calls); this module stores NO secret in the database, only non-sensitive metadata. `produit_id`/`facture_id` reference `stock.Produit`/`ventes.Facture` by opaque integer, never a cross-app FK. No frontend screen this batch.

### promotions Ã¢â‚¬â€ cart promotion engine (Groupe NTRET12/13/15, new app)  *( `/api/django/promotions/` )*
Configurable cart promotion rules, unique-code coupons, and gift cards Ã¢â‚¬â€ consumed by `apps.pos` at checkout (`services.evaluer_panier`, the ONLY entry point `pos.services` calls). The calculation itself lives in a pure, ORM-free `engine.py`; `services.py` is the ORMÃ¢â€ â€engine bridge. `categorie`/`produit` reference `apps.stock` by string-FK only. No frontend screen of its own Ã¢â‚¬â€ consumed inside the `pos` coquille (Ã‚Â§5).
- **ReglexPromotion** Ã¢â‚¬â€ a configurable rule: `type_regle` (remise_%_produit/remise_montant_panier/n_pour_m/plage_horaire "happy hour"), `priorite` + `cumulable` (non-cumulable rules: only the most advantageous applicable one is kept), optional targeting (`categorie`/`produit`/`montant_min_panier`) and validity window/days/hours. The only model with a CRUD ViewSet Ã¢â‚¬â€ read open to any role (the till needs to know active rules), write responsable/admin.
- **CouponUnique** + **CouponUtilisation** (NTRET13) Ã¢â‚¬â€ a single-code coupon tied to ONE `ReglexPromotion`, `mode_limite` (unique_par_client/global), consumed via `services.consommer_coupon` (server-side, journaled Ã¢â‚¬â€ no standalone CRUD, only through `pos`'s `coupon` action). Distinct from `compta.CodePromotion` (generic marketing campaign code, no usage tracking).
- **CarteCadeau** (NTRET15) Ã¢â‚¬â€ a gift card, sold at the till (no stock line Ã¢â‚¬â€ not an inventoried product), usable as a partial/total payment method across multiple visits until exhausted; debited via `services.debiter_carte_cadeau` (`select_for_update`, never negative).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/promotions/`): `regles/` (+ `simuler`, a dry-run cart-promotion preview for the till, no sale created). Coupons/gift cards have no standalone CRUD Ã¢â‚¬â€ consumed through `pos`'s `{id}/coupon`, `emettre-carte-cadeau/`, `{id}/payer-carte-cadeau` actions.

### roles Ã¢â‚¬â€ RBAC  *( `/api/django/roles/` )*
- **Role** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `company` FK; `nom` (unique per company); `permissions` JSON (validated against canonical `ALL_PERMISSIONS`); `est_systeme` bool (system roles undeletable). Linked from `CustomUser.role`.
- 2026-06-18 (Feature D): `ALL_PERMISSIONS` expanded to a moduleÃƒâ€”action grid + governance codes (`*_export`, `crm/ventes/sav_reassign`, `technicien_assign`, `prix_achat_voir`, `journal_activite_voir`, scope markers `records_scope_equipe`/`records_scope_sous_arbre`). **NTCPQ36** adds 4 CPQ permission codes: `cpq_regles_gerer`, `cpq_prix_contractuels_gerer`, `cpq_marge_voir` (ELEVATED tier, same palier as `marge_voir`/`prix_achat_voir`), `cpq_approbation_approuver`. `CANONICAL_SYSTEM_ROLES` seeds **seven** roles per company Ã¢â‚¬â€ Directeur, Administrateur (=Admin), Commercial responsable, Commercial, Technicien responsable, Technicien, Viewer Ã¢â‚¬â€ plus the legacy Responsable/Utilisateur kept for existing accounts. Seeder: `init_roles` (also maps ownersÃ¢â€ â€™Directeur, custom commercialÃ¢â€ â€™Commercial; N103: self-heals a drifted same-named system role to `est_systeme=True`). `role_tiers.py` now derives the tier from the authoritative permission signal first (`roles_gerer`Ã¢â€ â€™admin, `users_voir`Ã¢â€ â€™responsable) with the name mapping as fallback Ã¢â‚¬â€ so a Directeur/Administrateur whose seeded row drifted to `est_systeme=False` still resolves to the admin tier and keeps access to `/users/` and `/roles/` (N103 regression fix), without widening Commercial/Technicien/Viewer.
- Endpoints: Role ViewSet (CRUD, open to the Administrateur + Responsable tier via `IsAdminOrResponsableTier` Ã¢â‚¬â€ limited tier blocked; delete blocked if system or in-use) + `permissions-disponibles/`.

### identity Ã¢â‚¬â€ security & access foundation (Groupe NTSEC, new app)  *( `/api/django/identity/` )*
Company-scoped security controls, all INERT by default (off-by-default `mode`/toggle, never blocks a request until explicitly configured).
- **Models** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `NetworkPolicy` + `IpAllowRule` (NTSEC11: per-company IP/CIDR allowlist, `mode` off/monitor/enforce, enforced by `middleware.NetworkPolicyMiddleware`), `TrustedDevice` (NTSEC14: "remember this device" Ã¢â‚¬â€ skips MFA on a non-expired/non-revoked trusted device only if the company opts in), `IdentityProvider` (NTSEC1: per-company SSO SAML/OIDC federation metadata), `ScimToken` + `ScimGroupMapping` (NTSEC5/6: SCIM 2.0 provisioning bearer token, hashed like `publicapi.ApiKey`; SCIM-groupÃ¢â€ â€™`roles.Role` mapping via string-id, no `roles.models` import), `BreakGlassGrant` (NTSEC22: time-boxed audited emergency Administrateur elevation that bypasses `enforce_sso`, gated `apps.identity.selectors.is_break_glass_active`).
- Endpoints: `network-policies/`, `ip-allow-rules/`, `trusted-devices/`, `providers/`; SCIM 2.0 `scim/v2/<company_slug>/Users|Groups` (token-authenticated, machine-to-machine); `service-accounts/` (NTSEC24, model lives in `publicapi`, exposed here); `login-banner/` (public); `break-glass/` and `posture/` (NTSEC27 consolidated security posture) Ã¢â‚¬â€ both Directeur-only.

### accessreview Ã¢â‚¬â€ access governance (NTSEC19, new app)  *( `/api/django/accessreview/` )*
- **Models** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `AccessReviewCampaign` (periodic access-review campaign: pÃƒÂ©rimÃƒÂ¨tre all/role/module, dates, statut ouverte/Ã¢â‚¬Â¦), `AccessReviewItem` (one row per in-scope user, attested or revoked by a manager Ã¢â‚¬â€ revocation removes the role via `apps.roles.services`, never a direct `roles.models` import), `SodRule` (separation-of-duties: a conflicting permission pair, `severite` info/warning/critique Ã¢â‚¬â€ critique can BLOCK a role assignment that would create the cumul).

- **WIR136** Ã¢â‚¬â€ mounting dedup: `identity`/`accessreview` were each mounted TWICE in `erp_agentique/urls.py` Ã¢â‚¬â€ `identity` once inside the shared `_APP_URLS` list (Ã¢â€ â€™ both `api/django/` and `api/v1/`) and AGAIN standalone at `api/django/identity/` (harmless duplicate); `accessreview` was mounted ONLY standalone at `api/django/`, so it never got an `api/v1/accessreview/` mirror like every other app. Both are now solely in `_APP_URLS` Ã¢â‚¬â€ same `api/django/Ã¢â‚¬Â¦` paths as before, `accessreview` gains its missing `api/v1/` mirror, `frais` (new this run) joins the same list.

### audit Ã¢â‚¬â€ activity log (audit trail)  *( `/api/django/audit/` )*
- **AuditLog** Ã¢â‚¬â€ company-scoped (server-forced, nullable for failed login); `user` FK (null=system) + `actor_username` snapshot; `action` (create/update/delete/status/login/logout/login_failed/pdf/email/whatsapp/export/accept/refuse); `content_type` + `object_id` + `object_repr` (link-back snapshot); `detail`; `timestamp` (UTC, bucketed in Africa/Casablanca at read time).
- Capture: `apps/audit/signals.py` (post_save/post_delete + status-change via pre_save cache) on the main business models, gated by `apps/audit/middleware.py` (records only inside a request Ã¢â€ â€™ no seed/migration noise); login/logout in `authentication/views.py`, failed login via `user_login_failed`; key actions (PDF/export/WhatsApp) via explicit `recorder.record` calls. Best-effort Ã¢â‚¬â€ never blocks the request.
- Endpoints (gated on `journal_activite_voir`, Directeur-only by default): `stats/` (hourly buckets for a day, per-day for week/month, Casablanca, filterable), `entries/` (paginated filterable list, newest first), `meta/` (filter-bar data).

### records Ã¢â‚¬â€ generic activities + attachments  *( `/api/django/records/` )*
ContentType-based, attachable to Lead/Client/Installation/Ticket.
- **ActivityType** Ã¢â‚¬â€ configurable types (Appel/Email/RelanceÃ¢â‚¬Â¦), `delai_defaut_jours`.
- **Activity** Ã¢â‚¬â€ generic FK target; `activity_type` FK; `due_date`; `assigned_to` FK; `done` bool + `done_at/by`; `auto_relance` bool (auto-synced from `Lead.relance_date`).
- **Attachment** Ã¢â‚¬â€ generic FK target; `file_key` (MinIO); `phase` (avant/pendant/aprÃƒÂ¨s for field photos).
- Endpoints: `activity-types/`, `activities/` (+ `mine/`, `{id}/done/`), `attachments/` (+ `{id}/download`, `attachments-count/`).
- **ARC30** switches `ALLOWED_TARGETS` from a literal set to a lazy union Ã¢â‚¬â€ `core.platform`'s `record_targets` surface, computed on first use, never at import Ã¢â‚¬â€ with the 19 historical targets migrated into 8 new `platform.py` manifests (outillage/rh/qhse/kb/ged/flotte/gestion_projet/ao) plus crm/contrats/sav/stock/ventes/installations from ARC29; a drop-in replacement (in/iteration/len identical, non-regression tested). **ARC8/26/SCA4/37** guard logic (no new bespoke `*Activity` chatter, no new `FileField` outside `records.Attachment`/`ged.Document`, no hand-rolled `company` FK model, no hand-rolled "document mÃƒÂ©tier" outside the SCA30 kit) lives in `apps/records/platform_guards.py` Ã¢â‚¬â€ pure text/regex scanners against frozen baselines in `apps/records/platform_baselines/`, consumed by both `scripts/check_platform.py` (CI) and the Django test runner.

### customfields Ã¢â‚¬â€ admin-defined custom fields  *( `/api/django/custom-fields/` )*
- **CustomFieldDef** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `module` (LEAD/CLIENT/PRODUIT), `code` (slug), `type` (TEXT/NUMBER/DATE/CHOICE/BOOLEAN), `options` JSON, `obligatoire/visible_liste/actif`. Values live in each target model's `custom_data` JSON (no schema migration).
- **ARC14** adds a data-driven registry (`app_label`/`model` pairs, not a hardcoded module enum) with 2 pilots Ã¢â‚¬â€ `contrats.Contrat`, `flotte.Vehicule` (each own a `custom_data` JSON field, migrations contrats/0038 + flotte/0057). **ARC31** switches the registry's central loader to `register_from_platform_manifests()` (`CustomfieldsConfig.ready()`), reading the `customfield_models` surface off `core.platform` manifests instead of explicit `register()` calls in each app's `ready()` Ã¢â‚¬â€ the 8 native + 2 pilot models are unchanged, non-regression tested.
- **CustomObjectDef** + **CustomRecord** (XPLT16, previously undocumented here) Ã¢â‚¬â€ no-code custom business objects: an admin defines an object (`code`/`libelle`/`icone`) whose fields are ordinary `CustomFieldDef` rows under `module='custom:<code>'` (full reuse, no second field-definition engine); each row of data lives in `CustomRecord.data`. NTEXT2/NTEXT3 auto-generate the list/form views from the field schema. Endpoints `objects/`, `custom-objects/<code>/records/(<id>/)`, `custom-objects/<code>/vue-liste/`, `custom-objects/<code>/vue-formulaire/`. **PACT140** gives it its first screens: admin `/parametres/objets-personnalises` (define objects/fields, admin-only) + one generic `/objets/:code` records screen serving every defined object.
- Endpoints: `definitions/` ViewSet.
- **NTEXT24** adds `blueprint.py` Ã¢â‚¬â€ export/import of the platform CONFIG (custom objects/fields), idempotent by natural key (re-import never duplicates). **NTEXT34** adds `objets-catalogue/` (list installable custom-object templates) + `objets-catalogue/installer/<code>/` (install one into the tenant). **NTEXT38** adds `verrouille` bool (migration 0008) on `CustomFieldDef`/`CustomObjectDef` (admin lock against accidental edit/delete) + `verrouiller`/`deverrouiller` actions on `definitions/<pk>/` and `objects/<pk>/`; `audit_plateforme.py` reports it.

### tiers Ã¢â‚¬â€ cross-app party directory (ARC17, foundation app)  *( `/api/django/tiers/` )*
The Odoo `res.partner` equivalent: one identity record instead of the same name/coordinates/legal-IDs being re-entered in 5+ domain models. A FOUNDATION app (import-linter `tiers-is-a-base-layer` Ã¢â‚¬â€ domain apps depend on it, never the reverse); this run only extends the pre-existing `Tiers` model + `TiersViewSet` (`ARC17`) with bridges and read-side reconciliation.
- **Tiers** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `company` FK; identity fields (`nom`/`prenom`/`raison_sociale`, `telephone`/`whatsapp`/`email`/`adresse`/`ville`/GPS, Moroccan IDs `ice`/`rc`/`identifiant_fiscal`/`cin`/`rib`); role flags `is_client`/`is_fournisseur`/`is_partenaire`/`is_soustraitant`; `type_tiers`.
- **ARC18** adds additive nullable `tiers` FKs (string-FK `'tiers.Tiers'`) mirroring identity ONE-WAY from `crm.Client` and `stock.Fournisseur` (migrations crm/0050, stock/0075) into a `Tiers` row, via `apps/{crm,stock}/tiers_bridge.py` + `attacher_ou_creer_tiers` (dedup by company-scoped email/ICE, else create Ã¢â‚¬â€ never duplicates; also persists the role flags on the create path, a real bug fix landed mid-run). **ARC19** extends the same bridge to `compta.Partenaire` (migration compta/0108) and `rh.DossierEmploye` (migration rh/0082). **ARC56** adds the bridge to `crm.Lead` (migration crm/0051) Ã¢â‚¬â€ pre-conversion identity, so a lead already has a `Tiers` before it becomes a client. In every case the historical model stays the IDENTITY MASTER; `tiers` is a mirror only.
- **ARC20** adds read-only cross-referencing: `selectors.find_by_ice`/`find_by_email` (company-scoped lookups) and `selectors.find_duplicates` Ã¢â‚¬â€ a same-ICE/same-email cluster report (e.g. one party registered as both a Fournisseur and a Partenaire) surfaced at `GET tiers/doublons/` (admin-only, no fusion/write).
- **ARC21** is a DECISION, flag-gated OFF: `TIERS_SOURCE_ECRITURE` would make `Tiers` the write-source of identity instead of a mirror Ã¢â‚¬â€ documented in `docs/decisions/ARC21-tiers-source-ecriture.md`, not flipped in this run.
- `management/backfill_tiers` Ã¢â‚¬â€ idempotent one-off command materializing `Tiers` bridges for existing rows across the four bridged models.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/tiers/`): `tiers/` ViewSet (CRUD, company-scoped, no dedicated fine-grained permission Ã¢â‚¬â€ authenticated + scoped suffices) + `tiers/doublons/` (ARC20 report, admin-only).

### documents Ã¢â‚¬â€ field-execution PDFs  *(no models, `/api/django/documents/`)*
- `GET chantiers/{pk}/pv-reception|bon-livraison|dossier-remise|attestation/` Ã¢â‚¬â€ generates post-delivery PDFs for an installation.

### publicapi Ã¢â‚¬â€ clÃƒÂ©s d'API publique + jobs bulk  *( `/api/public/` )*
- **BulkJob** (`TenantModel`) Ã¢â‚¬â€ job asynchrone export/import dÃƒÂ©clenchÃƒÂ© par clÃƒÂ© API, traitÃƒÂ© hors requÃƒÂªte (Celery), suivi par `GET jobs/<id>/`, reprenable (`cursor` mÃƒÂ©morise le dernier offset traitÃƒÂ©, `POST jobs/<id>/relancer/` repart de lÃƒÂ  sans doublon ni saut). `api_key` en `SET_NULL` : rÃƒÂ©voquer une clÃƒÂ© n'efface pas l'historique.
- `POST exports/` + `POST imports/` (create/upsert, journal d'erreurs par ligne), `GET jobs/` + `GET jobs/<id>/` (`Retry-After` tant que le job tourne), `GET exports/<entite>.csv?token=` (tirage CSV synchrone pour Google Sheets `IMPORTDATA()`), `GET /ventes/export-comptable/?layout=quickbooks` (IIF).
- **Gardes d'ÃƒÂ©crasement des imports (vague 3).** Les importateurs de prix fournisseur, limites de crÃƒÂ©dit, entitÃƒÂ©s et compteurs d'usage sont passÃƒÂ©s en REMPLISSAGE-SEUL par dÃƒÂ©faut, avec aperÃƒÂ§u champ par champ (valeur actuelle Ã¢â€ â€™ valeur du fichier) et journalisation de la valeur PRÃƒâ€°CÃƒâ€°DENTE. Un BUG DE PERTE RÃƒâ€°ELLE a ÃƒÂ©tÃƒÂ© corrigÃƒÂ© au passage : une cellule PRÃƒâ€°SENTE mais VIDE ÃƒÂ©crasait un `prix_achat` saisi ÃƒÂ  la main.

- **NTEXT6** adds `list_sources.py` Ã¢â‚¬â€ a CLOSED registry of list sources for a FOR_EACH loop step (never an arbitrary queryset). **NTEXT7** adds `AutomationScheduledStep` (a frozen context + resume rank + statut, `en_attente/traitee/echouee`) for a WAIT step, resumed by the `automation.process_due_automation_steps` beat task (every 5 min, `beat_tasks.py`). **NTEXT31** adds `AutomationRun.SIMULATION` statut + `rules/{id}/simuler/` Ã¢â‚¬â€ a dry-run (no side effect) evaluation of a rule.

- **FG120 Ã¢â‚¬â€ Cession/rebut**: **CessionImmobilisation** (`type_cession` vente/rebut, `prix_cession`, computed `valeur_nette_comptable` = cost Ã¢Ë†â€™ cumulated FG119 amortization, signed `resultat_cession` plus/moins-value, `posted` + FK `ecriture`). `services.poster_cession` posts the balanced disposal ÃƒÂ©criture (reprise amortissements + sortie class-2 + rÃƒÂ©sultat 6513/7513 + 3481 on sale) Ã¢â‚¬â€ **respects the period lock** and marks the asset inactive. - **ODX15 (2026-07 Ã¢â‚¬â€ moved OUT to `apps.frais`).** FG135/FG136/ZACC6/XACC27/XACC28 Ã¢â‚¬â€ `NoteFrais`, `RapportNoteFrais`, `PlafondNoteFrais`, `BaremeIndemnite`, `IndemniteChantier` Ã¢â‚¬â€ no longer live here: a state-only split (`SeparateDatabaseAndState`, `db_table` frozen on the historical `compta_*` names, no data moved) relocated the model CODE to `apps/frais` with a re-export shim left in `apps/compta/models.py` for existing importers.
