### frais Ã¢â‚¬â€ expense notes & site allowances (ODX15, new app)  *( `/api/django/frais/` )*
State-only split of FG135/FG136/ZACC6/XACC27/XACC28 OUT of `apps.compta` (migration `frais/0001_odx15_frais_split.py`, `SeparateDatabaseAndState`, `database_operations=[]`): every model **freezes its `db_table` on the historical `compta_*` name** Ã¢â‚¬â€ no table renamed, no data moved, revert is a plain `git revert`. `apps.frais` never imports `apps.compta.models`; it reaches accounts/ÃƒÂ©critures/trÃƒÂ©sorerie only via **string-FK** (`'compta.CompteComptable'`, `'compta.EcritureComptable'`, `'compta.CompteTresorerie'`) and calls `apps.compta.services` for every posting + the FG115 period lock. A re-export shim in `apps/compta/models.py` (`from apps.frais.models import ...`) keeps existing importers working. **Deliberately distinct from `rh.NoteDeFrais`** (an employee's self-service portal declaration, no GL posting) Ã¢â‚¬â€ the two surfaces are never merged (see `docs/module-map.md`).
- **NoteFrais** (FG135) Ã¢â‚¬â€ an employee's out-of-pocket expense claim: `company`+`employe` FK, `categorie`, `montant` TTC, `motif`, `justificatif` photo (MinIO FileField), reference `NDF-YYYYMM-NNNN` (race-safe). Cycle `brouillonÃ¢â€ â€™soumiseÃ¢â€ â€™validee`(posts debit charge 6143 / credit personnel-crÃƒÂ©diteur 4432, **respecting the compta period lock**)`Ã¢â€ â€™remboursee`(debit 4432 / credit treasury GL) or `rejetee`. **XACC27** adds `hors_politique` (flagged when it beats a `PlafondNoteFrais.montant_max` for its `categorie` Ã¢â‚¬â€ warning only, never blocking). **XACC28** adds billable-expense refacturation fields (`refacturable`, `taux_marge`, opaque `client_refacturation_id`/`facture_refacturation_id` Ã¢â‚¬â€ string-refs, no ventes import). **ZACC6** adds an optional `rapport` FKÃ¢â€ â€™`RapportNoteFrais` Ã¢â‚¬â€ once attached, the note is posted/reimbursed IN BLOCK by its report, never individually.
- **RapportNoteFrais** (ZACC6, Odoo Ã‚Â« Expense Report Ã‚Â» equivalent) Ã¢â‚¬â€ groups N `NoteFrais` of one employee into one submission: validation posts ONE aggregated ÃƒÂ©criture (ÃŽÂ£ charges by compte / credit 4432), reimbursement settles in ONE payment. A note with `rapport=None` keeps its individual FG135 cycle untouched.
- **PlafondNoteFrais** (XACC27) Ã¢â‚¬â€ per-company, per-`NoteFrais.categorie` spend ceiling referential (`montant_max`, optional `seuil_justificatif_obligatoire`); a category with no configured row is never flagged. **NTP2P11** (migration frais/0003) adds `jours_max_apres_depense` + `escalade_direction_au_dela_de` (both optional, unset = no-op): at `soumettre_note_frais`, `services.note_frais_warning_delai`/`note_frais_escalade_direction` stamp `NoteFrais.warning_delai` (non-blocking text) / `escalade_direction` (bool) server-side and journal them to `records.Activity` (best-effort, never blocks the submission). When `stock.AchatsParametres.sod_stricte` is on, the note's own creator cannot validate its DIRECTION escalation (`_verifier_separation_taches_note_frais`, reads `stock.selectors` only).
- **BaremeIndemnite** + **IndemniteChantier** (FG136) Ã¢â‚¬â€ kilomÃƒÂ©trique/per-diem site-trip allowances: `BaremeIndemnite` (per-company `taux_km` MAD/km + `per_diem` MAD/jour, one `defaut` active barÃƒÂ¨me); `IndemniteChantier` computes `distance_km` via a LOCAL haversine copy (keeps `frais` decoupled from installations/sav) between GPS dÃƒÂ©part/chantier Ãƒâ€” `taux_km` Ãƒâ€” (`aller_retour` ? 2 : 1) + `per_diem` Ãƒâ€” `nombre_jours`, all FROZEN at calc. Same `brouillonÃ¢â€ â€™soumiseÃ¢â€ â€™validee`(posts)`Ã¢â€ â€™remboursee`/`rejetee` cycle as NoteFrais, respecting the period lock.
- Endpoints (dual-served, byte-identical, at BOTH `/api/django/frais/Ã¢â‚¬Â¦` and the legacy `/api/django/compta/Ã¢â‚¬Â¦`): `notes-frais/` (+ `soumettre|valider|rejeter|rembourser`), `rapports-notes-frais/`, `plafonds-notes-frais/`, `baremes-indemnite/` (auto-demotes prior default), `indemnites-chantier/` (+ lifecycle actions). **NTP2P26/27** add `notes-frais/releve/` Ã¢â‚¬â€ a per-period statement of expense notes (PDF via `pdf_notes_frais.py` or `?export=xlsx`).

### marketing Ã¢â‚¬â€ campaigns, sequences, events, loyalty, surveys (ODX9/10)  *( `/api/django/marketing/` )*
Previously undocumented here (36 models, predates this run). State-only split OUT of `apps.compta` (**ODX9**, same `db_table='compta_<model>'`/`SeparateDatabaseAndState`/shim pattern as `frais`/`portail`); **ODX10** gave it its own `/api/django/marketing/Ã¢â‚¬Â¦` prefix while the historical `/api/django/compta/Ã¢â‚¬Â¦` routes keep serving the SAME ViewSets byte-identically (basenames prefixed `mkt-Ã¢â‚¬Â¦` to avoid router-name collisions). `apps.marketing.selectors` is the ONLY sanctioned read surface for other apps (never a `marketing.models` import) Ã¢â‚¬â€ **WIR96** (this run) is the first real cross-app consumer: `ouverture_partage_pour_token`/`relances_devis_abandonne`, read by `ventes.views.devis.suivi_partage` (see Ã‚Â§4 ventes), and the public proposal open-hook now writes `OuverturePartage` (was routed but previously never populated).
- **Models, by sub-domain** Ã¢â‚¬â€ mailing (`Campagne`, `EnvoiCampagne` one row per recipient, `SuppressionMarketing` opt-out list, `ListeDiffusion`/`AbonnementListe`, `SegmentMarketing`, `RebondSoft`, `ApprobationEnvoiCampagne`, `DomaineEnvoi`); drip sequences (`SequenceRelance` triggered on a lead entering a funnel stage, `EtapeSequence`, `InscriptionSequence`, `ExecutionEtapeSequence`); link tracking (`LienTrackee`, `ClicLien`, `StatutEngagementContact`, **NTMKT18** `ScoreMaturite`/`VariationScoreMaturite` Ã¢â‚¬â€ multi-signal lead maturity score + its change history); quote recovery/capture (`RelanceDevisAbandonne`, `OuverturePartage`, `FormulaireIntake` + landing capture, `MessageWhatsAppEntrant`, `AppelTelephonique`); satisfaction (`EnqueteNPS`, `AvisClient`, `Enquete`/`ReponseEnquete` a configurable survey engine); loyalty (`CompteFidelite`, `MouvementFidelite`, `RegleUpsell`); events (`TypeEvenement`, `EvenementMarketing`, `BilletEvenement`, `QuestionEvenement`, `CommunicationEvenement`, `InscriptionEvenement`); plus `SupportOffline` (QR print collateral) and `PostSocial` (social content calendar).
- **Journey graph (NTMKT12/14/15)** Ã¢â‚¬â€ additive graph-routing layer over the existing linear `SequenceRelance`/`EtapeSequence` engine (a sequence with zero graph node still ticks exactly as before). `NoeudJourney` (dÃƒÂ©clencheur/attente(J+n)/**attente_jusqu_a** date-ou-crÃƒÂ©neau-ouvrÃƒÂ© (NTMKT14)/action/branche/sortie, free `config` JSON, `position_x/y` for the canvas) + `ArcJourney` (source/cible nÃ…â€œuds, `condition` toujours/a_ouvert/a_clique/score_seuil/tag_present, `ordre` Ã¢â‚¬â€ supersedes `EtapeSequence`'s single condition with N evaluated arcs). `ModeleJourney` (NTMKT15, a ready-to-instantiate pre-built graph template, complementing the 5 linear XMKT20 recipes) Ã¢â‚¬â€ seeded by `manage.py seed_modeles_journey`. Endpoints `noeuds-journey/`, `arcs-journey/`, `modeles-journey/`; Celery beat `marketing.executer_journeys` (08:15, company-by-company).
- **Landing pages & content (NTMKT16/23/31)** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `VersionFormulaireIntake` (NTMKT16, an editorial version of a `FormulaireIntake` landing: titre/pitch/image, server-incremented `version`, `publie` flag Ã¢â‚¬â€ the public page always renders the latest published version). `BlocContenu` (NTMKT23, a reusable content fragment texte/image/cta; inserting COPIES it into the campaign body Ã¢â‚¬â€ editing the source never retro-edits a sent campaign). `ParametresMarketing` (NTMKT31, one singleton per company Ã¢â‚¬â€ expÃƒÂ©diteur nom/email/domaine, additive silence-window hours + daily send cap, all-NULL = unchanged behaviour). Endpoints `versions-formulaire-intake/`, `blocs-contenu/`, `parametres/` (NTMKT31).
- **Ops/reporting additions** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `heatmap-engagement/` (NTMKT24, read-only dayÃƒâ€”hour engagement heatmap), `campagnes/importer-couts/` (NTMKT26, CSV import of external Meta/Google Ads spend), `campagnes/{id}/rapport-pdf/` (NTMKT27, internal campaign-bilan PDF), `registre-consentement/export-pdf/` (NTMKT28, CNDP consent-register export), public `preferences/<token>/` (NTMKT22, self-service per-channel/per-list preference centre Ã¢â‚¬â€ never a blanket unsubscribe). `services.purger_tokens_expires` (NTMKT33, daily 03:00 beat, purges expired public unsubscribe/preference tokens) and `services.rappeler_approbations_envoi_en_attente` (NTMKT35, 4-hourly beat, one reminder per pending campaign-send approval past 24h Ã¢â‚¬â€ anti-duplicate).
- Endpoints (dual-served at both `/api/django/marketing/Ã¢â‚¬Â¦` and the legacy `/api/django/compta/Ã¢â‚¬Â¦`): `campagnes/`, `envois-campagne/`, `approbations-envoi-campagne/`, `listes-diffusion/`, `abonnements-liste/`, `segments-marketing/`, `sequences-relance/`, `etapes-sequence/`, `inscriptions-sequence/`, `relances-devis-abandonnes/`, `ouvertures-partage/`, `formulaires-intake/`, `messages-whatsapp/`, `appels/`, `enquetes-nps/`, `avis-clients/`, `comptes-fidelite/`, `mouvements-fidelite/`, `regles-upsell/`, `enquetes/`, `evenements-marketing/`, `inscriptions-evenement/`, `types-evenement/`, `billets-evenement/`, `questions-evenement/`, `communications-evenement/`, `supports-offline/`, `domaines-envoi/`; **NTMKT18-20/39-41** add `scores-maturite/<lead_id>/` (current maturity score + history), `attribution/comparaison/` (the 4 attribution models side-by-side for a signed devis), `campagnes/export/` (XLSX), `campagnes/<pk>/envois/export/` (CSV), `segments-marketing/<pk>/export/` (XLSX of resolved members), `evenements-marketing/<pk>/importer-inscrits/` (bulk CSV/XLSX import); public token/slug views `webhooks/brevo/`, `webhooks/sms-stop/`, `desinscription/<token>/`, `preferences/<token>/`, `double-optin/<token>/`, `r/<token>/` (tracked-link redirect), `enquetes-publiques/<token>/(+soumettre)`, `evenements-marketing/<id>/inscription-publique/`, `intake/<slug>/(+soumettre)`. **PACT106/107/108** give `avis-clients/` (FG239, + Google Reviews routing), `enquetes-nps/` (FG238, distinct from the Pulse eNPS survey) and `messages-whatsapp/` (FG207, read-only inbound log) their first screens (`/marketing/avis-clients`, `/marketing/enquetes-nps`, `/marketing/messages-whatsapp`). Frontend: `JourneyBuilder`/`LandingVersions`/`HeatmapEnvoi`/`UtmBuilder` (NTMKT25, campaign URL/UTM generator, no model) screens + guided `CampagneWizard`/`SequenceWizard`/`EnqueteWizard` (NTMKT29/30/32, opened from their list's Ã‚Â« (guidÃƒÂ©) Ã‚Â» button) at `/marketing/{campagnes,sequences,enquetes}/nouveau` + `/marketing/parametres` (`MarketingParametres.jsx`) + `/marketing/generateur-url`. **NTMKT44/45** add `CampagneViewSetAudite`/`EnqueteNPSViewSetNotifiant` + `evenement_inscription_publique_notifiante` Ã¢â‚¬â€ envelope subclasses/wrappers over the `apps.compta` originals (audit-log the real send / notify on an NPS detractor / notify on public event signup) registered ONLY on the marketing router; the legacy `/compta/Ã¢â‚¬Â¦` routes keep serving the unmodified originals.

### flotte Ã¢â‚¬â€ fleet: vehicles + rolling equipment (FLOTTE1, new app)  *( `/api/django/flotte/` )*
- **Vehicule** (`company` FK; immatriculation, marque, modÃƒÂ¨le, ÃƒÂ©nergie diesel/essence/ÃƒÂ©lectrique/hybride, kilomÃƒÂ©trage, valeur, statut actif/maintenance/rÃƒÂ©formÃƒÂ©) and **EnginRoulant** (`company` FK; type nacelle/groupe ÃƒÂ©lectrogÃƒÂ¨ne/chariot, compteur d'heures, marque, modÃƒÂ¨le, valeur, statut). Company-scoped ViewSets (company forced server-side, an injected body `company` is ignored) at `vehicules/`, `engins/` with ÃƒÂ©nergie/statut/type filters + search. Uses only the `authentication.Company` string FK Ã¢â‚¬â€ no domain-app imports. **FLOTTE3** adds `Vehicule.emplacement_stock_id` (PositiveInteger, NOT a cross-app FK) referencing a `stock.EmplacementStock`; validated same-company + labelled via a function-local `apps.stock.selectors.get_emplacement_scoped` call (degrades to `#id`; never imports stock models). **FLOTTE6** adds **ReferentielFlotte** (editable per-company lookup lists: `domaine` type_vehicule/type_engin/energie/categorie_permis, `code`/`libelle`/`ordre`/`actif`, unique company+domaine+code) Ã¢â‚¬â€ additive (hardcoded choices untouched) Ã¢â‚¬â€ plus an idempotent `seed_referentiels_flotte` command. ViewSet `referentiels/` (`?domaine`/`?actif`). **FLOTTE5** adds **ActifFlotte** Ã¢â‚¬â€ a unified asset reference linking entretien/sinistre/document to EITHER a `Vehicule` OR an `EnginRoulant` via one model (exactly-one-target + same-company enforced in `clean()`/`save()`); company-scoped ViewSet `actifs/` (`?type_actif`) + selectors for cross-app reads. **FLOTTE13** adds fuel/energy consumption: `selectors.consommation_vehicule` computes **L/100 km and kWh/100 km** plein-to-plein from `PleinCarburant` + odometer (per-segment, divide-by-zero guarded, L vs kWh kept separate), surfaced read-only at `pleins/consommation/?vehicule=<id>` (`IsAnyRole`, 400 missing/non-int param, 404 cross-company). No migration. **FLOTTE14** adds **CarteCarburant** (fuel card: `numero`, `plafond`, optional `vehicule`/`conducteur` FK) + `selectors.anomalies_pleins` flagging four families per plein Ã¢â‚¬â€ `km_recul` (odometer decreasing), `km_saut` (>5000 km jump), `conso_aberrante` (>2Ãƒâ€” the vehicle's median baseline, reusing FLOTTE13's conso), `plafond_depasse` Ã¢â‚¬â€ at `cartes/anomalies/?vehicule=` (read action, `IsAnyRole`). ViewSet `cartes/`. **FLOTTE15** adds **PlanEntretien** (preventive maintenance via an `ActifFlotte` FK Ã¢â‚¬â€ reaches both Vehicule km and EnginRoulant heures): triggers by `intervalle_km`/`intervalle_jours`/`intervalle_heures` + last-done refs + alert margins; `selectors.plans_entretien_status` computes next-due vs current km/date/hours (due/upcoming/ok). ViewSet `plans-entretien/` (+ `echeances/` read action). **FLOTTE16** adds **EcheanceEntretien** (a generated due-maintenance record per plan: `due_le`/`due_km`/`due_heures`, `statut` a_faire/planifie/fait) + `services.generer_echeances_entretien` (idempotent Ã¢â‚¬â€ no duplicate OPEN ÃƒÂ©chÃƒÂ©ance per plan; best-effort alert via `notifications.notify`) + a `manage.py generer_echeances_entretien` command. ViewSet `echeances-entretien/` (read any role, `generer` write action; POST-create disabled). Migration flotte/0014. **FLOTTE17** adds **Garage** (atelier: nom/adresse/tÃƒÂ©lÃƒÂ©phone) + **OrdreReparation** (repair order on an `ActifFlotte`: garage, description, `cout_main_oeuvre`+`cout_pieces`Ã¢â€ â€™derived `cout_total`, `statut` ouvert/en_cours/cloture, optional `EcheanceEntretien` link); ViewSets `garages/`, `ordres-reparation/` (+ `couts/` summary, `cloturer/` which solde the linked ÃƒÂ©chÃƒÂ©ance). Migration flotte/0015. **FLOTTE18** adds **Pneumatique** (tire: position/dimension/montage/dÃƒÂ©pose/statut/coÃƒÂ»t) + **PieceFlotte** (part: dÃƒÂ©signation/rÃƒÂ©f/quantitÃƒÂ©/coÃƒÂ»t, optional OrdreReparation link); ViewSets `pneumatiques/`, `pieces/` + a per-vehicle `synthese/` summary. Migration flotte/0016. **FLOTTE19** adds **EcheanceReglementaire** Ã¢â‚¬â€ generic regulatory deadlines (visite technique/assurance/vignette/carte grise/taxe ÃƒÂ  l'essieu) on an `ActifFlotte`, with a today-injectable status (a_jour/a_renouveler/expire) and `expirantes/?within=N`; distinct from the FLOTTE16 maintenance ÃƒÂ©chÃƒÂ©ances. ViewSet `echeances-reglementaires/`. Migration flotte/0017. **FLOTTE20** adds **BaremeVignette** Ã¢â‚¬â€ an editable per-company TSAV grid (ÃƒÂ©nergie Ãƒâ€” CV bracket Ã¢â€ â€™ montant, per year) + `Vehicule.puissance_fiscale` + `selectors.calcul_tsav` (electric exempt, no bracket Ã¢â€ â€™ None) + an idempotent standard-grid seed. ViewSet `baremes-vignette/` + `vehicules/{id}/tsav/`. Migration flotte/0018. **ARC14** adds `Vehicule.custom_data` (JSON), the second `customfields` registry pilot alongside `contrats.Contrat`. **ARC28** adds `apps/flotte/platform.py`.

### ged Ã¢â‚¬â€ document management / DMS (GED1, new app)  *( `/api/django/ged/` )*
- Governed DMS reusing `records.storage` (MinIO `file_key`). **Cabinet** + **Folder** (self-FK tree with a materialized `path` recomputed in `save()`/`services.move_folder`, sub-tree prefix rewrite + cycle refusal), **Document** (lives in a Folder) + **DocumentVersion** (`file_key`, SHA-256 `checksum` for dedupe via `services.find_duplicate`, server-set incremental `version`, `uploaded_by`). All `company`-scoped (company/created_by/uploaded_by forced server-side). Endpoints: `cabinets/`, `folders/` (+ `descendants`), `documents/`, `document-versions/`. **GED4** Ã¢â‚¬â€ move (dÃƒÂ©placement) over HTTP: `POST folders/{id}/deplacer/` (body `{parent}`, reparent/to-root, anti-cycle + cross-cabinet refusal via `services.move_folder`) and `POST documents/{id}/deplacer/` (body `{folder}`, via `services.move_document`); destination always resolved inside the caller's company (404 cross-tenant). **GED14** Ã¢â‚¬â€ inline same-origin preview: `GET document-versions/{id}/apercu/` streams the document bytes through Django (PDF/image/text Ã¢â€ â€™ `Content-Disposition: inline`, else attachment; `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`), gated as a READ action (`IsAnyRole`, like list/retrieve) so read-only roles can preview. **GED17** adds a document lifecycle: `Document.statut` (**brouillonÃ¢â€ â€™revueÃ¢â€ â€™approuvÃƒÂ©Ã¢â€ â€™archivÃƒÂ©Ã¢â€ â€™obsolÃƒÂ¨te**, default brouillon) with a guarded `LIFECYCLE_TRANSITIONS` state machine in `services.change_lifecycle_status` (illegal/unknown/same-status Ã¢â€ â€™ `ValueError`, cross-company Ã¢â€ â€™ `PermissionError`, `select_for_update`), exposed read-only on the serializer and advanced only via `POST documents/{id}/cycle-vie/` (responsable/admin) + a `?statut=` filter. Local GED statuses Ã¢â‚¬â€ **separate from the STAGES.py funnel**. **GED18** adds **DemandeApprobation** (review/approval workflow): `demandeur`/`approbateur`, `statut` en_attente/approuve/rejete; `services.request_review` opens a demande + moves brouillonÃ¢â€ â€™revue, `approve_demande`/`reject_demande` **reuse the GED17 `change_lifecycle_status`** (no duplicate state machine) to advance revueÃ¢â€ â€™approuvÃƒÂ© / back to brouillon; guards duplicate-pending / already-decided / cross-company. Actions `documents/{id}/demander-revue|demandes` + ViewSet `demandes-approbation/` (`approuver`/`rejeter`). **GED19** adds **AclGed** Ã¢â‚¬â€ per-folder/document ACL: exactly-one target (folder XOR document, two CheckConstraints), principal = `utilisateur` and/or `role`, `niveau` lecture/ecriture/gestion, `herite` flag. `selectors.acl_effective` walks up the materialized `Folder.path` (document override > nearer folder > ancestor only when `herite`; most-permissive at equal scope; admin always gestion), softly wired into `documents_visible_to_user` Ã¢â‚¬â€ **backward-compatible: no ACL rows Ã¢â€ â€™ behaviour unchanged**. Migration ged/0013. **GED20** adds **PartageGed** Ã¢â‚¬â€ a tokenized public document share (`token` via `secrets`, `expires_at`, hashed `password_hash`, `quota_max` + `telechargements` counter, `actif` kill-switch); a PUBLIC `AllowAny` endpoint `GET /api/django/ged/public/<token>/` resolves **by token only** (never trusts request company/identity), streams the document, atomic quota-conditional increment, and returns 404 (unknown/revoked) / 410 (expired/quota) / 403 (missing/wrong password). Management ViewSet `partages/` (+ `revoquer/`) is fully multi-tenant. Migration ged/0014. **GED21** adds watermarking & diffusion control: flags `Document.watermark_diffusion` + `PartageGed.watermark` and `services.apply_watermark` (image via Pillow Ã¢â‚¬â€ already a dep; PDF via PyMuPDF imported lazily, graceful degrade if absent Ã¢â€ â€™ original bytes), wired into the GED14 `apercu` + GED20 public download (no-watermark path byte-identical). Category DEP but NO hard new dependency. Migration ged/0015. **GED22** adds **PolitiqueRetention** Ã¢â‚¬â€ document retention policies (durÃƒÂ©e de conservation + action ÃƒÂ  l'ÃƒÂ©chÃƒÂ©ance, default the NON-destructive `signaler`) + `selectors.documents_echus(company, today)` (most-specific policy, today-injectable) + a `lister_documents_echus` command. Never auto-deletes passively. ViewSet `politiques-retention/` (+ `echus`). Migration ged/0016. **GED23** adds **ArchivageLegal** Ã¢â‚¬â€ legal probative-value write-once archiving (SHA-256 integrity hash, best-effort MinIO object-lock with graceful degrade Ã¢â‚¬â€ no hard dep). Once archived, app-layer immutability blocks edit/delete/new-version/move/lifecycle/check-out/check-in (all Ã¢â€ â€™ 403, never 500); the ArchivageLegal row is create-only. ViewSet `archivages-legaux/` (+ `documents/{id}/archiver-legalement`). Migration ged/0017.
- **WIR163** finally gives the GED19 `AclGed` model a ViewSet Ã¢â‚¬â€ `acls/` (was model-only/no REST surface before). **WIR164** exposes the pre-existing XGED16 `TamponSociete` (per-company custom document stamp, on top of the 3 hardcoded systÃƒÂ¨me stamps) via `tampons-societe/` (was model-only before too). **WIR73** schedules the GED7 `migrate_attachments_to_ged` importer (idempotent by company+file_key) as a weekly Celery beat (`ged.migrer_pieces_jointes`) Ã¢â‚¬â€ a `records.Attachment` created after the last MANUAL import previously never reached GED on its own. **WIR165** gives `core.events.document_produit` its first real emitter: `apps/ventes/utils/pdf.py generate_facture_pdf` sends it (`source='ventes_facture'`) right after storing the legacy invoice PDF; the receiver stays a no-op until an admin creates a `RoutageDocumentaire(source='ventes_facture', Ã¢â‚¬Â¦)` for their company. *(Note: this section's endpoint/model inventory pre-dates several XGED*-prefixed features Ã¢â‚¬â€ e.g. `coffres/`, `demandes-document/`, `regles-dossier/`, `regles-approbation/`, `validations-ocr/`, `annotations/`, e-signature champs/signataires, quotas Ã¢â‚¬â€ that already exist in `apps/ged/urls.py` but were never written up here; out of scope for this pass.)*

### rh Ã¢â‚¬â€ human resources: employee master (FG154, new app)  *( `/api/django/rh/` )*
- **DossierEmploye** (`company` FK; employee record). **FG155** adds the employment-contract layer: `type_contrat` (TextChoices CDI/CDD/ANAPEC/stage/intÃƒÂ©rim) + `contrat_date_debut`/`contrat_date_fin` (nullable dates; empty `date_fin` = open-ended/CDI). Company-scoped ViewSet (`employes/`) with `@action cdd-a-echeance/?within=N` (default 30 days) returning only same-company CDDs whose `contrat_date_fin` falls within the window. **FG156** adds the mandatory Moroccan payroll identity fields to `DossierEmploye`: `cnss`/`cimr`/`amo`, `situation_familiale` (cÃƒÂ©libataire/mariÃƒÂ©/divorcÃƒÂ©/veuf), `nombre_enfants` (IR deductions) Ã¢â‚¬â€ all nullable (CIN/RIB already existed). **FG166** adds **Pointage** (clock-in/out: `company`+`employe` FK, `type_pointage` arrivÃƒÂ©e/dÃƒÂ©part/complet, server-set `heure_arrivee`/`heure_depart`, optional GPS, computed `duree_minutes`) with actions `pointages/pointager-arrivee` + `pointages/{id}/pointager-depart` (server timestamp; Ã¢â€ â€™ COMPLET + duration once arrival is set). `IsResponsableOrAdmin`-gated. **FG172** adds the skills matrix: **Competence** (per-company catalogue, `domaine` pose_structure/raccordement_dc/raccordement_ac/mes_onduleur/pompage/soudure/autre, unique `(company, code)`) + **CompetenceEmploye** (one row per `(employe, competence)`, `niveau` 0Ã¢â‚¬â€œ4 Non-acquisÃ¢â€ â€™Expert, server-set `evalue_par`/`evalue_le`). ViewSets `competences/`, `competences-employe/` (+ `matrice/` grid action, `?domaine`/`?niveau_min` filters), admin-gated, company forced server-side; duplicate-code Ã¢â€ â€™ clean 400. **FG173** adds **Habilitation** Ã¢â‚¬â€ electrical authorizations (NF C 18-510: B0/H0/B1V/B2V/BRÃ¢â‚¬Â¦) per employee with `organisme`, `date_obtention`, `date_validite` (expiry) and a computed `valide` flag; ViewSet `habilitations/` + `expirantes/?expire_within=N` (expiring-soon + already-expired), unique `(employe, type_habilitation)`. Distinct from the FG172 competence matrix. **FG174** adds **Certification** Ã¢â‚¬â€ the non-electrical family (travail_hauteur/harnais/caces_nacelle/secourisme_sst/conduite/autre) per employee with `organisme`, `date_validite` + computed `valide`; ViewSet `certifications/` (+ `expirantes/?expire_within=N`), unique `(employe, type_certification)`. Modelled distinctly from the FG173 electrical Habilitation. **FG175** adds `selectors.echeances_rh` Ã¢â‚¬â€ a unified expiry-alert engine unioning expiring Habilitations + Certifications + DocumentEmploye within X days (normalized `{type, employe, libelle, date_validite, jours_restants}`, `today` a param) at `echeances/?within=N`, plus a `manage.py alertes_expiration_rh` command dispatching one notification per ÃƒÂ©chÃƒÂ©ance via the shared `notifications.notify` service (function-local import). No model. **FG176** adds `selectors.verifier_habilitation_requise(company, employe, type_requis)` Ã¢â€ â€™ `{autorise, manquantes, expirees, message}` (reuses FG173's validity rule; `INTERVENTION_HABILITATIONS` map) + endpoint `employes/{id}/verifier-habilitation/?type=&intervention=`. A SOFT guard (reports; enforcement at assignment stays in installations). No model. **FG177** adds **VisiteMedicale** (occupational-health visits per employee: `date_visite`, `prochaine_visite`, `aptitude` apte/restrictions/inapte, mÃƒÂ©decin/organisme, computed `a_jour`); ViewSet `visites-medicales/` (+ `expirantes/?expire_within=N`), and feeds FG175's `echeances_rh` with a `visite_medicale` family. Migration rh/0017. **FG178** adds **EpiCatalogue** (PPE catalogue: `type_epi` casque/harnais/gants_isolants/chaussures/lunettes/autre) + **DotationEpi** (nominative issue: `employe`, `epi`, `taille`, `date_dotation`, `date_renouvellement`, `quantite`); ViewSets `epi-catalogue/`, `dotations-epi/` (+ `a-renouveler/`, `employe/`), feeds FG175. Migration rh/0018. **FG179** adds EPI life/expiry tracking: `EpiCatalogue.duree_vie_mois`/`intervalle_controle_mois` Ã¢â€ â€™ derived `DotationEpi.date_peremption`/`date_prochain_controle` (end-of-month-clamped month math), computed `perime`/`a_controler` (`today`-injectable), endpoint `dotations-epi/a-remplacer-controler/?expire_within=N`; feeds FG175 with `epi_peremption`/`epi_controle` families. Migration rh/0019. **FG180** adds **EmargementEpi** (signed EPI-handover acknowledgement, loi 53-05 typed name + server-side IP/user-agent evidence) + `accuse_remise`/`date_accuse` on DotationEpi; `services.emarger_dotation` records it (company + acting user server-side, accusÃƒÂ© frozen at first signature); actions `dotations-epi/{id}/emarger|emargements`. No external e-sign dep. Migration rh/0020. **FG181** adds **AccidentTravail** Ã¢â‚¬â€ workplace-accident register (race-safe `AT-` reference, date/lieu/employÃƒÂ©/gravitÃƒÂ©/arrÃƒÂªt+jours/photo, CNSS declaration flags) + a CNSS CSV export (`?export=csv`). ViewSet `accidents-travail/`. Migration rh/0021. **FG182** adds **PresquAccident** Ã¢â‚¬â€ a lightweight near-miss register (race-safe `NM-` ref, lieu/gravitÃƒÂ© potentielle/mesure corrective, server-side declarant; no injured person/CNSS Ã¢â‚¬â€ distinct from FG181) + a stats-by-gravitÃƒÂ© selector. ViewSet `presqu-accidents/`. Migration rh/0022.
- **ARC19** adds a nullable `DossierEmploye.tiers` FK (migration rh/0082, `tiers_bridge.py`) mirroring identity one-way into `tiers.Tiers`. **ARC28/33/40** add `apps/rh/platform.py` Ã¢â‚¬â€ `agent_actions_module` (read-only employÃƒÂ©s + demandes de congÃƒÂ© actions) and `kpi_providers` (effectif actif + absences en cours, consumed by the ARC40 federated KPI endpoint).

### paie Ã¢â‚¬â€ Moroccan payroll engine (Groupe PAIE, new app)  *( `/api/django/paie/` )*
Full CNSS/CIMR/AMO/IR payroll run, distinct from `rh.DossierEmploye` (the employee master `paie` reads via `ProfilPaie`, string/FK-id, never a duplicate identity). `company`-scoped throughout, additive-only.
- **ParamÃƒÂ©trage versionnÃƒÂ©** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `ParametrePaie` (PAIE2: SMIG/SMAG, plafond CNSS, taux CNSS/AMO/taxe formation professionnelle, versioned per `date_effet` Ã¢â‚¬â€ history never edited) + `BaremeIR`/`TrancheIR` (PAIE4: versioned IR tax brackets) + `Rubrique` (PAIE6: configurable pay-slip line items) + `StructurePaie`/`StructurePaieRubrique` (named rubrique bundles applied to a profil).
- **Profil employÃƒÂ©** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `ProfilPaie` (PAIE8: salary/contract payroll profile), `RubriqueEmploye` (PAIE9: recurring per-employee rubriques), `RegimeMutuelle`/`AdhesionMutuelle` (private health-insurance plan + enrolment).
- **Cycle mensuel** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `PeriodePaie` (PAIE10: one monthly run, statut-gated) + `ElementVariable`/`TypeEntreePonctuelle` (PAIE11: one-off variable entries e.g. overtime/bonus) Ã¢â€ â€™ `BulletinPaie`/`LigneBulletin` (PAIE17: the frozen/immutable payslip snapshot once validated) Ã¢â€ â€™ `CumulAnnuel` (PAIE27: per-employee YTD brut/net-imposable/IR/CNSS/congÃƒÂ©s) + `ProvisionPaieMensuelle` (13th-month/severance accrual) + `VentilationAnalytiquePaie` (fixed analytic cost-center split).
- **Recouvrement & virement** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `AvanceSalarie` (PAIE28: salary advance/loan + monthly deduction), `SaisieArret` (PAIE29: statutory wage-garnishment, seizable-quota guarded), `OrdreVirement`/`LigneVirement` (PAIE30: bank payroll-transfer file).
- **DÃƒÂ©claratif** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `EcheanceDeclarative` (statutory filing deadlines) + `DepotBDS` (CNSS BDS deposit).
- Endpoints: `parametres/`, `baremes/`, `rubriques/`, `types-entree-ponctuelle/`, `profils/` (+ `synchroniser-salaire`, `attestation`, `stc`/`stc-pdf` solde-de-tout-compte, `simulation`, `registre-conges`, `historique-carriere`), `rubriques-employe/`, `structures/` (+ `appliquer`), `regimes-mutuelle/`/`adhesions-mutuelle/`, `periodes/` (+ `run-gratification`, `importer-elements-rh`, `reporter-elements`, `cloturer`, `livre-de-paie`, `cout-global`, `cout-employeur`, `journal-ventile`, `provisions`, `rapprochement-gl`), `elements-variables/`, `bulletins/` (+ `bulletin`, `declaration-cnss`, `declaration-cimr`, `fichier-cimr`, `fichier-damancom(-strict)`, `mouvements-cnss`, `etat-ir`, `etat-charges`), `cumuls-annuels/`, `avances/`, `saisies/`, `ordres-virement/`, `lignes-virement/`, `echeances-declaratives/` (+ `echeances`, `notifier-echeances-retard`), `mes-bulletins/` (employee self-service payslip vault). **PACT95** gives `types-entree-ponctuelle/` (`TypeEntreePonctuelle`, ZPAI9) its first screen, `/paie/types-entree-ponctuelle`. GL reconciliation + PDF slips reuse `core.pdf.render_pdf` (ARC11) Ã¢â‚¬â€ no bespoke PDF plumbing. **WIR89** adds `selectors.divergences_cnss_periode` + `services.controler_coherence_cnss` Ã¢â‚¬â€ the CNSS-number analogue of the pre-existing ARC25 RIB-divergence check (compares `ProfilPaie.numero_cnss` against `rh.DossierEmploye.cnss` via `apps.rh.selectors`, a signal only Ã¢â‚¬â€ never a fusion): run automatically inside `declaration_cnss`, best-effort, and notifies payroll managers (`notify_many`, event key `paie_cnss_divergence`) only when a genuine mismatch exists.

### gestion_projet Ã¢â‚¬â€ project management (PROJ1, new app)  *( `/api/django/gestion-projet/` )*
- **Projet** + **ProjetChantier** (`company`-scoped). **PROJ2** adds **ProjetLien** (`company` + `projet` FK; `type_cible` devis/facture/ticket/achat, `cible_id` target PK, cached `libelle`) linking a project to other apps' documents by **string-FK only** (no real cross-app FK). Endpoints: `projet-liens/` (CRUD, `?projet=`/`?type_cible=` filters) + `projets/{id}/liens/` (enriched). `selectors.liens_enrichis` enriches devis links via a function-local `apps.ventes.selectors.devis_card` call and degrades to the stored label otherwise (cross-app boundary respected; import-linter clean). **PROJ3** adds a project-lifecycle state machine on `Projet` (`statut` brouillonÃ¢â€ â€™planifieÃ¢â€ â€™en_coursÃ¢â€¡â€žen_pauseÃ¢â€ â€™termine, annule from any non-terminal Ã¢â‚¬â€ **independent of `STAGES.py`**, rule #2) via actions `planifier`/`demarrer`/`mettre-en-pause`/`reprendre`/`terminer`/`annuler` (illegal Ã¢â€ â€™ 400; statut read-only outside actions) + a **ProjetActivity** transition log (`historique/`). **PROJ4** adds **PhaseProjet** (project WBS: `type_phase` etude/appro/pose/mes/reception Ã¢â‚¬â€ own enum, not STAGES; prÃƒÂ©vu/rÃƒÂ©el dates, `statut`, `avancement_pct` 0-100; unique projet+type_phase) + `services.instancier_phases_standard` (5 ordered phases, idempotent). ViewSet `phases/` + action `projets/{id}/instancier-phases`. **PROJ14** adds delay detection: `selectors.retards_projet` + `GET projets/{id}/retards/` classifying unfinished tasks and unreached milestones as `en_retard` (past due) or `a_risque` (due within `seuil_jours`, default 7) with `retard_jours` (no migration). **PROJ18** adds `selectors.plan_de_charge` Ã¢â‚¬â€ per-resource capacity (working days Ã¢Ë†â€™ IndisponibilitÃƒÂ© Ãƒâ€” hours/day) vs allocated (AffectationRessource charge, direct + team-split, pro-rated to the window) over a period with a `surcharge` flag + `utilisation_pct` (None when capacity 0); endpoint `ressources/plan-de-charge/?debut=&fin=`. No model. **PROJ19** adds `selectors.conflits_affectation` Ã¢â‚¬â€ double-booking: same `RessourceProfil` allocated to Ã¢â€°Â¥2 `AffectationRessource` whose windows overlap (direct + via ÃƒÂ©quipe; bonus: allocation during an IndisponibilitÃƒÂ©), at `ressources/conflits-affectation/?debut=&fin=`. No model. **PROJ20** adds `selectors.nivellement_charge` (resource levelling) Ã¢â‚¬â€ proposes moving direct affectations off over-allocated RessourceProfil to under-loaded ones without creating a PROJ19 conflict (read-only), at `ressources/nivellement-charge/`. No model. **PROJ21** adds **BudgetProjet** + **LigneBudgetProjet** (categorie materiel/main_oeuvre/sous_traitance/divers, montant_prevu, optional quantite/pu) + `selectors.budget_total` (total + par_categorie, all 4 categories present); ViewSets `budgets/`, `lignes-budget/` + a `/total/` action. Migration gestion_projet/0013. **PROJ22** adds `selectors.couts_engages_vs_reels` Ã¢â‚¬â€ committed/actual project cost vs the PROJ21 budget per category (labour from internal AffectationRessource quantized to 2dp; matÃƒÂ©riel/sous-traitance via ProjetLien with graceful degrade Ã¢â‚¬â€ no cross-app amount selector exists yet), ÃƒÂ©cart + ÃƒÂ©cart % (divide-by-zero guarded), at `projets/{id}/couts-engages-reels/`. No model. **ARC22** adds an additive nullable `SousTraitant.fournisseur` FK (migration gestion_projet/0042) bridging the project's local subcontractor notebook to the DC34 unified `stock.Fournisseur` master; `services.creer_sous_traitant_via_master` is the recommended creation path (calls `stock.services.create_sous_traitant` via a function-local import, never `apps.stock.models`) + `management/backfill_sous_traitant_fournisseur` for existing rows. **WIR87** closes the ARC22 gap for good: `SousTraitantViewSet.perform_create`/`perform_update` are now the ONLY write path, always setting `fournisseur` on create and freezing the duplicated columns (nom/specialite/contact/telephone/email/actif) on both sides of every update Ã¢â‚¬â€ they can no longer diverge; the canonical screen (`RisquesPage.jsx` Ã¢â€ â€™ Sous-traitance tab) no longer even calls this local endpoint, reading/writing the `stock.Fournisseur` master directly via `installations/sous-traitants/`.
- **PeriodeVerrouilleeTemps** + **PortailProjetToken** + **RecurrenceTache** (previously undocumented here) Ã¢â‚¬â€ timesheet month-locks, the project client-portal share-link tokens, and recurring-task templates; endpoints `periodes-verrouillees-temps/`, `portail-tokens/`, `recurrences-tache/`. **PACT78** gives all three their first screen, `/projets/parametres` (Ã‚Â« ParamÃƒÂ¨tres avancÃƒÂ©s Ã‚Â»).

### qhse Ã¢â‚¬â€ quality / health / safety / environment (QHSE1, new app)  *( `/api/django/qhse/` )*
- NCR/CAPA (non-conformities + corrective/preventive actions), `company`-scoped. **QHSE2** adds the ITP (inspection & test plan) templates: **PlanInspectionModele** (code/nom/actif) + **PointControleModele** (FK plan; `phase`, `type_releve` mesure/visuel/document/essai, `hold_point` bool, `ordre`). ViewSets `plans-chantier/` (+ `instancier`), `releves/`. `IsResponsableOrAdmin`-gated. **QHSE19** adds **RetourClientQualite** (client quality satisfaction): `note_satisfaction` 1Ã¢â‚¬â€œ5 + `commentaire`, string-id cross-app links `chantier_id`/`client_id` (no model import), `traite` bool, `selectors.satisfaction_moyenne` + ViewSet `retours-client/` (+ `moyenne/` action, `?chantier_id`/`?traite` filters). **QHSE20** adds `selectors.iso9001_readiness` Ã¢â‚¬â€ a read-only Ã‚Â« ISO 9001 readiness Ã‚Â» dashboard: weighted global score + 6 criteria mapped to ISO 9001:2015 clauses (NCR closed 10.2, CAPA on-time 10.2, audits 9.2, procedures published 7.5, ITP coverage 8.5/8.6, client satisfaction 9.1.2), divide-by-zero guarded, at `iso9001-readiness/` (responsable/admin). No model. **QHSE21** adds **EvaluationRisque** (document unique d'ÃƒÂ©valuation des risques: `reference` `DUER-` race-safe, `statut` brouillon/validee/archivee, string-ref `chantier_id`) + **LigneEvaluationRisque** (poste/activitÃƒÂ©/danger, `gravite`Ãƒâ€”`probabilite` (1Ã¢â‚¬â€œ5) = stored `criticite`, mesures, risque rÃƒÂ©siduel). ViewSets `evaluations-risque/` (+ `criticite/` summary), `lignes-evaluation-risque/`, role-gated. Migration qhse/0014. **QHSE22** adds the document-unique gate: `selectors.document_unique_valide(company, chantier_id)` (True iff Ã¢â€°Â¥1 validated EvaluationRisque with lines) + `services.exiger_document_unique` (raises `ValidationError` Ã¢â‚¬â€ consumed by `installations` to gate the pose transition) + endpoint `evaluations-risque/document-unique-statut/?chantier_id=`. chantier_id is a string-ref (no installations import). No model. **QHSE23** adds **PermisTravail** (work permit: hauteur/consignation_elec/point_chaud/espace_confine, server-set race-safe `PT-` reference, string-ref `chantier_id`, validity dates, `valider`/`cloturer` actions). ViewSet `permis-travail/`. Migration qhse/0015. **QHSE24** adds **ConsignationLoto** Ã¢â‚¬â€ a lockout-tagout electrical-isolation record on a QHSE23 `PermisTravail` (point de consignation, cadenas/ÃƒÂ©tiquette, vÃƒÂ©rif absence tension, server-set race-safe ref, statut consignÃƒÂ©e/dÃƒÂ©consignÃƒÂ©e) with a `deconsigner` action. ViewSet `consignations-loto/`. Migration qhse/0016.
- **ARC10** makes NCR closure the pilot domain consumer of the generic `core` BPM engine: `services.demarrer_workflow_cloture_ncr` installs the FG369 `cloture_ncr` template idempotently per company and attaches a `WorkflowInstance` to the NCR via contenttypes (qhse drives the engine; `core` never imports qhse) Ã¢â‚¬â€ a two-step agentÃ¢â€ â€™responsable approval cycle; the rule going forward is any NEW multi-step approval uses this engine, never an ad hoc one. **ARC38** repatriates `incident_declared` onto the `core.events` bus (see notifications paragraph). **ARC28** adds `apps/qhse/platform.py`.

### contrats Ã¢â‚¬â€ contracts (CONTRAT1, new app)  *( `/api/django/contrats/` )*
- **Contrat** (`company`-scoped). **CONTRAT3** adds **PartieContrat** (`company` + `contrat` FK `related_name='parties'`; `type_partie` client/prestataire/temoin/garant/autre, `nom`, `fonction`, `email`, `telephone`, `ordre`) Ã¢â‚¬â€ the parties/signatories of a contract. ViewSet `parties/` (CRUD, `?contrat=` filter; a party is validated same-company as its contract Ã¢â€ â€™ 400). The "Ã¢â€°Â¥2 signatories" rule lives in `Contrat.valider_parties()` for finalization (not enforced at create). **CONTRAT4** adds **ContratLien** (string-FK devis/lead/installation/maintenance, like ProjetLien) with `selectors.liens_enrichis` enriching via function-local `ventes`/`crm`/`installations` selectors (sav degrades to stored label). **CONTRAT5** adds `Contrat.sav_contrat_maintenance_id` (PositiveInteger, string-id to `sav.ContratMaintenance` Ã¢â‚¬â€ additive, no sav import, validation deferred until a sav selector exists). **CONTRAT6** adds `Contrat.confidentialite` (public/interne/confidentiel, default interne) Ã¢â‚¬â€ CONFIDENTIEL contracts are visible only to Administrators, gated in `get_queryset` on the authoritative `user.menu_tier` (not the unreliable `role_legacy`/Role-FK divergence). `IsResponsableOrAdmin`-gated. **CONTRAT13** adds **RegleApprobation** (approval rule by `type_contrat` and/or `montant_min`/`montant_max` bounds + `niveau_approbation`/`nombre_approbateurs`/`priorite`/`actif`) with `selectors.resoudre_regle_approbation` (most-specific wins: exact type > narrowest bounded interval > prioritÃƒÂ© > id). ViewSet `regles-approbation/` + `GET /resoudre/?montant=&type_contrat=`, company forced server-side (never body-set). **CONTRAT14** adds **EtapeApprobation** (internal approval workflow): `services.lancer_workflow_approbation` instantiates one step per the matching RegleApprobation's `nombre_approbateurs` (via the `resoudre_regle_approbation` selector), and `approuver_etape`/`rejeter_etape` advance it sequentially (out-of-order Ã¢â€ â€™ 400, relaunch refused). Statuses are local (en_attente/approuve/rejete) and the workflow **never mutates `Contrat.statut`**. Actions `contrats/{id}/lancer-approbation|etapes-approbation|approuver-etape|rejeter-etape`. **CONTRAT15** adds **ContratActivity** (chatter/journal): auto-logs statut/confidentialitÃƒÂ© transitions + the approval-workflow steps (LOG, with `field`/`old_value`/`new_value` snapshots in TextField) plus manual notes; actions `contrats/{id}/historique` (most-recent-first timeline) + `noter`. Acting user + company always server-side. **CONTRAT16** adds **SignatureContrat** (in-app e-sign, loi 53-05 typed name): `signataire_nom` + server-side evidence (`ip_adresse`/`user_agent`/acting user), `role_signataire`, `methode`; `services.signer_contrat` records it, logs via the chatter, and flips `Contrat.statut`Ã¢â€ â€™signÃƒÂ© through the existing state machine **only when all required parties (client+prestataire) have signed**. Unique `(contrat, role_signataire)`. Actions `contrats/{id}/signer|signatures`. No external e-sign provider. Migration contrats/0013. **CONTRAT17** chains an auto signÃƒÂ©Ã¢â€ â€™actif: once all required parties have signed, `signer_contrat` advances the contract to `actif` via the existing state machine **iff `date_debut` is null or Ã¢â€°Â¤ today** (future start stays `signe`), logged in the CONTRAT15 chatter (`today` injectable). No model. **CONTRAT18** adds **VersionContrat** (immutable contract-render versioning): server-incremented `version` (`select_for_update` max+1, never count()+1), frozen `contenu` + optional MinIO `fichier_key`; `services.creer_version` snapshots on demand + auto-snapshots on the signÃƒÂ© transition (best-effort, CONTRAT16/17 preserved); read-only retrieval viewset `versions/` (paginated). Migration contrats/0014.
- **Revenus rÃƒÂ©currents** (NTSUB1-4, previously undocumented here) Ã¢â‚¬â€ a sellable-offer catalogue: **PlanAbonnement** (Zuora/Chargebee-style Product Catalog, distinct from the pre-existing `PlanRecurrent` billing-cadence-only model) + **AddOnAbonnement**/**AbonnementAddOnLigne** + **PalierUsage**, nullably referenced by `Contrat.plan_abonnement` (`services.appliquer_plan_abonnement` snapshots price/cadence onto the contract at attach time, never a live link). **SequenceDunning**/**EtapeDunning** (NTSUB8) Ã¢â‚¬â€ configurable multi-step unpaid-invoice dunning sequences, also nullably attached. Endpoints `plans-abonnement/`, `addons-abonnement/`, `addon-lignes/`, `paliers-usage/`, `sequences-dunning/`, `etapes-dunning/`. **PACT138** gives the subscription catalogue its first screen (`/contrats/abonnements`); **PACT139** gives dunning sequences theirs (`/contrats/relances-impayes`).
- **SCA35** adopts the `core.documents` kit's TRANSITIONS read-contract on `Contrat` WITHOUT inheriting `DocumentMetier` itself: `Contrat` keeps its own `company` FK (historical `related_name='contrats'`) and `date_creation` rather than switching to `TenantModel`'s generic accessor (no functional gain, would break the historical accessor); a read-only `TRANSITIONS`/`transitions_permises` property exposes the EXISTING `machine_etats._transitions()` state graph (CONTRAT12) in the kit's expected shape Ã¢â‚¬â€ `services.changer_statut` (the `machine_etats.changer_statut` alias) stays the one write path, still enforcing the "Ã¢â€°Â¥2 parties" guard the generic kit doesn't know about. **ARC14** adds `Contrat.custom_data` (JSON), the first `customfields` pilot alongside `flotte.Vehicule`. **ARC28/33/40** add `apps/contrats/platform.py` (`agent_actions_module` Ã¢â‚¬â€ read-only list + `contrat_voir`; `kpi_providers`).

### kb Ã¢â‚¬â€ knowledge base (KB1, new app)  *( `/api/django/kb/` )*
- **KbArticle** (`company`-scoped; `statut` brouillon/publie/obsolete). **KB2** adds **KbArticleVersion** (`company` + `article` FK `related_name='versions'`; server-incremented `version` via `select_for_update` Ã¢â‚¬â€ never count()+1; `titre`/`contenu`/`auteur` snapshot). ### litiges Ã¢â‚¬â€ disputes / claims (LITIGE1, new app)  *( `/api/django/litiges/` )*
- **Reclamation** (`company`-scoped; `statut` ouverte/en_traitement/resolue/rejetee). **LITIGE2** adds a server-enforced state machine (actions `prendre-en-charge`/`resoudre`/`rejeter`, illegal transitions Ã¢â€ â€™ 400; statut read-only outside actions) plus a chatter **ReclamationActivity** (`company` + `reclamation` FK; `type` log/note, `old_value`/`new_value`/`message`/`auteur`) Ã¢â‚¬â€ auto-logs each transition and manual notes via `noter/`; timeline via `historique/`. ### adsengine Ã¢â‚¬â€ Meta Ads autonomous engine (Groupe ENG/ADSENG, new app)  *( `/api/django/adsengine/` )*
An in-ERP ads engine that runs Meta (Facebook/Instagram) campaigns on the company's own behalf Ã¢â‚¬â€ guardrail-gated proposeÃ¢â€ â€™approveÃ¢â€ â€™apply, never a black box. **Company-scoped and OFF by default**: with no `MetaConnection` row the whole app is inert (no sync, no spend, no writes). **Every campaign the engine creates is born `PAUSED`** (`meta_client.py`, rule #3) Ã¢â‚¬â€ nothing goes live without a human flipping it.
- **Models** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `MetaConnection` (per-company Meta creds/ad-account, encrypted at rest), `GuardrailConfig` (spend/CPA/frequency ceilings gating every write), `EngineAction` (the proposeÃ¢â€ â€™approveÃ¢â€ â€™apply spine Ã¢â‚¬â€ every mutating step is a row here first), `EngineAlert` (guardian-detected anomalies), `CreativeAsset` + `CreativePolicy` (ad creative library + compliance rules), `AdCampaignMirror`/`AdSetMirror`/`AdMirror` (read-mirror of live Meta objects), `InsightSnapshot` (daily performance pull), `WeeklyBrief` (auto-generated FR ops summary), `Experiment`/`ExperimentArm`/`ArmDailyStat`/`DecisionLog` (A/B test + bandit decision trail), `RulePolicy`/`AnomalyEvent`/`PacingState` (rules-engine + budget pacing), `CreativeGenerationBatch`/`CreativeBacklogItem` (creative production queue), `FlightPlan`/`FlightPhase` (launch sequencing + preflight), `ReconciliationSnapshot` (Meta-vs-ERP spend/lead reconciliation); **ADSDEEP P0-P3**: `InsightBreakdown` (age_gender/platform/region/hourly demographics), `AdCreativeMirror` (per-ad creative body/video/image/preview mirror), `MetaLeadMirror` (leads-per-ad, fed by the `meta_lead_captured` domain event + pull-sync, phone-key attribution to Odoo signatures per ad). `InsightSnapshot` gained typed columns (impressions/reach/clicks/link_clicks/conversations/leads_count/video_metrics). **ASG/AGEN/SIG (Assumption Engine)**: `AssumptionNode` (the living belief tree Ã¢â‚¬â€ per-class weekly-forgetting Beta posteriors ÃŽÂ±/ÃŽÂ² + prior ÃŽÂ±Ã¢â€šâ‚¬/ÃŽÂ²Ã¢â€šâ‚¬, half-lives 8/13/26 wk, parent + `invalidation_links` DAG, season tags, statut assumed/testing/validated/stale/retired), `FactTable`/`FactEntry` (versioned brouillon/publiÃƒÂ©e checked-facts table Ã¢â‚¬â€ the ONLY source of publishable numbers for grounded generation, one published table/company), plus audit columns on `CreativeGenerationBatch` (fact_table_version/claim_verdicts/template_quarantined) and fixed health-score weights on `GuardrailConfig`.
- **Key modules** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `meta_client.py` (the PAUSED-only Meta Marketing API client); `services.py` (the proposeÃ¢â€ â€™approveÃ¢â€ â€™apply `EngineAction` spine, guardrail-gated dispatch); `guardrails.py`; decision engine (`bandit.py` Thompson beta-binomial, `allocation.py`, `rewards.py`, `authority.py`, `mde.py`); guardian (`rules_engine.py`, `anomaly.py`, `rule_templates.py`, `watchdog.py`, `alerts.py`); treasury (`pacing.py`, `budget_applier.py`, `identity.py`, `launch_templates.py`); creative (`rotation.py`, `recombine.py`, `backlog.py`, `flightplan.py`, `dco.py`, `creative_factory.py`); attribution (`reconciliation.py`, `capi_crm.py`, `reporting.py`, `attribution.py`, `metrics.py`); Odoo cost-per-signature connector (`odoo_client.py` READ-ONLY JSON-RPC Ã¢â‚¬â€ rule #1, key-gated `ODOO_*`; `odoo_selectors.py` won-lead/confirmed-sale-order signed deals; `odoo_metrics.py` spendÃƒÂ·Odoo-signatures with phone-match per-campaign attribution; `odoo_views.py`; `manage.py odoo_pull` diagnostic); runner (`flightrunner.py` state machine, `simulator.py`, `preflight.py`); `platforms/` (the `AdsPlatform` interface Ã¢â‚¬â€ ADSENG48/49, Google/Snapchat/TikTok adapters GATED behind founder budget/product decisions); **Assumption Engine (ASG/AGEN/SIG)** Ã¢â‚¬â€ living tree (`assumption_decay.py` weekly ÃÂ=0.5^(1/H) forgetting, `voi.py` VoI scheduler `SÃ‚Â·UÃ‚Â·RÃ‚Â·T/C` behind cache flag `voi_scheduler_active` OFF-by-default replacing the fixed FlightRunner transition, `assumption_graph.py` invalidation cascade, `seeding.py` idempotent YAML seed+preflight, `priors.py` intra-tenant hierarchical priors Ã¢â‚¬â€ never cross-company); autonomous creative generation (`generation.py` FactTable-grounded, `claim_check.py` FR-numeric whitelist, `groundedness.py`, `policy_lint.py`+`policy_lint_config.py`, `tier_router.py` A/B/C tiers Ã¢â‚¬â€ C never generated, `video_queue.py`, `blast_radius.py` born-PAUSED + home-grown auto-pause, `generation_audit.py` rollback+quarantine Ã¢â‚¬â€ all key-gated NO-OP, AI produces ASSETS never decisions); management command `seed_fact_table` (idempotent Taqinor checked-facts seed); two Celery beats `adsengine.decay_assumptions_weekly` + `adsengine.autopause_blast_radius`. **ADSDEEP P4-P11**: `whatsapp/webhook/` (CTWA env-gated, HMAC-verified Ã¢â€ â€™ `CtwaReferral`), CAPI signature loop (`CapiOdooEvent`, beat `emit_capi_signatures`), Meta write surface (swap-creative/rename/spend-cap + EngineAction kinds edit_copy/set_spend_cap/rename/set_schedule/duplicate/create_ad_study/edit_post/create_post/boost_post/comment+IG kinds Ã¢â‚¬â€ all proposeÃ¢â€ â€™approveÃ¢â€ â€™apply, PAUSED-only invariant intact), rules v2 (compared-window templates, name-pattern targeting, `cadence_minutes` 15-min beat), creative analytics (hook/hold/fatigue, naming tags), organic (`PagePostMirror`, `CommentMirror`/`CommentKeywordRule` + `commentaires/*` endpoints, Instagram `InstagramMediaMirror`/`InstagramCommentMirror`/`InstagramPublishJob` + `instagram/*` endpoints), consent-gated Custom/Lookalike + engagement `audiences/*`, console endpoints `metrics/ads-cockpit/`, `metrics/dashboard-v2/`, `campaigns/<id>/hierarchie/`, `reporting/audit/`, `reporting/creatifs/{classement,nuage}/`, `regles/journal/`, digest beat `daily_ads_digest`. **PUB batch 1 (2026-07-19)**: routes `signaux/`+`signaux/cohorte/` (health/signal_guards/cohorts enfin cÃƒÂ¢blÃƒÂ©s), actions `file-voi`/`tests`/`tests/<id>/leads` sur `noeuds-hypothese/`, `annotations/` (Annotation, mig 0038), `sync-status/`, `today-queue/` (file Ã‚Â« Aujourd'hui Ã‚Â»), `ads/<meta_id>/histoire/` (fiche ad agrÃƒÂ©gÃƒÂ©e), `reporting/entonnoir-variantes/`, `reporting/incrementalite/` (geo-holdout, `incrementality.py`), `generation/variantes-ancrees/` (pipeline gÃƒÂ©nÃƒÂ©ration IA enfin exposÃƒÂ©, key-gated), `alertes/<id>/snooze/`, `actions/proposer/<kind>/` + `annuler` (proposition inverse), `experiences/<id>/conclure/` (verdict humain Ã¢â€ â€™ `evidence.py` ÃŽÂ±/ÃŽÂ²) ; quality rankings (mig 0036) + attribution incrÃƒÂ©mentale (mig 0037) synchronisÃƒÂ©s ; HMAC sur le webhook Lead Ads ; auto-crÃƒÂ©ation Lead CTWA ; `MotifPerte.est_junk` + junk-rate/no-show/entonnoir par variante (crm 0065-0066) ; ### cpq Ã¢â‚¬â€ Configure-Price-Quote enterprise (Groupe NTCPQ, new app)  *( `/api/django/cpq/` )*
Satellite app UPSTREAM of the quote PDF Ã¢â‚¬â€ models/rules/config only, NEVER renders a client PDF (rule #4: the premium engine at `apps/ventes/quote_engine` + `/proposal` stays the only client-quote path). String-FK to ventes/stock/crm; company-scoped.
- **Models** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `OptionProduit`/`ContrainteCompatibilite` (product option groups + INCOMPATIBLE/REQUIERT/RECOMMANDE constraints, NTCPQ1), `RegleProduitCPQ` (product rules evaluated through `core.rules.evaluate_condition_group`, NTCPQ2; **NTCPQ21** adds `bloquante` Ã¢â‚¬â€ a triggered rule is a warning badge by default, only a `bloquante=True` rule invalidates the configuration), `OffreGroupee`/`LigneOffreGroupee` (cascaded-price bundles, NTCPQ3), `PrixContractuel` (per-client/product negotiated price, priority-1 in `prix-applicable`, NTCPQ5), `SeuilMargeFamille` (min-margin guard per category Ã¢â‚¬â€ INTERNAL flag `marge_sous_seuil`, never client-facing, NTCPQ6), `RegleApprobationRemise`/`EtapeApprobationDevis` (discount-depth approval matrix modelled on `contrats.RegleApprobation`, NTCPQ7), `QuestionConfigurateur`/`SessionConfigurateur`/`ReponseConfigurateur` (guided configurator, NTCPQ9), `ClauseCGV` (dynamic deal-type clauses snapshotted onto `Devis.clauses_appliquees` at send, NTCPQ11), `ProduitEquivalent` (per-tier ÃƒÂ©conomique/standard/premium product-substitution rule powering the variant generator, NTCPQ16), `ParametresCPQ` (NTCPQ30, per-company CPQ settings singleton, OneToOne `company`, extends `core.models.TenantModel`).
- **reports.py** (NTCPQ19/22/23/24) Ã¢â‚¬â€ a module SEPARATE from `selectors.py` on purpose (avoids reopening the `ventes.models Ã¢â€ â€™ cpq.selectors Ã¢â€ â€™ ventes.selectors` import cycle); reads `ventes` only via `apps.ventes.selectors`. `suggestions_produit` (cross-sell/upsell, max 3, `RECOMMANDE` constraints then co-purchase frequency, purely suggestive), `feuille_configuration` (internal technical config sheet, never client-facing), `marge_sous_seuil` dashboard (staff), `rapport_conformite` (config compliance-rate report).
- Endpoints: `valider-compatibilite/`, `regles/` (+`evaluer/`), `offres-groupees/{id}/appliquer/`, `prix-contractuels/`, `clauses-cgv/` (NTCPQ12), `produits-equivalents/` (NTCPQ16), `devis/{id}/variantes/` (NTCPQ16, generates the tier variant devis), `devis/{id}/feuille-configuration/` (NTCPQ22), `suggestions/` (NTCPQ19), `marge-sous-seuil/` (NTCPQ23), `rapports/conformite/` (NTCPQ24), `parametres-cpq/` (NTCPQ30, singleton ViewSet), `prix-contractuels/import-csv/` (NTCPQ41), `rapports/approbations/` (NTCPQ25, discount-approval history), `rapports/catalogue-regles/` (NTCPQ42, read-only compatibility-rule catalogue export), `devis/{id}/comparaison-variantes/` (NTCPQ26, INTERNAL variant comparison sheet, never client-facing), `devis/{id}/relancer-approbation/` (NTCPQ28), `configurateur/demarrer|{s}/repondre|{s}/resultat|{s}/generer-devis/`. Devis-side actions `approuver-etape/`/`rejeter-etape/` live in `apps/ventes/views.py` (call `cpq.services`).
- **PACT118** adds `apps/cpq/bulk_targets.py`, registered from `CpqConfig.ready()`: the generic `core.bulk_edit` registry (`GET core/bulk-edit/targets/`, `POST core/bulk-edit/appliquer/`) previously had **zero registered targets in production** (only the socle's own tests called `register_bulk_target`). CPQ is the first real caller, declaring 3 company-scoped targets with narrow field whitelists (never a price field) Ã¢â‚¬â€ `cpq.offre-groupee` (`actif`), `cpq.question-configurateur` (`actif`/`ordre`), `cpq.contrainte-compatibilite` (`type`/`message_utilisateur`).

### territoires Ã¢â‚¬â€ territory assignment engine (Groupe NTCRM, new app)  *( `/api/django/territoires/` )*
Sits ABOVE the existing round-robin (XSAL11): a lead is routed by territory rules first, else falls back byte-identically. No crm-model imports (function-local reads + string-FK).
- **Models** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `Territoire` (GEO/SEGMENT/SECTEUR + criteria JSON), `TerritoireRegle` (priority-ordered condition via `core.rules`, action = assign to team/user), `TerritoireMembre` (member + race-safe rotation counter, quota). `services.assigner_lead_territoire` (select_for_update, never a modulo on count()).
- Endpoints: `territoires/` CRUD, `territoires/{id}/resoudre/?lead_id=` (non-mutating preview), `couverture/?jours=30` (**NTCRM25**, `selectors.rapport_couverture` Ã¢â‚¬â€ recent leads via `crm.selectors.leads_recents_pour_couverture` matching NO active territory rule, counted par rÃƒÂ©gion/segment; read-only, Responsable/Admin, no rotation side effect). Frontend `TerritoryCoverageWidget.jsx`.

### contacts Ã¢â‚¬â€ multi-role client contacts (Groupe NTCRM, new app)  *( `/api/django/contacts/` )*
Additive org-chart of buying contacts per client; string-FK to `crm.Client`, keeps Client's existing single-contact fields (implicit principal when none exists).
- **Models** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `ContactClient` (nom/poste/email/tel/whatsapp, `role_achat` DECIDEUR/INFLUENCEUR/UTILISATEUR/GATEKEEPER/SPONSOR, single-principal guard via `clean()`).
- Endpoints: `contacts-client/` CRUD (filter `?client=`).

### onboarding Ã¢â‚¬â€ product onboarding Ã‚Â« Premiers pas Ã‚Â» (Groupe NTDMO, new app)  *( `/api/django/onboarding/` )*
Light app: global checklist catalogue + company-scoped progress, auto-completed via the `core.events` bus (never a cross-app model import).
- **Models** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `OnboardingChecklistItem` (stable key, target role, order, target screen), `OnboardingProgress` (company+user+item, `complete_le`/`ignore_le`, unique together). **NTDMO14** adds `ProductTourStep` (catalogue of Ã‚Â« product tour Ã‚Â» spotlight steps Ã¢â‚¬â€ `tour_key`+`ordre`, CSS `selecteur`, `ecran_cible`; `company` NULL = global catalogue entry, same socle divergence as `OnboardingChecklistItem`) + `TourProgress` (per-user completion, `vu_le` NULL = never shown/finished; only an explicit Ã‚Â« Revoir Ã‚Â», NTDMO16, resets it). **NTDMO28** adds `OnboardingChecklistItem.masque_pour` (M2MÃ¢â€ â€™`authentication.Company`) Ã¢â‚¬â€ additive per-company hiding of a global catalogue item, never deletes it for other companies.
- **Wiring** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `receivers.py` subscribes in `apps.py::ready()` to existing domain signals (devis_sent/devis_accepted/facture_payee, **WIR59** adds `intervention_completed` Ã¢â€ â€™ the Ã‚Â« Suivre votre 1er chantier Ã‚Â» item, which previously had an empty `event_key` and could never auto-complete) to auto-tick items.
- Endpoints: `onboarding/progress/` (list, `{id}/ignorer/`, `ignorer-tout/`); `onboarding/tours/` (NTDMO14, catalogue + per-user vu/non-vu in one call) + `{tour_key}/vu/` (idempotent) + `{tour_key}/revoir/` (NTDMO16 reset); `onboarding/items-masques/` (NTDMO28, + `masquer/`/`demasquer/`). Frontend `components/ProductTour.jsx` (spotlight player) + `pages/parametres` Ã‚Â« Visites guidÃƒÂ©es Ã‚Â» block (revoir button per tour).

### agriculture Ã¢â‚¬â€ farms, plots, campaigns, agronomic inputs (NTAGR*)  *( `/api/django/agriculture/` )*
Vertical agribusiness. **NTAGR1** adds **Exploitation** (farm master) + **Parcelle** (cultivable plot: surface/GPS geometry/crop/soil/statut). **NTAGR2** adds **CampagneCulturale** (sowingÃ¢â€ â€™harvest cycle per plot: culture/variety/dates/yield/statut) with `clean()` guard: one plot Ã¢â€°Â¤1 EN_COURS campaign at a time. **NTAGR3/NTAGR6** adds **EtapeCampagne** (dated campaign steps: semis/traitement/irrigation/dÃƒÂ©sherbage/fertilisation/rÃƒÂ©colte; cost + optional intrant link) + `check_dar_guard()` Ã¢â‚¬â€ blocking pre-harvest-delay (DAR) compliance on phyto treatments (ONSSA conformity, lÃƒÂ¨ve `ValidationError` if traitement applies within DAR days of harvest date, never silent). **NTAGR5** adds **IntrantAgricole** (agronomic attributes on stock.Produit: catÃƒÂ©gorie semence/engrais/phyto, dose/ha, DAR jours, matiÃƒÂ¨re active/AMM Ã¢â‚¬â€ produit_id string-ref to stock, unique, never import stock.models). **NTAGR9** adds seasonal labour: **EquipeSaisonniere** (team) + **PointageAgricole** (daily timesheet: equipe/travailleur_nom/campagne/parcelle/date/tÃƒÂ¢che/journÃƒÂ©es/taux Ã¢â‚¬â€ optional employe_id string-ref to rh.DossierEmploye; informative only, no payroll effect). All `company`-scoped. ViewSets `exploitations/`, `parcelles/`, `campagnes/`, `etapes-campagne/`, `intrants-agricoles/`, `equipes-saisonnieres/`, `pointages/`, `lots-recolte/`. **WIR141** exposes the pre-existing NTAGR14 irrigation-cost selectors (`cout_irrigation_campagne`, `volume_irrigation_solaire_campagne` Ã¢â‚¬â€ until now with no REST caller) at `campagnes/{id}/cout-irrigation/` (paid-irrigation cost + solar-pumped volume over the campaign window). **LotRecolte** (NTAGR15/16, previously undocumented here) Ã¢â‚¬â€ a harvest batch tied to `stock` (never a second lot-tracking system), traced via `stock.selectors.trace_serie(company, numero_lot=...)`. **PACT79** gives it its first screen (`/agriculture/lots-recolte`) Ã¢â‚¬â€ the 12th agricultural resource, until then the only one with neither route nor menu entry.

### btp_chantier Ã¢â‚¬â€ BTP/EPC vertical: site issues, requests, document approvals, journals  *( `/api/django/btp-chantier/` )*
Project-execution traceback for EPC/BTP contractors. **NTCON1** adds **ReserveChantier** (site issue/finding: gÃƒÂ©o-located on plan, statut, severity, photo/document refs). **NTCON2** adds **RFI** (request-for-info: dated, destinataire, topic, response statut). **NTCON3** adds **VisaDocument** Ã¢â‚¬â€ technical document approval workflow tied to ged.DocumentVersion: statut en_attente/visÃƒÂ©/rouvert, signataire, `clean()` enforces visa prerequisite before document advance (ged.Statut brouillonÃ¢â€ â€™revue path). **NTCON5** auto-opens expired visas on ged new version (via a receiver wired in `apps.ready()`, function-local `apps.get_model('ged.DocumentVersion')`, never imports ged.models). **NTCON4** adds **JournalChantier** (site journal entries: date/auteur/ÃƒÂ©vÃƒÂ©nement/photo). All `company`-scoped, string-refs to installations.Chantier (`chantier_id`, never imports installations.models). ViewSets `reserves-chantier/`, `rfi/`, `visas/`, `journal-chantier/`.

### esg Ã¢â‚¬â€ ESG/sustainability reporting (cross-app, liÃƒÂ© qhse via selectors)  *( `/api/django/esg/` )*
ESG/durability consolidated reporting (GRI-lite framework, aggregation, target tracking). Multi-app reader (qhse, rh, stock, installations raw indicators via function-local selectors Ã¢â‚¬â€ never imports their models). **NTESG1** adds **PeriodeReportingESG** (frozen reporting period: annÃƒÂ©e, trimestre/semestre/annÃƒÂ©e, `date_debut/fin`, scope perimeter, statut brouillon/finalisee/archivee). **NTESG2** adds **CatalogueIndicateurESG** (GRI-lite catalogue: code, libelle, unite, scope S/E/G, formula description Ã¢â‚¬â€ company master copy per scope). **NTESG3** adds **ObjectifESGTrajectoire** (target + yearly milestones per indicator per period: objectif_2030, annee_baseline, trajectory points `{annee, valeur}`). Selectors aggregate `qhse` raw HSE data + rh attrition/training + installations energy-yield into normalized tiles. ViewSets `periodes-esg/`, `catalogue-esg/`, `objectifs-esg/` (read any-role on finalized periods). All `company`-scoped.

### hospitality Ã¢â‚¬â€ hotel/riad/restaurant vertical: rooms, reservations, guest folios, housekeeping  *( `/api/django/hospitality/` )*
Vertical for hospitality operators. **NTHOT1** adds room/unit master: **TypeChambre** (category: Standard/SuiteÃ¢â‚¬Â¦, capacity) + **Chambre** (physical unit: type/statut libre/occupÃƒÂ©e/sale/en_nettoyage, optional rate-link). **NTHOT2** adds rate calendars: **PlanTarifaire** (pricing grid: per room-type, per date-range, per occupancy-basis). **NTHOT3** adds guest lifecycle: **Reservation** (dates/guest/room/statut en_attente/confirmÃƒÂ©e/arrivÃƒÂ©e/annulÃƒÂ©e, optional client_ventes_id string-ref) + actions `arrivee/` `depart/` (state machine). **NTHOT4** adds unified **Folio** (guest bill: reservation, room-charges + additional services, montant_ttc, statut brouillon/emise/payÃƒÂ©e/en_retard). **NTHOT5** adds housekeeping: **TacheMenage** (cleaning task: chambre, date_prevue, priorite, statut, agent assignÃƒÂ©, durÃƒÂ©e, notes). All `company`-scoped. ViewSets `types-chambre/`, `chambres/`, `plans-tarifaires/`, `reservations/`, `folios/`, `taches-menage/` + a `tableau-bord/` dashboard (overview: taux_occupation, revenu_jour, taches_en_retard).

### immobilier Ã¢â‚¬â€ real-estate portfolio, leases, rent accounting, GMAO  *( `/api/django/immobilier/` )*
Vertical for property management (fonciÃƒÂ¨res, syndics, facility managers). Hierarchical patrimony **Site** Ã¢â€ â€™ **BÃƒÂ¢timent** Ã¢â€ â€™ **Niveau** Ã¢â€ â€™ **Local** (unit type/surface/tantiÃƒÂ¨mes/statut libre/louÃƒÂ©/en_travaux); all `company`-scoped with materialized FK chains. **NTPRO2** adds **Locataire** (tenant: type_locataire particulier/societe, coordinates, optional client_ventes_id string-ref to crm.Client via `selectors`, never imports crm.models). **NTPRO3** adds **Bail** (lease: type habitation/commercial, dates, loyer_ht/charges/depot_garantie, statut brouillon/actif/preavis/resilie/expire + snapshot nom bailleur/locataire at signature, NTPRO5 deposit lifecycle: recu/restitue with retenue justifiÃƒÂ©e). **NTPRO4** adds **RevisionLoyer** (immutable rent-revision history: date_effet/ancien/nouveau loyer, indice, taux_variation Ã¢â‚¬â€ audit trail). **NTPRO6** adds **EcheanceLoyer** (monthly rent due: bail/periode/montant_loyer_ht/charges/total, statut a_emettre/emise/payee/impayee/relancee, optional facture_ventes_id string-ref). **NTPRO8** adds **RelanceLoyer** (delinquency escalation distinct from ventes relances: niveau 1-3, canal whatsapp/email/courrier, template). ### extensions Ã¢â‚¬â€ extension marketplace catalogue (NTEXT13, new app)  *( `/api/django/extensions/` )*
A GLOBAL, read-only registry of installable no-code extension packages (templates describing the custom objects / automation rules / reports a tenant could install). Not tenant business data Ã¢â‚¬â€ company-less by design (reviewed global, in `tenant_exempt_models.txt`/`tenant_view_allowlist.txt`); per-tenant installation is a separate future brick (NTEXT14).
- **Models** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `ExtensionPackage` (code unique, nom, version, categorie, `manifest` JSON, no company FK).
- Endpoints Ã¢â‚¬â€ `GET extensions/catalogue/` (read-only list of global packages). **NTEXT25** adds `journal.py` + `GET extensions/journal/` Ã¢â‚¬â€ a company-scoped unified timeline of platform execution events (admin). **NTEXT40** adds `statistiques.py` + `GET extensions/statistiques/` Ã¢â‚¬â€ platform-usage stats (admin cockpit) for the requester's own company.

### sante Ã¢â‚¬â€ cabinet/clinic (agenda, admission, acts nomenclature, patient/third-party billing) (NTSAN1, new app)  *( `/api/django/sante/` )*
Administrative-only healthcare vertical (no clinical medical data): multi-praticien scheduling, admission, act catalogue, patient/tiers-payant invoicing. Every model `TenantModel`-scoped, cross-tenant isolation tested.
- **Models** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `Praticien` (**WIR92** adds an optional `employe_id` Ã¢â‚¬â€ string-ref to `rh.DossierEmploye`, resolved lazily via `apps.rh.selectors`, never an `rh.models` import Ã¢â‚¬â€ for a salaried practitioner's HR/payroll link; additive, no lien = unchanged behaviour), `Salle`, `Patient` (numero_dossier via `core.numbering`), `RendezVous` (overlap guard), `Admission`, `ActeMedical`, `Convention`, `GrilleTarifaire`, `ActeRealise` (tarif snapshot), `PriseEnCharge`, `FactureSante` (tiers-payant/patient split), `PaiementSante`.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/sante/`): CRUD viewsets per model + agenda calendar filters; cross-app `Client` resolution via `crm.selectors` string-FK. **CycleSterilisation** + **InstrumentSterilise** (NTSAN23/24, previously undocumented here) Ã¢â‚¬â€ autoclave cycle traceability + a lightweight instrumentÃ¢â€ â€patient M2M; a non-conforming cycle blocks use. Endpoints `cycles-sterilisation/`, `instruments-sterilises/`. **PACT114** gives it its first screen (`/sante/sterilisation`, open to all roles) Ã¢â‚¬â€ the app had no API client and no screen for this before.

App NEUVE et dÃƒÂ©libÃƒÂ©rÃƒÂ©ment SÃƒâ€°PARÃƒâ€°E d'`apps/ao` (dont la chaÃƒÂ®ne de migrations est rÃƒÂ©servÃƒÂ©e au groupe AOF ; cÃƒÂ´tÃƒÂ© ÃƒÂ©cran `features/ao/**` appartient ÃƒÂ  AOF, la veille a `features/veille_ao/`). **Ne promet JAMAIS l'exhaustivitÃƒÂ©** : l'AO fondateur (FRDISI) n'a jamais ÃƒÂ©tÃƒÂ© publiÃƒÂ© Ã¢â‚¬â€ c'ÃƒÂ©tait une consultation privÃƒÂ©e sur invitation Ã¢â‚¬â€ d'oÃƒÂ¹ DEUX portes, l'automatique (portail public) et la manuelle (tuyau partenaire), la seconde ÃƒÂ©tant la seule qui l'aurait captÃƒÂ©.

- **Le collecteur HTTP (VAO15-20) est CODE mais DESARME** (2026-08-26) : paquet `apps/veille_ao/portail/` (client PRADO `client.py`, parseur pur bs4 `parser.py`, enrichissement a la demande `detail.py`, garde-fous `garde_fous.py` : cadence 2 s + quota dur 10/jour + interrupteur `VEILLE_AO_COLLECTE_ACTIVE=0` par defaut, verdicts fail-loud `resultats.py`), fixtures HTML committees (reconstructions declarees, jamais capturees), contrat de purete AST + `GardeReseau` (zero socket en test). **Son enregistrement dans `lecteurs.py` est VOLONTAIREMENT ABSENT** : brancher le lecteur = l'acte d'armement VAO4, gate fondateur (regle #5, approbation du fichier de risque `tos_risk/marchespublics_gov_ma.md` encore vide). Dependance : `beautifulsoup4==4.14.2`. User-Agent HONNETE, arret definitif sur 403/401/406/429, jamais de maquillage.

### credit Ã¢â‚¬â€ client credit management (Groupe NTCRD, new app)  *( `/api/django/credit/` )*
Credit limits, credit-hold (avertissement/blocage), payment-behaviour scoring, dÃƒÂ©rogations, credit-insurance coverage and consolidated exposure. **Additive & non-blocking by default** Ã¢â‚¬â€ no `LimiteCredit` for a client means unchanged behaviour (no hold); references ventes/crm strictly by string-FK (`crm.Client`, `ventes.Devis`, `ventes.BonCommande`), reads encours via `ventes.selectors` only.
- **Models** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `LimiteCredit` (unique per company+client, `montant_limite` nullable = unlimited), `ReglageCredit` (per-company settings singleton), `ConditionPaiementSegment` + `SegmentClientCredit`, `EncoursCache`, `DerogationCredit` (request/approve/reject lifecycle lifting hold), `PoliceAssuranceCredit`, `EncoursGarantiClient` (per police+client insurer quota).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/credit/`): ViewSets `limites/`, `derogations/`, `conditions-segment/`, `segments-client/`, `polices-assurance/`, `encours-garantis/`; `reglage/`, `clients/<id>/fiche|score|position-pdf|limite-suggeree/`, `import-limites/`, `exposition/`, `rapport-derogations/`, `badges/`. Selectors: `encours_client`, `disponible_credit`, `fiche_credit`, `rapport_exposition`, `quota_assurance_utilise`. Service `verifier_hold_credit`. Beat tasks: `recalculer_encours_quotidien`, `expirer_derogations`, `alerter_exposition_globale`, `alerter_polices_expirantes`. **PACT47** gives `import-limites/` its first screen (`/credit/import-limites`); **PACT48** gives `polices-assurance/` theirs (`/credit/assurance`); **PACT50** gives `segments-client/` theirs (`/credit/segments-clients`).
- **WIR93 Ã¢â‚¬â€ coexistence, documented and locked.** Two credit-hold engines exist in the repo: THIS app (`LimiteCredit`/`verifier_hold_credit`, NTCRD6 Ã¢â‚¬â€ has **no production caller today**, its NTCRD7/8 hooks are `[BLOCKED]`) and the actually-wired `crm.Client.plafond_credit`/`CompanyProfile.credit_hold_actif` Ã¢â€ â€™ `ventes.services.verifier_credit_hold` (FG41/XFAC28, consumed at devis-accept + tranche generation). `services.ecart_encours_moteurs(client)` materializes the ONE authorized gap between them (engine B intentionally excludes `brouillon` factures, via the new `ventes.selectors.reste_du_factures_brouillon`) and is locked by `test_wir93_encours_non_divergence.py` Ã¢â‚¬â€ any future drift (new statut, one-sided assiette change) turns it red. No behaviour change; a documentation + regression-test decision, switching to a single source stays open to the founder.

### fpa Ã¢â‚¬â€ enterprise FP&A / budgeting (Groupe NTFPA, new app)  *( `/api/django/fpa/` )*
Financial Planning & Analysis: departmental budget cycles, submission/validation workflow, rolling forecasts, what-if scenarios + sensitivity, variance analysis (budget vs actual vs forecast). DISTINCT from per-chantier micro-budget (`gestion_projet.BudgetProjet`). Reads compta/ventes/crm/paie only via their `selectors.py`; emits `budget_cycle_clos` domain event on `core/events.py`.
- **Models** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `Departement` (hierarchy), `CycleBudgetaire` (brouillon/ouvert_saisie/en_validation/clos state machine, write-locked once clos), `LigneBudgetDepartement` (monthÃƒâ€”category), `SoumissionBudgetDepartement`, `MappingCategorieCompte` (FP&A category Ã¢â€ â€ CGNC prefix), `PrevisionGlissante` + `LignePrevisionGlissante`, `HypotheseRecrutement`, `ScenarioBudgetaire` + `LigneScenario` (deltas, one base per cycle), `CommentaireVariance`.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/fpa/`): ViewSets `departements/`, `cycles-budgetaires/` (+ `ouvrir-saisie`/`dupliquer`/`export`), `lignes-budget/`, `soumissions-budget/` (+ `historique`/`noter` chatter actions on `SoumissionBudgetDepartement`), `previsions/`, `scenarios/`, `variance/`, `commentaires-variance/`, `mappings-categorie/`, `drivers/` (revenu-pipeline/engage, masse-salariale), `consolidation/`. **PACT53** fixes `historique`/`noter` to serialize through `ChatterActivitySerializer` (was `ActivitySerializer`, which exposes neither `body` nor the author Ã¢â‚¬â€ notes rendered empty/anonymous) and gives the submission-validation workflow its first screen, `/fpa/soumissions`. Frontend routes `/fpa/*` (saisie, dashboard, previsions, scenarios, variance, soumissions) gated Directeur/FP&A.

### assurances Ã¢â‚¬â€ enterprise insurance & claims registry (Groupe NTASS, new app)  *( `/api/django/assurances/` )*
Company insurance policies (RC pro, dÃƒÂ©cennale, multirisque, cyber, homme-clÃƒÂ©) with guarantees, premium schedules, covered assets, claims (sinistres) + indemnities and attestations. ### einvoice Ã¢â‚¬â€ Moroccan DGI e-invoicing (Groupe NTMAR, new app)  *( `/api/django/einvoice/` )*
Flag-gated (`EINVOICE_ENABLED`, default OFF Ã¢â€ â€™ app entirely inert) generator of DGI-schema electronic invoices. RE-USES the existing `apps.ventes.dgi` UBL export + art.145 validator via the module API + `ventes.selectors.get_facture_scoped` (never a ventes model import); no new render engine.
- **Models** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `FactureElectronique` (facture-id string-ref + dÃƒÂ©normalized ref, format UBL/CII, mode dry_run/rÃƒÂ©el, statut brouillonÃ¢â€ â€™genereÃ¢â€ â€™signeÃ¢â€ â€™transmis/rejete, immutable `version`, `xml_key` MinIO, `hash_contenu`, signature scaffold fields), `TransmissionDGI` (inert Simpl queue, unique per e-facture).
- **Services** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `generer` (dry-run XML Ã¢â€ â€™ MinIO, new version), `regenerer` (NTMAR9 immutable re-gen), `preparer_signature` (NTMAR6 empreinte only, never signs Ã¢â‚¬â€ `EINVOICE_SIGNATURE_PROVIDER=noop`), `transmettre` (NTMAR7 no-op without `DGI_TRANSMISSION_ENABLED`+URL), `validators.controler_avant_transmission` (NTMAR8, reuses art.145 validator).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/einvoice/`): ViewSets `factures-electroniques/` (actions `generer`/`regenerer`/`telecharger`/`preparer-signature`/`transmettre`/`controler`), `transmissions/` (read-only). Live DGI transmission + certified signature stay `[BLOCKED-ESCALATE]` (gate G14, no DGI API/credential). **PACT54** gives `transmissions/` its first screen Ã¢â‚¬â€ `/fiscal/transmissions-dgi` (grouped under the `fiscal` frontend module for UX, still reads `einvoiceApi`).

### fiscal Ã¢â‚¬â€ Moroccan fiscal compliance calendar & attestations (Groupe NTMAR, new app)  *( `/api/django/fiscal/` )*
Full Moroccan tax calendar per obligation + reminders + compliance dashboard, tenant attestations with expiry, UBO registry, actionable regulatory watch. Distinct from `compta.ObligationFiscale` (XACC9 one-off calendar view) Ã¢â‚¬â€ here the recurring RULE + materialized dated deadlines, covering obligations compta doesn't (CNSS/taxe pro/enregistrement).
- **Models** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `ObligationFiscale` (recurring rule: type TVA/IS/IR/acompte_IS/timbre/RAS/CNSS/taxe_pro/enregistrement, pÃƒÂ©riodicitÃƒÂ©, rÃƒÂ¨gle d'ÃƒÂ©chÃƒÂ©ance), `EcheanceFiscale` (dated deadline, `rappel_envoye_le` idempotence, declaration string-ref), `AttestationTenant` (expiry), `BeneficiaireEffectif` (UBO), `VeilleReglementaire` (per-company or global, `parametre_cible` actionable link).
- **Services/selectors** Ã¢â‚¬â€ `seed_obligations_standard`, `calendrier(company, annee)` (idempotent), `envoyer_rappels_fiscaux` (+ `manage.py rappels_fiscaux`), `tableau_conformite` (feu tricolore), `attestations_expirantes`, `pieces_reutilisables_attestations` (NTMAR29 source for ao dossiers), `registre_ubo` (+ completeness alert), `export_declaration_ubo` (OMPIC-shaped).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/fiscal/`): ViewSets `obligations/` (`seed-standard`/`calendrier`/`tableau-conformite`/`rappels`), `echeances/`, `attestations-tenant/` (`expirantes`/`pieces-reutilisables`), `beneficiaires-effectifs/` (`registre`/`export-declaration`), `veille/` (`marquer-impact-traite`); function view `tableau-conformite/`. **PACT52** gives `beneficiaires-effectifs/` (UBO registry) its first screen, `/fiscal/beneficiaires-effectifs` (completeness alert + OMPIC export).

### education Ã¢â‚¬â€ ÃƒÂ©cole/ÃƒÂ©tablissement scolaire (NTEDU, new app)  *( `/api/django/education/` )*
Company-scoped school ops: annÃƒÂ©e scolaire / niveau / classe, dossier famille/ÃƒÂ©lÃƒÂ¨ve, inscriptions (rÃƒÂ©inscription masse + liste d'attente FIFO), scolaritÃƒÂ© (grille tarifaire, remises fratrie/bourse en `brouillon` jusqu'ÃƒÂ  approbation, ÃƒÂ©chÃƒÂ©ancier auto), prÃƒÂ©sences (bulk-saisie + notif absence famille), matiÃƒÂ¨res/coefficients, ÃƒÂ©valuations/notes, emploi du temps, cantine, discipline, and a parent self-service account Ã¢â‚¬â€ a wider surface than previously written up here.
